| 2.70.0 | **Bot frozen after a rest - dead Sentinel leg.** The 12:12 session shows resting working (drank Refreshing Spring Water at MP 6 and 18, stood up at 100), but right after the second rest a Sentinel "pull" leg issued to close on a wolf (12:15:12) never ended: Sentinel neither arrived nor failed, `R.sn_active` stayed true, the walker stays silent while Sentinel drives, and every later move - the walk to the corpse, then the vendor trip - was refused as "already moving" while the character stood still for over two minutes. `N.watch` now drops a leg once the character has not moved 1.5 yd in 4 s while not casting (logged as a `move:` trail line), and `combat_release` stops a "pull" leg when the fight ends. |
| 2.71.0 | **Questing idle on "step complete".** The 12:35 session: the "accept Eagan Peltskinner" goal's waypoint was converted with z 0 - `get_terrain_height` answers 0 when it has none, and the 200-yard sanity check let that through at a spot ~82 high - so Sentinel replied "Position not on navmesh" (HTTP 422), the goal was skipped as unreachable, the quest was never taken and RestedXP never moved on. (1) A terrain height of 0 is rejected unless the player stands near 0, and a height must be within 60 yd of the player's. (2) Accept / turn-in / talk goals are never skipped for reachability; they keep walking. (3) The player's target is only taken as the quest giver when it stands within 24 yd of the goal's waypoint: a vendor trip had left Godric Rothgar targeted and the accept was tried on him. |
| 2.72.0 | **Crash while pathing to a target - facing hands the SDK a unit.** Crash 12:45:04 (v2.71.0, questing, closing on a Kobold Vermin at 46 yd) is the fourth today, all while facing or casting at a mob; the last completed tick is `f:combat_engage > f:face > f:combat_scan > r:face > r:smart > sm:range Fireball`. `Rg.face` and the cast lock gave the SDK movement handler the target GAME OBJECT (`look_at_target`), which the handler keeps across frames and whose `on_render` reads it every frame for the whole session - a freed unit (corpse despawn, mob out of view) there is a native crash, the stale-handle class of 2.32.0. Facing and cast locks now pass a POSITION (`look_at_position`, re-read every FACE_GAP), and the handler's render pass only runs with movement debug on. |
| 2.73.0 | **Food / water never bought.** Every zone merchant in `grind/zones` is an armorer or weaponsmith (Godric Rothgar, Corina Steele, William MacGregor, Jaquilina Dramet), and none stocks food or water, so `supplies.lua` always ended on "No food stocked here". When the merchant lacks what is needed, the trip now goes on to the nearest friendly innkeeper (any "Innkeeper ...") or general-goods NPC (Brother Danil, Adlin Pridedrift) within 80 yd, opens its vendor window and buys there; each seller is tried once per trip. Buying is up to 5 per call (was 1 per 0.8 s), capped by gold; a buy that does not raise the bag count is retried at most 3 times; every buy and every "not stocked" is a `supplies:` trail line. |
| 2.74.0 | **Repair verified; buying on by default.** Repair sent `repair_all_items` once and marked the trip repaired whatever happened. The cost is now re-read on the next pass and the repair re-sent until it is 0 (up to 3 tries); "cannot repair", "not enough gold" and "refused" each end it with a `vendor:` trail line, as does each merchant window opening (item count, can repair). "Buy Food / Drink" (Vendor popup) now defaults to on - off, nothing was ever bought. |
| 2.75.0 | **Training.** (1) The bot never went to a trainer - it only trained when a gossip window was already open, so a grinding character never trained. After a level-up it now walks to a class trainer in sight (within 100 yd, by name - starting-zone and capital trainers of all nine classes), talks to it and buys every affordable class rank, once per level (gives up on that level after 6 unanswered tries). (2) A RestedXP trainer step counted as done 2 s after the frame opened and the quest engine closed the gossip mid-training; it now waits while `trainer.busy()`. (3) The trainer gossip option is found like the vendor's (izi icon lookup, then type "trainer" or "Train me"). "Train Spells" now defaults to on. |
| 2.76.0 | **Crash: Sentinel pull-in legs switched off.** The 13:17 session ran 14 minutes (the longest yet since 2.72.0 stopped handing the movement handler units) and crashed at 13:31:22 inside the Echo Ridge mine, 0.6 s after a Sentinel "pull" leg started toward a Kobold Laborer at 44-48 yd. The 11:49:55 and 12:45:04 crashes were the same: within about a second of a pull leg toward a fresh target 44-50 yd away. While a pull leg runs this plugin does almost nothing per frame - Sentinel drives - and pull legs had already been seen hanging (2.70.0). Combat approach is now the local walker's only (direct chase, steering, find_path-planned chase); Sentinel keeps long-distance travel. Also: a raid-marked quest mob that combat movement has marked unreachable is no longer re-engaged every 0.8 s. |
| 2.77.0 | **Player Detection removed.** The General tab's "Player Detect (pause pulls)" checkbox and "Player Detect Range" slider, their `player_detect` / `player_yards` aliases, and `targeting.lua`'s `player_nearby` check in `find_mobs` are gone; mob searches no longer stop when another player is near. |
| 2.78.0 | **Line of sight before attacking.** The rotation checked only range before a cast, so it kept casting into walls ("Target not in line of sight") while combat movement - which already checked LoS - was still repositioning. `movement.has_los(player, unit)` (the same los_to / izi.is_los / eye-height trace chain combat movement uses, cached 0.25 s per unit) now gates every enemy-targeted cast in `smart.lua` (interrupts on other pack members included) and the melee auto-attack start in `targeting.lua`. Self-cast and self-centred spells are not gated. Offline test added: no cast without LoS, cast once it returns. |
| 2.79.0 | **Quest objects on the ground.** Object goals (and the object half of collect goals) used `interact_with_object`, which the SDK documents as not covering world objects - `use_object` is their entry point; re-clicked every 1.5 s, interrupting the Collecting / Opening cast; never emptied the loot window the object opens; clicked only within 4 yd of the object's centre; and matched object names only as a substring of the objective text. Now: `use_object` (falling back to interact), the cast is waited out, the loot window is looted slot by slot and closed, the click happens within 5 yd or within 8 yd once the walk has stopped, object names match either way round or on a shared word of 4+ letters, and an object used 4 times with nothing to show is skipped for 60 s so the next one is tried. Each use and loot is a `act:` trail line. |
| 2.80.0 | **Vendor stuck - selling by item id.** The 14:25 log shows every sale failing: inventory_helper gave backpack items as "bag 0 slot 61..72" (global 95..106) - impossible for a 16-slot bag - so `use_container_item` never hit them, each item burned two retries, and with bags still full the trip came back every minute. The raw get_items_in_bag slot is documented as shifted by one, so correcting it by guesswork could sell the item beside the junk. Selling now uses `core.input.use_item(item_id)` (with the merchant open that sells a stack of exactly that item and can touch nothing else), verified by the bag count of that id going down (`bags.count`). Auto-equip also uses the item id first, the slot only as a fallback. |
| 2.81.0 | **Targeting skips mobs the player cannot see.** New targets were picked with no visibility test, so the bot chose mobs behind walls, down a mine under it or on a ledge overhead, walked at them and cast "not in line of sight". `targeting.can_see(player, unit)` rejects a mob more than 12 yd above/below when it is also close in plan (under 3x the height gap), and any mob without eye-height LoS (`movement.has_los`); cached 1 s per mob. Applied to grinding and path-mode target search (`targeting.find_mobs`), every quest mob finder (`guide` fightable: find_mob / find_source_mob / find_camp_mob / find_path_mob / raid-marked), and the pick of a new target from the fighting pack (visible first, nearest otherwise). The current target and mobs already attacking are not filtered. |
| 2.82.0 | **Obstacle avoidance engine.** Every "can I walk from a to b" question (combat chase, local walker legs, steering, reachability) was one `trace_line` at eye height with the Collision flags: knee-high rocks, fences and crates were invisible and gaps narrower than the body read as open. `U.corridor` (behind `U.walk_open`) now traces a body-width corridor, cheapest-to-fail first: chest height with Collision (walls, trees, cliffs, terrain), then knee height and both shoulders (0.5 yd off centre) with Doodad + Wmo + Entity collision only (`K.FLAG_OBSTACLE`, from `enums.collision_flags`) so slopes are not walls; cached 0.3 s on a half-yard grid; trace budget 10 -> 40 per pulse. Steering prefers a detour hop whose way onward toward the goal is also open (no dead-end pockets), falling back to the first open one. New look-ahead: every 0.25 s while a walker move runs, the corridor 4 yd ahead is re-checked and a detour hop is issued before the character reaches the obstacle. Offline-tested with simulated obstacles (knee rock, wall, 0.4 yd gap blocked; 1.6 yd gap and low terrain open; detour takes the open side). Sentinel's long-distance travel is unchanged (it paths on the navmesh). |
| 2.83.0 | **Mini status window.** When grinding, questing or Rotation Only starts, the main window hides and a 200x100 window shows the mode, the time since the start (HH:MM:SS) and the player's level, with a Stop button. Stop ends the run (grinding / questing and Rotation Only), closes the mini window and brings the main window back; the main window also returns by itself when the run ends any other way (Numpad 2, pause, the NPC watchdog). The mini window's close cross only brings the main window back and leaves the run going. |
| 2.84.0 | **Adaptive re-pathing** (`movement/repath.lua`). (1) Moving goals: a walker / Sentinel move used to run to where the goal WAS - no new move was accepted while moving. `RP.reaim` now lets `navigate` replace the move in flight when the goal has shifted more than max(2 yd, 15% of the distance): every 0.2 s within 30 yd, every 1.0 s beyond, never inside a 3 yd dead zone (no orbiting a target already reached). (2) Progress, not motion: the goal's distance (nav destination or combat target) must shrink by 1 yd; without that - walls, circling, orbiting - a ladder climbs: after 4 s re-plan (Sentinel benched 20 s so walker steering takes over, steering widened, other side first); after 3 s more jump and back off 2 yd at 45 degrees; after 4 s more give up - a combat target is marked unreachable and released, a destination is blacklisted (8 yd) so its caller moves on. The old standstill check hands over to the same ladder instead of cancelling and blacklisting at once. Quest mob finders now skip mobs marked unreachable. Every rung is a `move:` trail line. Offline-tested (11 checks). |
| 2.85.0 | **Moving without stopping.** (1) Hop chaining: steering walks in hops of a few yards and the character stood still at the end of each (move cleared after 0.3 s, re-issued after the 0.85 s move gap). While a hop is walked, the next hop toward the leg's real end (`R.goal_*`, set by `navigate`) is now planned from this hop's end point every 0.25 s, and issued 1.8 yd before arrival if the body corridor fits. (2) Re-aims beyond 30 yd no longer send Sentinel a fresh `move_to` (which stands the character still in "awaiting_path"): `N.prefetch` asks `find_path` in the background while the current leg runs and switches with `follow_path` once the points arrive. (3) The ladder's re-plan rung no longer halts the walker - it clears the destination and forces the next move through at once. (4) Tight corridors: steering tries a half-length straight hop first (taken at once only when the way on from it is open, otherwise kept as a fallback), and when nothing fits the body-width corridor it retries once with a 0.25 yd half width (`R.tight_corridor`) before backing off. Offline tests re-run (corridor, detours, re-path ladder). |
| 2.86.0 | **Vendor idle limit.** At a merchant - its window open, or standing next to it (zone merchant or food / water seller) trying to open it - with nothing sold, bought or repaired for 15 s, the vendor trip ends ("Idle at the vendor - moving on", `vendor:` trail line) and grinding / questing carries on. Every sale, purchase and repair restarts the clock; walking to a merchant does not count as idle. |
| 2.87.0 | **Automatic light crash capture.** The 16:34 session (v2.86.0) ended at 16:35:53, 0.9 s after a rest, closing on a Prowler at 37-41 yd. WoW's EditMode.log shows its UI state saved at 16:35:54.110 - and at 13:31:22.579, the moment of the Echo Ridge crash - with no WoW crash report: the client is shut down in an orderly way (injector / client exit after a fault), not by a bot request (nothing in the code quits, logs out or runs console commands). Every traced shutdown came 0.6-1 s after the bot began closing on a target, and the opt-in recorder was never on. `errorlog.arm_light` now records for 1.5 s whenever combat movement starts on a new target and when a rest ends: every bot-decision probe, no window-drawing probes, per-frame movement probes at most every 0.1 s. "Auto crash capture" (General tab, on by default) controls it. |
| 2.88.0 | **Crash capture covers the first 30 s and the game update.** The 16:40 session (v2.87.0) shut down 4 s after Start (EditMode save 16:40:50.841), ~1.7-2.0 s into the first Fireball at a Prowler 27-31 yd away - just after the 1.5 s approach capture had ended, so the moment was not recorded. Most shutdowns have come 4-45 s after Start. Start now arms the light capture for 30 s, and the light mode no longer skips `u:izi.on_update` (izi's spell queue, which completes casts) - every per-frame stage (izi update, keybinds, movement pulse, render) is written at most every 0.05 s; only window drawing is skipped. |
| 2.89.0 | **Logging quiet by default - suspect in the shutdowns.** The session log lives under OneDrive (Documents) and was appended 10+ times a second (a BEAT line per bot tick, far more during capture) while OneDrive may be syncing or locking the same multi-MB file; the shutdowns are orderly (injector / client exit after a native fault) and the 16:40 one followed the heaviest write burst yet. BEAT heartbeat lines now need the new "Detailed session log" box (General tab, off by default), and "Auto crash capture" is now off by default. Errors, warnings, change-only trail lines and the PERF / MEM summaries (every 10-30 s) are still written. |
| 2.90.0 | **Engage distance.** Two Spells-tab sliders: "Melee attack distance" (1-5 yd, default 3) and "Ranged attack distance" (10-40 yd, default 25). `rotation.combat_range` now returns the engage distance: melee (by class, or a Cat / Bear druid / Stormstrike shaman) uses the melee slider; casters and hunters the ranged slider, capped 1 yd inside the longest ticked ranged damage spell's own `maximum_range` (`smart.max_range`). Combat movement closes to it - melee stand-off / in-position / hold now follow the slider (3 / 4 / 5 at the default, unchanged). Out of combat `smart.combat` holds every offensive role until the target is inside the engage distance (+1 yd), so a mage no longer opens with a max-range Fireball while still walking in; buffs, heals, defensives and openers (Charge) still run, and once in combat every spell casts at its own range. Not applied in Rotation Only. The attacker / pack scans keep a 10 yd minimum radius. Offline tests: 34 pass (3 new engage-gate checks). |
| 2.91.0 | **Buff Randoms.** New Spells-tab checkbox for mages (off by default). Out of combat, not resting or mounted and above 50% mana, the bot casts Arcane Intellect (best known rank) on the nearest friendly player within 30 yd in line of sight who has neither Arcane Intellect nor Arcane Brilliance; one player every 4 s, each player skipped for 10 min once buffed and 5 min after a failed cast (too low for the rank, out of reach). Offline tests: 36 pass. |
| 2.92.0 | **Buff Randoms casts spell 1459.** Other players are buffed with Arcane Intellect Rank 1 (spell 1459) rather than the best known rank, so a low-level player can always take it; the mage's own Arcane Intellect upkeep still uses the best rank. The option does nothing if 1459 is not in the spellbook. |
| 2.93.0 | **Melee / ranged slider shown by fighting style.** "Melee attack distance" is shown only while the character fights in melee, "Ranged attack distance" only while it fights at range. `gui.fights_in_melee` asks the same `rotation.is_melee` that picks the engage distance (ticked spells first - a Cat / Bear druid or Stormstrike shaman flips to melee as soon as it is ticked - then the class module), falling back to the class (Warrior, Rogue, Paladin = melee) before the spellbook scan; re-read at most every 0.5 s. |
| 2.94.0 | **100 yd enemy scan for every class.** `targeting.ENEMY_SCAN = 100`: grinding (was the zone's pull, usually 50), path mode (was the class scan range - melee 20, casters ~40 - or the path's pull), quest mob searches (was 50) and `rotation.scan_range` all use it, and `find_mobs`' cap rose from 80 to 100. The nearest valid mob (line of sight, reachable, level band, not unreachable) is attacked, closing to the GUI melee / ranged attack distance before the rotation opens. |
| 2.95.0 | **Flight paths, level cap, 360-degree scan.** (1) RestedXP `.fly <destination>` is now a goal of its own: walk to the flight master (found like any NPC goal), pick its taxi gossip option if it talks first, find the destination on the open map by name via `core.taxi.num_nodes / node_name` (never a cached index) and `take_node` it; while `is_flying` the whole bot stands down ("On a flight"). A destination missing from the map is logged with the known names and the step skipped. `.fp` talk goals count the open flight map as the NPC's frame, so learning a flight path completes. (2) No target more than 5 levels above the player - grinding / path mode (`find_mobs`, whatever the attack-level band) and every quest finder (`guide` fightable). (3) `targeting.scan_enemies`: every visible object, each living attackable non-player unit within 100 yd (3D) of the player in a full circle, split into an attack list and an avoid list (hostile, > 5 levels above); cached 0.25 s, run every bot tick. `find_mobs` now draws from it (the SDK enemy list left neutral mobs out). The avoid list feeds a movement danger map: steering rejects hops into / through a 12 yd radius around those mobs (unless the goal is there), and the look-ahead re-steers when the next 4 yd would enter one. |
| 2.96.0 | **Tight Sentinel paths indoors.** Sentinel shapes its paths from its own config (`get_path_opts`) - variation / humanising and corridor width that walk the character into door frames and walls inside buildings, caves and mines. When `player:is_indoors()`, every `move_to` now passes a copy of those options (Sentinel's documented `opts` argument) with each numeric key named like variation / jitter / random / deviation / wander / spread / noise / humaniz set to 0 (booleans false) and every corridor / path width or margin capped at 1.0 yd. Outdoors Sentinel's own settings are unchanged; `update_config` is not used (its UI rewrites it every frame). The option keys are undocumented, so the first read logs every key and value of `get_path_opts` and `get_corridor_opts` to the session log (`sentinel:` trail line). |
| 2.97.0 | **Mage: Frost Nova, then back off.** Frost Nova now fires when an enemy is within 6 yd (was 8); on a successful cast (mage only, not in Rotation Only) `movement.backpedal(3)` walks the character backwards with `core.input.move_backward_start` for 3 s and then `move_backward_stop`. While it runs it owns movement: walker and Sentinel are halted first, the walker is paused ("backpedal"), `may_issue` and `combat_engage` refuse every other move, the movement pulse does nothing else, and the rotation casts only what can be cast on the move (no cast locks). It always ends with `move_backward_stop` - on time, when a rest starts, on a halt / bot stop, and once whenever the movement module loads (a reload mid-backpedal cannot leave the key held) - and normal movement resumes on the next tick. Offline tests: 3 new checks. |
| 2.98.0 | **Follow patterns.** (1) Scan cooldown: `targeting.find_npc` / `find_named` walked the whole object list on every call, and the NPC walks (quest giver, flight master, merchant, food seller, trainer) called them every tick. A match is now reused for 2 s while it stays valid, alive and within the caller's range, rescanned after that or once it has gone. (2) The mini status window gains a HUD line: the current target's name and distance, or "Searching..." (the player's own target in Rotation Only); time and level share a line. The rest of the pattern set was already in place: distance-scaled re-aim at moving goals with a 3 yd dead zone (2.84.0), background re-plans for far goals (2.85.0), lazy optional modules. |
| 2.99.0 | **Eat and drink in the same rest.** A rest only consumed what had crossed its own line (Eat at HP % / Drink at MP %), so a caster drank at 20% mana, stood up with 70% health, and sat down again to eat soon after. Once a rest starts for either reason, the other resource is now topped up in the same sit-down when it is under 95% and a usable food / drink is in the bags: both are used on the same tick and the rest ends when both are full. Logged as `rest: also eating / also drinking`. |
| 2.100.0 | **Frost Nova backpedal 2.5 s.** The walk backwards after Frost Nova is now 2.5 s (was 3 s); everything else about it is unchanged. |
| 2.101.0 | **Loot first, then rest.** Looting already ran ahead of resting, but right after a kill the corpse is not lootable or queued yet and the nearby-corpse scan runs a tick later, so the rest could sit down first. `loot.hold_rest` now holds a rest from STARTING while looting has worked in the last 1.5 s, combat ended less than 1.5 s ago, or corpses are still queued and looting has acted in the last 3 s. A rest already under way is never interrupted. |
| 2.102.0 | **Nothing until every attacker is dead.** `targeting.attackers(player)` counts every living unit from the 360-degree scan (attack and avoid lists, so neutral and too-high mobs count) that is in combat and targeting the player or the pet; cached 0.2 s. While it is above zero, looting does not run (it used to check only while the player's own combat flag was up, and only the SDK enemy list), a rest does not start, and a rest under way is cancelled so the character fights back. Both resume once every attacker is dead, loot first (2.101.0). |
| 2.236.0 | **Ameisen navigation replaces Sentinel.** The tested local fork 2.235.3-ameisen (`scripts\Master_Farmer_Bot`) folded back into the Grindbot with its name, `MASTER_FARMER_ERRORS` logs, `mfg` settings and TBC / Forever header gate. Out-of-combat travel goes through AmeisenNav (`_G.AmeisenNav.client`, server 127.0.0.1:47110): avoid plans built from `find_path` + a detour point, no `/raycast`, grind paths sent as one 60-node route, seamless `move_to` re-targets, `flags = 17` (Chaikin smoothing + VALIDATE_MAS) on every path, map change via `core.get_map_id()`. GUI status row "Ameisen". Learned hazards for the Ameisen meshes in `scripts_data\mfb\`. New `ameisen/` folder: AmeisenNav plugin, server bridge / bat / config and install README; skipped by the manifest and the local build. The 1.9 GB mmaps and the server exe stay local (not in git). |
| 2.237.0 | **Gathering mode (port of EP_Herb_Mine, PORT_PLAYBOOK.md).** New Gathering tab after Questing: Enable Gathering (runs alone - Grinding, Questing and Gathering are mutually exclusive), Herbalism / Mining, Fight While Patrolling, Use Mount, Keep Gathered Materials, Train Professions, Teleport Alarm, scan range / interval, max gather time, fight-back health % and range, alarm yards. Lazy `gather/` pack (loader.ensure_gather): 24 routes and the herb / ore node table copied from the script; route picked in file order by faction and rank under the client cap (375 on TBC, 300 on Classic / Forever); patrol as Ameisen route chunks; node scan by entry id or English name, `core.input.use_object` once a second at 5 yd, every loot slot taken, looted / blacklisted GUIDs skipped; fight-back through the existing targeting + Spells tab rotation; mount / Travel Form for travel; Find Herbs / Find Minerals kept up; profession trainer trips (8 trainers). Vendor uses the route's merchant, never sells Trade Goods with Keep Gathered Materials on (Dreamfoil, Mountain Silversage, Arcane Crystal always kept) and never deletes items while gathering. Header / gamever accept "Vanilla" (WoW Classic). |
| 2.238.0 | **Gathering: hunter ammo and route food / drink.** New gather/supply.lua trips to each route's ammo_vendor / food_vendor. Hunters buy the best arrows (bow / crossbow) or shot (gun) the vendor sells that they can use, under Ammo Buy Below (100) up to Ammo Stop At (500), then use it into the ammo slot. Food / drink trips start at Restock Food / Drink At (5) and buy up to Food / Drink Stock (20): the route's food / drink when usable at the character's level, else the best usable item from data/consumables. Mages never buy; warriors / rogues buy no drink. Purchases are measured from the bag counts; no gold, or three purchases that do not arrive, end the trip. Off on WoW Forever (vendor items unreadable). New Gathering tab options for all of it. |
| 2.239.0 | **Questing travel and flight masters (Ameisen).** 1. A far guide goal (811 yd in Burning Steppes) whose height is only a guess (RestedXP gives x, y; the Ameisen server has no height query) answered no_path once, and every kill goal at that spot was skipped as unreachable, so the guide never moved on. Points more than 150 yd away are no longer judged unreachable, and a far leg with no path is walked as 120 yd hops (through Ameisen) at the ground height read there, until the goal is in range. 2. A quest giver beside the flight point the player stood on was walked to through the recorded road's end under the player: it arrived at once, every tick, 44 yd from the NPC. A road point under the player is no longer a hop. 3. Flight masters: the NPC with the FLIGHTMASTER flag is picked first, GUIDs are compared as text (userdata on this client), a gossip window gets 3 looks before the NPC is given up (its options are logged, also on .fly steps), and only REACHABLE nodes on the flight map are taken. |
| 2.239.1 | **Game crash while walking a navigation path: route cliff scan switched off.** Three 2026-10-08 questing sessions (11:06, 11:19, 11:31) ended with the game gone and no Lua error and no WoW crash report, each 1-15 s into a fresh navigation path toward a far mob or goal, with AmeisenNav and with Marty Nav alike - so not the navigator. The one thing that runs only during such a walk is `movement/terrain.lua` scan_path (2.235.0): on every new path and every 10 yd walked it fired up to 20 `core.get_height_for_position` rays in one frame, every 2 yd up to 40 yd ahead, into terrain the client may not have loaded; the crash times line up with those scan points. `K.TERRAIN_ROUTE_SCAN = false` (movement/const.lua) skips it. The stalled-wall check (near the player, after 4 s without progress) and the learned hazards stay on. |
| 2.240.0 | **Mage rotation and resting vs navigation (review of the 2026-10-08 logs).** 1. Rest: food and drink were used in the same tick and the client dropped the drink (13:08: MP 3 -> 17 in 30 s, then 17 -> 89 in 31 s drinking alone; rests 52-60 s instead of 37-40) - now ONE use per tick, the drink first, the food `FOOD_AFTER_DRINK` (1.2 s) later. 2. The Food aura was not recognised (433 listed as a drink, Conjured Sweet Roll's 1131 missing), so food was re-eaten every 15 s and the threat check could walk the character off its meal - the aura is now matched by id, by the used item's own spell (`core.quests.get_item_spell`) and by NAME (Food / Drink / Refreshment / Food & Drink); the id list is fixed too. 3. A rest no longer builds the food / water lists every 0.1 s at full health and mana (early exit; not during a regen wait). 4. A mage who conjures water no longer quests under the drink line with too little mana to conjure - it waits for the mana. 5. Loot hold under the rest line holds the tick (`resting.below_line`) instead of letting the quest start an approach at 1% mana. 6. Quest approach: a gap of over 1 s (a rest) restarts `APPROACH_WAIT`, so the first tick after a rest no longer gives up the path ("closing directly" at 64 yd). 7. "Other known spells": utility spells are never listed (Conjure / Teleport / Portal / Create / Find / Ritual / Track prefixes, Blink, Slow Fall, Polymorph, Amplify / Dampen Magic, curse removal, Arcane Brilliance / Intellect, Evocation, Hearthstone) - a ticked Conjure Mana Agate fired 106 times in one session ahead of Frostbolt; other spells are cast guarded only (no unguarded `spell:cast` fallback) and a refused one waits 8 s. 8. `smart.upkeep` never casts over a cast or channel (Ice Barrier went out mid-Frostbolt). 9. Cast / channel locks are held while the player is still casting or channelling (up to 12 s): Evocation / Blizzard (8 s) were cut at 3.7 s. 10. Conjure water / food: a cast that does not start within 1 s is a failure (FAIL_GAP back-off) instead of a stop / walk loop. 11. `K.NATIVE_RAYS = false`: no `core.graphics.trace_line` / `native_intersect` (line of sight = `player:los_to`; avoidance corridor reads open) - suspected in the five native crashes of 2026-10-08. |
| 2.241.0 | **RestedXP questing: cached step waypoints, continuous movement.** 1. Per-step waypoint cache (quest/guide.lua): the step's waypoint list and arrow point are kept per step, keyed by the step number and its open goals, so a momentary empty read no longer leaves a goal with 0 waypoints; dropped when the step or its open goals change. 2. guide.route(goal, kind): kill / collect / xp goals walk every step waypoint (when none is tagged with the goal - goal_num is the step index) plus the arrow; a lone point becomes a patrol (the point + 6 points 25 yd around it, looped) instead of standing on 'waiting at'. Accept / turn-in / talk / fly goals are unchanged. 3. IS.walk_route (quest/engine.lua, outside tick_inner): the next point is handed to the path 12 yd before the current one is reached, moving or not; after a fight / loot / rest the walk resumes at the nearest route point; a point reported blocked / off the mesh is skipped for the next one instead of 'cannot reach'. |
| 2.242.0 | **Spells: no assumed AoE.** 1. Hunter Multi-Shot (2643 and every rank) is a single-target damage shot (class catalog role damage, after Arcane Shot) - it no longer waits for 2+ enemies. 2. data/spell_categories.lua: the AoE keyword rule is gone; a spell is AoE only by an explicit id or the class catalog, never from its name. 3. Spellbook scan: spells not in the class catalog are listed as 'Other DPS spells' (description mentions damage; cast at the target) or 'Other healing spells' (heals, no damage; cast on the player below the heal line), unticked by default; anything else stays under 'Other known spells'. Never classed as AoE. |
| 2.243.0 | **Vendor: selling, repair and buying on every client.** 1. Buying reads get_vendor_item_info's documented fields first (item_id, item_name, cost, quantity, is_usable); an item the character cannot use is never bought; each purchase logs 'Bought Nx <name> for Xg Ys Zc'. 2. Forever is no longer blocked: since the 2026-10-06 core vendor rows read there too. Buying stands down only when an open merchant's rows all read item id 0 (older core). 3. Repair short of gold sells grey junk first (whatever the Sell toggles say), then checks the cost again - the 2026-10-08 Forever trips left with 176 / 259 copper against a 384 / 452 repair having sold nothing. 4. A sell pass that sells nothing logs once per trip why every item was kept (keep list, food / water, consumable, no item info, quality not ticked...), and 'selling is off' when the Sell toggle is off. |
| 2.244.0 | **Rogue: Eureka! (1259812).** Added to the Rogue catalog as the first damage spell (ticked by default, found by name or by spell id 1259812), so it is cast every time it is off cooldown in a fight. New catalog flag `either` (smart.lua try): cast at the target, and on the player if that is refused. |
| 2.245.0 | **Enemy future position (documented method).** geometry.future_position(unit, t) = position + normalize(get_direction) * get_movement_speed * t (a unit not moving stays put; guarded, capped at 3 s), and geometry.future_distance. Used by movement/combat.lua predicted_distance (izi predict_position only as a fallback) and by smart.lua ground spells (Blizzard, Flamestrike, Rain of Fire, Hurricane...), now aimed where the target will be when the spell lands: cast time + 0.3 s (a channel: + 1 s), at most 2 s ahead. |
| 2.246.0 | **spell_helper gate on every cast.** New spellcheck.lua wraps common/utility/spell_helper (has_spell_equipped, is_spell_on_cooldown, is_spell_in_range, is_spell_within_angle, is_spell_in_line_of_sight(_position), get_spell_cost / can_afford_spell, is_spell_castable(_position)). smart.lua (rotation, ground spells, Arcane Intellect), racials.lua, buffs.lua and conjure.lua cast through spellcheck.cast_safe / cast / cast_position: is_spell_castable first (self casts skip facing and range), then the same izi call. Never a stall: no helper or an error lets the cast through; a spell refused ~10 s in a row for no reason other than cooldown / resource / range is logged once with the failing check and left to izi's checks for 30 s. |
| 2.247.0 | **Death zones: avoid an area after dying there 3 times.** New deathzones.lua. Every death records where the body fell and the killer (npc id, name). 3+ deaths to the same enemy (an unknown killer matches any) within 60 yd of each other inside an hour make that area a death zone: centre = average of the deaths, radius = spread + 25 (30-80 yd), avoided 10 min + 5 min per further death (max 60), extended when dying there again; saved to scripts_data/mfb/death_zones.txt with its end time so a reload keeps it. While active: targeting.find_mobs and the quest finders (guide fightable) skip mobs inside it and the killer npc within 20 yd past its edge (attackers are still fought); a kept navigation zone (radius capped 30) routes around it, removed when it expires (movement/zones Z.remove_area); grind waypoints and quest route points inside it are skipped (a quest goal with every point inside waits outside with a note); the Sylvanas Target Selector's pull flag is off within 40 yd of it (ts_override_helper set_pull_allowed, ON_CHANGE) and back on when away. |
| 2.248.0 | **Spell prediction picks the AoE spells, every class.** New predict.lua (common/modules/spell_prediction, cached 0.2 s per spell and target). Every catalog AoE spell's hits are predicted with its own shape, range and cast time: ground spells (Blizzard, Flamestrike, Rain of Fire, Hurricane) CIRCLE + MOST_HITS via get_cast_position; self-centred (Arcane Explosion, Whirlwind, Consecration, Holy Nova, Blast Wave, Hellfire...) a circle around the player; cones (Cone of Cold, Dragon's Breath - new catalog flag `cone`, `angle` 60) get_cone_list toward the target; target-centred (Chain Lightning, Cleave, Swipe, Seed of Corruption...) a circle around the target. smart.lua: the AoE condition uses the predicted count (radius count without the module); the AoE spells are tried most predicted hits first (list order among equals); ground spells are cast at the predicted most-hits position (the 2.245.0 future position as the fallback). |
| 2.249.0 | **Spell queue casting, every class.** New castq.lua (common/modules/spell_queue). smart.lua's rotation casts are queued at priority 1 (the rotation's order decides; higher priorities stay free for interrupt / dispel plugins and manual keys): GCD spells queue_spell_target / queue_spell_position, off-GCD spells (izi skips_gcd, or a client base cooldown with gcd 0) the _fast variants one at a time (is_in_fast_queue), the same spell at the same target not re-queued within 0.25 s. QUEUE AHEAD: the next spell is picked in the last 0.35 s of a cast (never during a channel) and goes out the moment the cast ends - checked with izi is_castable (skip_casting) then, with spell_helper (spellcheck) otherwise. 'Spell Queue Casting' on the Spells tab (on); unticked, or without the module, casts go through izi as before. |
| 2.250.0 | **Rogue cast nothing in combat (2.246-2.249).** The spell_helper gate (spellcheck.lua, 2.246.0) refused the Rogue's strikes for reasons the client did not share; every refusal put the spell on the rotation's 1-2 s fail gap, and the bypass after 40 refusals took a minute or more per spell - the 732dca Rogue went from 99 kills (2.240) to 0 (2.246) and died 3+ times at every Rockjaw camp. Now spell_helper only ADVISES: a refusal blocks only when the client confirms the spell is on cooldown (core.spell_book.get_spell_cooldown); any other disagreement is logged once per spell and reason (errorlog 'spellcheck', 2 min) and izi's own checks decide. The spell-queue path (2.249.0) gates every queued spell with izi's full is_castable_to_unit / _to_position check (it skips cast_safe). Same spellcheck.lua in sp-pull 1133, ameisen-follow 1.5.4, mf-route-tool 4.8.4. |
| 2.251.0 | **Rogue still cast nothing: the spell queue held its strikes.** 2.249.0 queued every rotation spell with allow_movement = false; the spell queue then waits while the player moves, and a melee Rogue steps after its target the whole fight - nothing went out while smart counted the spells as cast. Now instants and spells usable on the move are queued with movement allowed (and izi's check gets skip_moving for them); cast-time spells still wait. STUCK QUEUE: a GCD spell still in the queue snapshot 1.5 s after it was queued is purged; 3 of them switch spell-queue casting off for the session (warning + 'rotation' trail) and the rotation casts through izi. DIAGNOSTIC: in a fight with a target and nothing cast for 3 s, a 'rotation' trail line every 10 s lists each spell and why it was passed over (unticked, retry gap, role condition, out of reach, cast refused...), with distance, power, combo points and queue state. |
| 2.252.0 | **Rogue: queued strikes refused by izi's castable check.** The 2.251.0 diagnostic (03:29 log): at 0.1-2.4 yd with 105 energy, Sinister Strike and Eureka! reached the cast and were refused every time - izi's is_castable_to_unit, the spell-queue path's gate since 2.250.0, said no; spell_helper had no objection. On 2.240 the same Rogue cast through izi cast_safe with the plain izi cast as the fallback. Now a cast the queue's izi check refuses goes the direct way (cast_safe, then cast) in the same tick, and the diagnostic line carries izi's refusal reason ('cast refused (izi: <reason>)'). |
| 2.253.0 | **Ready timing: spells fire the first tick they can (every class).** The 03:33 Rogue log (2.252.0, casting again): Sinister Strike was tried every ~1.65 s - FAIL_GAP 1.5 s + a tick. Energy-short attempts were refused and the spell then sat out 1.5 s even after the energy was back (10 energy/s: up to 15 energy wasted per strike, energy capped). smart.lua try() now checks READY first, without the fail gap: the spell's real cost (core.spell_book.get_spell_costs, any power type) against the player's power ('pooling energy 30/45' in the diagnostic), and the global cooldown (izi gcd_remains; with the spell queue the next spell may queue in the GCD's last 0.35 s); a GCD cast holds the next GCD spell 0.3 s so a second attempt does not go out before the server starts the GCD. Off-GCD spells and Auto Shot are not held by the GCD. |
| 2.254.0 | **Rogue: finishers waited for 5 combo points; queued strikes stuck.** 03:38 log (2.253.0): (1) get_spell_costs lists a finisher's combo points as a cost (power type 4, '5'), so Slice and Dice / Eviscerate showed 'pooling power 4 3/5' - only mana, rage, focus, energy and runic power are pooled now; the finisher rules own combo points. (2) With energy to spare, Sinister Strike was re-queued every 0.3 s for 2 s and never left the spell queue; each re-queue reset its time in castq, so the stuck check never fired. The first queue time is kept now; a spell still in the queue after 1.0 s is purged and cast directly the same tick (GCD hold cleared), and 2 stuck spells in a row switch spell-queue casting off for the session (izi casts, as on 2.252). |
| 2.255.0 | **Gnome Escape Artist (20589) on root, stun or sap.** It was only looked at inside the combat rotation, only for roots (cc 'root'), and never cast in any 2026-10-09 session. Now racials.escape_tick runs every 0.25 s from the main loop, in and out of combat (a sap comes before any fight) and outside the racial gap: rooted, stunned or sapped (izi is_rooted / is_stunned / is_sapped) -> Escape Artist on the player, each attempt logged as a 'racial' trail ('Escape Artist: stunned - cast' / 'refused by the game' / 'on cooldown'). Note: the game lets Escape Artist break roots and snares only; under a stun or sap it is tried and the game may refuse. |
| 2.256.0 | **Corpse close enough to revive; Rogue Pick Pocket.** 1. death.lua measured the walk to the 'safe spot' (up to ~14 yd beside the corpse when mobs stand on it) and stopped at 32 yd from it - the ghost could stand ~46 yd from the corpse and press revive for good. Now the distance is to the CORPSE: walk to 20 yd (the safe spot only while it is within that), and every 3 refused revives step closer - 10 yd, then onto the corpse. 'death' trail lines: each revive try with its distance, each step closer. (The 03:33 / 03:38 runs that ended at 83 / 110 yd were stopped by reloads mid-run, not stuck.) 2. Rogue: in the Stealth opener, a HUMANOID target within 5 yd gets one Pick Pocket (921) - cast directly, not queued, no GCD hold - then the opener goes on in the same tick whether it worked or not; a loot window it opens is emptied and closed; 'rotation' trail per attempt. |
| 2.257.0 | **Audit fixes (uncommitted).** 1. write_data_file APPENDS (core.lua stub, confirmed 2026-10-06): deathzones.lua, debuglog.lua and movement/hazards.lua now empty the file with create_data_file before each write (every save had appended every zone / hazard / the whole debug buffer again, and a missing file was never written at all); main.lua comment corrected. 2. UNLOAD: main.lua returns { on_unload } - the session counter is bumped (every callback of the old instance sees is_stale() and returns), path_runner.stop, movement.halt (AmeisenNav stop + walker halt), strafe.stop, movement keys released, the death-zone Target Selector session released, the debug log flushed. _G.MasterFarmer_Grindbot is kept: header.lua, events.lua and gui.lua read it. on_render: movement / pets / debug-log ticks moved behind the stale check. 3. deathzones: the Target Selector override is a ts_override_helper SESSION (create_session / set_pull_allowed / tick / destroy) instead of the permanent set_pull_allowed - destroy() restores the player's own setting when leaving a zone, on Stop and on unload. 4. AmeisenNav 1.5.2: follower saves simple_movement's threshold / final_threshold before its walk and restores them in F.stop; header version string corrected. 5. plugin_loader/http_loader.lua: the uncommitted change reading bare globals files / version reverted to the committed data.files / data.version. |
| 2.258.0 | **Quest and RestedXP calls after quest/api_examples.lua.** npc.lua: turn-in selects the quest on one tick and presses Continue (complete_quest) on a LATER tick once the progress panel is there (QUEST_PROGRESS or get_open_quest_info) - 2.187.0 pressed it in the same tick, before any panel; the reward count comes from get_num_quest_choices, RestedXP's goal.reward is taken when in range, and choices without item links take 1, never get_quest_reward(0); accept and turn-in refuse a panel that shows another quest (get_open_quest_info); is_complete asks is_quest_complete(quest_id) first; abandon uses abandon_quest_by_id when the core has it; one gossip-id rule (npc.gossip_ids_are_rows: exact build with _ps, or Vanilla, gives row indexes - never compared or stored). engine.lua: guide-file gossip option ids are compared only on builds with real ids; RestedXP's reward choice is passed to npc.turn_in. guide.lua: step num 0 is no step; step records keep active / requires / requires_step / level / label and goal records units / objective / objective_max / reward / money; a waypoint with map_id 0 or on another continent is never stored (the step cache only holds usable points, and a cached arrow is not reused across continents); an inactive step holds its goals, and one that waits on nothing is skipped once with skip_current_step (guide.skip_inactive, note 'step N not active yet'); kill-target names take goal.units; quest objectives by quest id (get_quest_objectives) before the log leader boards; the quest log is read on Forever with the 2026-10-06 core. api_examples.lua: stub reference, gossip-row rule (stub: _ps builds) and the quest_log index fixed. |
| 2.259.0 | **Melee pathing with the izi positioning API.** combat_engage, melee only (warrior, rogue, paladin, feral druid, enhancement shaman, hunter melee band): a target running at the player (is_moving / is_moving_towards_me) that predict_distance(0.5 s) puts inside swing range is waited for, facing it - the chase is halted, no running into or through each other; a target moving away or across is intercepted - the direct chase aims at predict_position(lead), lead = gap / run speed clamped 0.3-1.5 s; the melee stand distance is never inside the target's model - at least both get_bounding_radius values + 0.5 yd (1.5 yd minimum), capped at the GUI melee distance; standing inside a still target (under 0.75 yd) takes one short walker step back to that stand (at most every 3 s, not while the profile wants the rear arc), and the in-position latch keeps the band check from halting it; the rogue rear-arc step also re-flanks when is_behind_future(0.5 s) says the target is turning. Ranged classes are unchanged. The 2026-10-09 Rogue log stood 0.0-0.6 yd from the target's centre on most combat ticks. |
| 2.260.0 | **Previous melee movement back; fleeing targets finished; rest not broken off.** movement/combat.lua is the 2.258.0 code again - the 2.259.0 izi positioning changes (charging-target hold, predict_position intercept, bounding-radius stand, step back, is_behind_future re-flank) are removed; the chase and the rogue's rear-arc step are as before. New targeting.fight_unfinished: the current kill target (or a game target the bot engaged) alive within 40 yd and in combat or wounded - a fleeing mob drops its target, so it was no 'attacker': the 17:05 log walked off to loot (and pulled again) with a Tunnel Rat Scout 0.1-9 yd away, which came back with help; the rogue died at 2% walking to a corpse. Looting (loot.under_attack) and the rest (loot.hold_rest) wait while it is unfinished; the grind switches back to it ('Finishing a fleeing target') and chases it down; the approach watch never blacklists an engaged target while it runs (the kill timeout still applies). Resting: once sat down the rest is not broken off to walk away from a nearby mob (it stood up at 82% HP mid-meal when the food aura read missed); a mob that attacks still ends it through combat. |
| 2.261.0 | **Wand only when low on mana.** targeting.start_auto_attack (run on every engage) started the wand for a mage, priest or warlock with a wand equipped at ANY mana, so the wand fired between spells with plenty of mana to cast; the rotation's own rule (wand below the class's 'Wand below mana %' slider, sp_wand, default 20) was never asked there. Now the wand auto-attack starts only below that slider; above it the melee swing is armed as for other casters and a wand already firing is stopped. The 'nothing cast' trace shows mana % for mana users (it printed get_power(3), energy - always 0 on a mage). |
| 2.262.0 | **AmeisenNav 1.6.0: rolling 20-yard windows; handoff to combat movement.** AmeisenNav move_to now walks validated windows: an unsmoothed server path from the player's position and height, its first 20 yd as 5-yard waypoints, checked before walking through the nav server only (ground height at four probe heights; climb / drop / cliff legs re-planned unsmoothed and spliced in; 1-2-3 yd side probes keep every waypoint 2 yd from walls, ledges and drops, corridors walked down the middle; cached objects pushed aside within the measured room). 5 yd before a window ends the next is planned from the player and swapped in without a key release, until the destination. New client:handoff('simple' / 'combat') and move_to opts.handoff = { at = yd } (simple_movement finishes the last yards without a stop). Grindbot: combat taking the player from a running Ameisen leg hands the walker over (sentinel N.handoff) instead of stopping it, so the chase continues with no forward-key release; older AmeisenNav without handoff: stopped as before. Recorded routes (follow_path) keep the 1.5.0 path check. |
| 2.263.0 | **Indoors and close enough to interact (AmeisenNav 1.6.1).** The 10:11 Thelsamar log never reached Mountaineer Stormpike one floor up: quest arrival and talk reach were flat (ARRIVE_DZ 12), so the bot stood under him and the game refused three interacts; the 8-yard close-in was a straight-line move that cannot take stairs; AmeisenNav counted 6 yd of height as arrived. Quest engine: NPC / object targets walk with exact height (walk_to exact - same floor within 2.5 yd; the door stop too), the talk reach is 3D; quest objects are clicked from 6 yd at most (was 8) and a use that brought nothing is retried after walking within 3 yd. Sentinel: no straight-line move to a point more than 2.5 yd higher or lower - it is pathed. AmeisenNav 1.6.1: arrival needs the same floor (arrive_dz 2.5); a walk that ends on another floor is re-planned; an object or NPC on the destination (matched against the requested destination, not the server's snapped end) ends the final window at its edge (radius + body + 0.5 yd) and makes the destination height exact; already at the edge is arrived at once. |
| 2.264.0 | **RestedXP / quest / event API audit.** Every core.addons.rested_xp, core.quests and game-event call checked against the API docs and the stubs: function names, argument order (get_quest_log_leader_board(obj, log_index)), gossip ids passed straight back (gossip_option_id / quest_id, never stored), QUEST_GREETING by index, get_quest_reward(0) for no choice (stub default 0; complete_quest is Continue), LOOT_BIND_CONFIRM slot - 1, one registered event callback - all correct. Fixed: RestedXP goal records keep money_greater_than / item_total / item_operator / item_eq; .itemcount / .money goals are conditions RestedXP ticks itself - passed over like text_only (fallback only), they used to fall to 'goto' and walk to the waypoint; a .complete goal's objective comes from goal.objective (exact, language-free) before the text match; the guide snapshot is re-read at once on QUEST_LOG_UPDATE / QUEST_ITEM_UPDATE / QUEST_ACCEPTED / QUEST_TURNED_IN / QUEST_FINISHED (0.1 s gap, never in combat) instead of waiting out its 0.25 s window. Events: quiet edges recorded (QUEST_LOG_UPDATE, QUEST_ITEM_UPDATE, LOOT_OPENED/CLOSED, ITEM_TEXT_BEGIN/CLOSED, PLAYER_INTERACTION_MANAGER_FRAME_HIDE) without moving the window counter; UI_ERROR_MESSAGE read by string id (args[3]: ERR_INV_FULL, ERR_USE_TOO_FAR ...) with the text only as the old-client fallback; events.too_far_since. Vendor (both paths), trainer and quest objects: an interact refused as too far is followed by a walk up to the NPC / object (same floor) instead of the same click again; a refused object use does not count as one of its tries. |
| 2.265.0 | **Recorded roads no longer flip the walk.** The 10:37 Thelsamar log: accepting at the inn 30 yd away, travel_routes.hop swapped between three recorded roads (LochmodanINN_to_LochmodanFP, thunderbrewINN_to_LochModanINN, LochmodanFP_to_Badlands...) every 0.1-1.5 s, each a different hop point - the AmeisenNav log shows move_to / cancel alternating between two spots 55 yd apart for 90 s and the character never arriving. Now: no road for a trip under 80 yd (the navmesh walks it); a hop is never further from the destination than the player (+10 yd); the road chosen for a destination is kept while it still gives a hop instead of every road being re-scored each tick. Quest walks and vendor trips both use it. |
| 2.266.0 | **Recorded inn / flight-path roads removed.** At the user's request the saved PathTool roads (LochmodanINN_to_LochmodanFP, thunderbrewINN_to_LochModanINN, LochmodanFP_to_Badlands_to_BlackrockmountainSearingorge and every other data/ek_alliance_routes road) are no longer used in navigation: travel_routes.lua, data/ek_alliance_routes.lua and tools/build_alliance_routes.py are deleted; quest walks and vendor trips navigate the destination itself (AmeisenNav windows). The food / water run keeps its innkeeper spots in data/ek_inns.lua (10 inns, positions only, taken from the old roads' inn ends). |
| 2.267.0 | **Far destinations with an unknown height are walked, not blacklisted (AmeisenNav 1.6.2).** The 10:53 Loch Modan log: accepting The Trogg Threat 514 yd away, the waypoint height was the player's own (coords_helper terrain height answers 0 on WoW Forever), AmeisenNav answered no_path, and W.mark_fail('unreachable') blacklisted 12 yd around the quest giver for 15 minutes - 'cannot reach' every tick, three stall recoveries that could not ask again, the step skipped without the quest. Now: a refused path to a point over 150 yd away is a plain failure (asked again after the 3 s hold), never a blacklist; the 120-yard far hop uses the player's height when there is no terrain height. AmeisenNav 1.6.2: on no_path / end_off_mesh / unreachable the same x, y is asked again at +-8, 20, 40, 80 and +150 yd in one batched request and the first full path is walked (the destination takes that height). |
| 2.268.0 | **Object / game_object API: elites, true movement direction, indoors (AmeisenNav 1.6.3).** Elites: targeting.too_strong (get_classification 1 elite, 2 rare elite, 3 world boss) - a grind scan (find_mobs without an npc list) and a quest path pull (guide.find_path_mob) skip one unless it is 3+ levels under the player; a grind profile that names the npc, and the guide's own kill targets, are unchanged (only the level was checked before, so a same-level elite was pulled like any mob). geometry.future_position follows get_movement_direction (where the unit moves) instead of get_direction (where it faces), facing only as the fallback - a backing-up or strafing unit was predicted the wrong way (spell leads, chases, threat ETA). AmeisenNav 1.6.3: while the player is indoors (is_indoors) the window keeps 1.25 yd from walls and 0.4 yd extra from objects (outdoors 2.0 / 1.0) - doorways and furnished rooms are narrower. |
| 2.269.0 | **Terrain heights from izi.get_terrain_height with the right ray start (IZI Maps).** The ray starts extra_height yards above the CHARACTER (fixed in the 2026-10-06 update; it used to cast from height 0). movement/terrain.lua: izi.get_terrain_height is now a height source between coords_helper (answers 0 on WoW Forever) and core.get_height_for_position, with extra = the caller's ray start - player z (the far hop asks from 80 yd up, so a hill above the character is read); an answer above the ray start or a 0 away from height 0 is rejected. Guide waypoints within 120 yd: ray 30 yd up outdoors (a waypoint up a hill was read at the player's height), 4 yd indoors (a larger start hits the floor above). movement/util ground_z: the izi fallback casts from 4 yd above the expected height, not above the character. |
| 2.270.0 | **Rotation and potions use the IZI game_object forecasts.** smart.lua try(): a damage spell (not self, not heal) is skipped when the target is damage immune (is_damage_immune - an evading mob running home, a bubble: 'target immune'), and a spell with a cast time is skipped when the target is under 35% health and time_to_die says it dies before the cast lands ('target dies before the cast lands' - the next instant or the auto attack finishes it). healing.lua: in combat the health potion threshold compares the health forecast 1.5 s ahead (get_health_percentage_inc) when that is lower than the current health, so the potion goes in before a big hit lands. |
| 2.271.0 | **Paths planned while moving (AmeisenNav 1.6.4); IZI combat-finished and spell-cancelled callbacks.** The 11:13 Loch Modan nav log showed 'navigating -> navigating.window' at every 20-yard window: the next window was asked for 5 yd before the end (full path + two validation batches, 0.5-1 s, against ~0.7 s of running), so the walker stopped, released the keys and restarted 0.25 s later - a pause each window, and a restart from where the request was made could turn the character back. AmeisenNav 1.6.4 builds the next window from the current window's END the moment that window starts walking; 5 yd before the end it is appended to the points still ahead of the player and swapped in as one list (no stop, no key release, nothing behind the player); planning from the player stays the fallback within 2 yd of the end, and a re-plan drops a prepared window. Grindbot callbacks.lua: izi.on_combat_finish (player only) drops targeting's attacker cache, tells loot the exact end of the fight and re-reads RestedXP; izi.on_spell_cancel (player's casts only) clears the spell from castq and the rotation's back-off, gives the unused GCD back and lifts the movement cast pause (Lk.cast_cancelled). Registered once per session (hot-reload safe), unsubscribed in on_unload. |
| 2.272.0 | **AmeisenNav 1.6.5: strafe, jump, strafe for stuck positions.** The 11:2x nav log: from (-5760,-2599) every request failed no_path at once, every 3 s for 90 s - the height search found nothing at any height because the START was off the navmesh, and the walk ladder never ran (no walk ever started). New manoeuvre (follower): forward held, strafe one side 0.4 s with a jump 0.2 s in, then the other side 0.4 s, all keys released; the starting side alternates; the walker is paused meanwhile. Walk ladder: 1 strafe-jump-strafe, 2 re-plan, 3 strafe-jump-strafe (other side), 4 detour, 5 back off, 6 jump + re-plan (max_stuck 6). No walk possible: after 2 failed plans from the same spot (no_path / start_off_mesh / unreachable / end_off_mesh) the manoeuvre runs on its own (every 4th failure backs off), and the next request starts from the new spot. |
| 2.272.1 | **AmeisenNav can be encoded.** The three goto-continue loops (anav/avoid.lua refresh and blocking_object, anav/follow.lua find_by_name) are written without goto - same behaviour - because the Prometheus encoder parses Lua 5.1, which has no goto. The encoded AmeisenNav 1.6.5 passes the same 48 offline checks as the source, and is packaged with the Nav Server installer (which now bundles the encoded plugin) in _MF_NAVIGATION (local, not published). |
| 2.273.0 | **Avoid Mob Packs (GUI option, General tab, off by default).** When on, hostile mobs at or below the player's level put their aggro range into the movement danger map (20 yd at the same level, 1 yd less per level below, 5 yd minimum, +2 yd margin), so travel paths, the local walker and the move look-ahead steer around them. Grey mobs (never aggro), mobs already in combat, neutral mobs and the current kill target are not avoided; a zone that holds the destination or the player is never planned around. Off: unchanged (only mobs more than 5 levels above are avoided). |
| 2.274.0 | **Adaptive Rotation (Spells tab, on by default) - every class.** New adapt.lua. Every spell the player casts is read (core.register_on_spell_cast_callback, the bot's casts and the player's own) and the target's health drop that follows is measured as its damage (drops on an auto-attack or pet swing are not measured); a newly learned spell starts from the damage in its description, through health_prediction speculate_damage. The ticked damage / filler / finisher / execute spells are tried by damage per second of casting (damage per mana under 30 % mana); a spell that kills the target now goes first, fastest cast first; a DoT is skipped in a fight shorter than half its duration; a cooldown when combat_forecast (SHORT, get_min_combat_length + is_valid_forecast_logic) says the fight is too short; a cast whose target health_prediction get_incoming_damage says is dead before it lands; and in melee a cast-time spell waits for the swing it would clip (auto_attack_helper). Kills are timed. Everything is saved per character GUID in scripts_data/mfg/adapt_<guid>.txt, loaded whenever that character plays, and updated on a new spell or rank (measured damage rescaled to the new rank). |
| 2.275.0 | **Coords Helper (fixed 2026-10-06) used properly + AmeisenNav 1.6.6.** movement/terrain.lua called coords_helper get_terrain_height without extra_height, so every ray started 4 yd above the character and terrain above that was unreadable (march window held at 2.5 yd): the caller's ray start is now passed as extra_height = start - character z, an answer above the start is refused, a 0 only means 'nothing there' away from height 0; the ground-reading window is 15 yd outdoors for every height source and 2.5 yd indoors (is_indoors), so slopes and ramps above the character are judged instead of refused; map floors use the helper's is_valid_map_coords. AmeisenNav 1.6.6 (anav/coords.lua): a destination within 150 yd takes the ground height under it (ray from the guess + 4 yd, + 30 yd outdoors; + 2 yd indoors) before the server is asked, when more than 3 yd off - no failed request and no 9-height search; new 'Test: walk to the minimap point' keybind (is_cursor_on_minimap + get_cursor_world_pos). |
| 2.276.0 | **Rogue: Sprint in the Spells tab.** Sprint (2983, +50% run speed for 15 s, keeps Stealth) was not in the Rogue catalog, and an uncatalogued spell is listed only after the game has called it usable - never while on its 5 min cooldown - so it was missing. Now a catalog entry (Cooldowns & Resources, ticked) with its id hard-coded (found by is_spell_learned when the scan has no Sprint family). Cast to catch a fleeing mob: in combat, target under 35 % health, moving and more than 8 yd away. New context check c.tmoving(). |
| 2.277.0 | **Closing the window unloads the bot (Master Farmer launcher 1.2.7); Rogue Sap.** Loaded from the Master Farmer launcher, the main window's close cross calls MASTER_FARMER.close: the launcher unloads the bot (on_unload stops it) on its next frame and shows its main GUI again. Loaded on its own (HTTP plugin_loader), the cross still only hides the window. Sap (6770) was not in the Rogue catalog, and as an uncatalogued spell it needs Stealth to be 'usable', so the Spells tab never listed it: now a catalog entry (crowd control, ticked, id hard-coded). The Stealth opener saps a second humanoid of the pack - alive, not in combat, within 10 yd of the target and in melee reach while sneaking in - once per pull, then opens on the target as before. |
| 2.277.1 | **AmeisenNav 1.6.7: no startup menu error.** The Sylvanas menu builds its elements over the first frames with a per-frame budget (menu_api consume_build_unit); since the 1.6.6 minimap keybind, the 'follow' section did not fit on the first frame and logged 'menu section follow failed' once at every load. A section failure in the first 10 menu frames is no longer reported (it draws on the next frame). |
| 2.278.0 | **New Master Farmer banner in the window header.** The header logo came from the Project Sylvanas mf_assets zip, which no longer arrives (startup line 'assets zip=0 ... logo=0'), so the header had no logo. ui.lua now downloads the new Master Farmer banner (logo_master_farmer.png, L333T/master-farmer-assets, the launcher's artwork repo) once, caches it in scripts_data/master_farmer/gui_v3, and uses the zip logo only as the fallback; the logo slot is 300 x 99 (the banner's 3:1). The same template change is in the Route Tool, Intelligent Ally and the Slave Pens Pull window (now on this template). |
| 2.279.0 | **Game emblem left of the banner.** The window header draws the game's emblem to the left of the Master Farmer banner, as tall as it: icon_forever.png on WoW Forever (MASTER_FARMER.version_id 'forever', or core.get_game_version containing 'forever'), icon_retail.png on every other client. Downloaded from L333T/master-farmer-assets once and cached with the banner (scripts_data/master_farmer/gui_v3). Same template change in the Route Tool, Intelligent Ally and Slave Pens Pull. |
| 2.280.0 | **Flight master (Taxi API).** A RestedXP .fly step matched the destination by name only and called take_node on it whatever its node_type - an undiscovered (NONE), other-continent (DISTANT) or current (CURRENT) flight point, for which take_node does nothing; that read as a failed take-off three times before the step was skipped. Now only a REACHABLE node is taken (an empty type from an older client is trusted); when the named point exists but cannot be flown to, no look-alike is flown to instead, and the log says why ('not discovered by this character', 'on another continent'; standing at it closes the step as done) and lists only the flyable points. The node is resolved by name on every visit, never by a cached index. A talk goal at a flight master (.fp, which learns the flight point) now closes the flight map with core.taxi.close when it is done - it used to stay open. |
| 2.281.0 | **Guide mismatch detection.** A dwarf in Coldridge Valley with the human RestedXP guide loaded (step: accept A Threat Within, Northshire, 2733 yd, no path on foot) stood on 'cannot reach' for good while the stall recovery skipped one goal after another. New quest/mismatch.lua: when every waypoint of the guide goal is in another zone (UI map) than the player's, more than 1000 yd away, the walk to it has been refused (no path / off the mesh) and the bot has not come 50 yd closer for 45 s, questing holds with the note 'Guide mismatch: step is in <zone>, you are in <zone> - load the guide for your zone' and one warning in the log; the stall recovery is paused meanwhile. It lets go by itself when the step changes (another guide loaded), when the player is in the step's zone, or when the step is within 1000 yd. A long walk that keeps making progress, a zone border, or a far point in the same zone is never a mismatch. |
| 2.282.0 | **Shaman: Rockbiter Weapon no longer spam-cast.** The main-hand imbue check called item_has_enchant / item_enchant_id / item_enchant_expiration on the PLAYER with slot 16; they belong to the ITEM object and take no slot, so the answer was 'no enchant' and Rockbiter (8017) was recast every tick. Now: GetWeaponEnchantInfo first (the game's own, temporary enchants only), then the main-hand item from get_item_at_inventory_slot(16): an enchant with time left or an enchant id counts as imbued; no readable item = never guessed. An imbue is also never recast within 270 s of the last imbue cast, so a client that cannot tell recasts on schedule (Rockbiter lasts 5 min), never every tick. What each source answered is logged once ('imbue check: ...'). Applies to every Shaman imbue (Windfury / Flametongue / Frostbrand / Rockbiter). |
| 2.282.1 | **AmeisenNav is now delivered from GitHub.** The encoded AmeisenNav is published to L333T/ameisen-nav (tools/publish_nav.py in the Master Farmer project: encode, manifest, push); users install the small AmeisenNav GitHub loader (scriptsAmeisenNav), which downloads the current AmeisenNav at every scripts load and runs the last downloaded copy when offline. AmeisenNav's header.lua version corrected to 1.6.7 (it still said 1.6.5). |
| 2.283.0 | **Melee swing timer.** targeting.start_auto_attack: (1) the melee swing was only armed inside 5 yd of the mob's CENTRE - on a large mob the character stood in melee range and did not swing; now it is armed in real melee range (is_in_melee_range via spell_range) or within 8 yd, so auto attack is on and swings the moment the target is in reach; (2) the attack was re-sent every second while already swinging at the same target - it is no longer re-sent while auto_attack_helper reports the player auto attacking (a re-sent Attack can toggle it off); a dropped swing is re-armed within 0.5 s (was 1 s); (3) SWING WATCH: in melee range, the time to the first swing is logged ('melee: first swing x s after reaching ...'), and no swing for the weapon speed + 1 s faces the target, re-arms the attack and logs it. |
| 2.284.0 | **Hunter: Mend Pet hold, Pet Takes Aggro, back-jump.** Mend Pet (a 5 s channel) was not in the channel list, so it was cast on the move and the walk broke it: now a channel (the channel lock holds the Hunter while it channels). MEND PET HOLD: once Mend Pet is cast out of combat the Hunter does not move until the pet is at 90 % health - Mend Pet is cast again when a channel ends with the pet still below; it lets go when the pet is healed, dies, a fight starts, a recast fails (no mana) or after 40 s. New Spells-tab option (Hunters only, off by default) 'Pet Takes Aggro': with a living pet, the pet is sent in and the Hunter holds every attack of its own (spells and Auto Shot) until the mob targets the pet, or the Hunter, or 6 s pass; and a mob within 5 yd of the Hunter starts the back-jump - backward for 1.5 s, a jump 0.5 s in while still moving backward, then stop (at most once every 4 s). It is the Mage's Frost Nova back-off (movement.backpedal), whose jump time is now a parameter (the Mage keeps 0.75 s). |
| 2.285.0 | **Professions learned correctly (Profession API).** New professions.lua answers which professions the character has: the core.skill skill lines (Classic / TBC / Forever), then core.spell_book.get_professions + get_profession_info, and how many primary professions are learned (core.trade_skill.get_num_primary_professions). A RestedXP 'Train [Profession]' step no longer walks to the trainer for a profession already known (done with its skill level), refuses a third primary profession up front with a warning (the trainer would refuse it), and counts the profession as learned when its skill line appears after the purchase (up to 5 s), not only when the trainer row changes. The gathering trainer reads the Herbalism / Mining level the same way and does not plan a trip to learn a third primary. The trainer is still opened through its gossip trainer option (gossip.select TRAINER). |
| 2.286.0 | **Questing follows RestedXP's goal and quests (20:07 Northshire session).** (1) KILL TARGETS: the goal's target set mixed the goal's own mob with every RestedXP target line of the step, the stickies and the guide-file list for the quest (quest 7: Brother Danil, Kobold Vermin, Timber Wolf, Wolf, Young Wolf); the closest won and was remembered, so 'Kobold Vermin slain 0/10' killed Young Wolf after Young Wolf. Now the current RestedXP goal's own mob (its line and objectives; the guide-file list only when they name nothing) is searched first and is the only one remembered; the step's other RestedXP targets are fought only within 25 yd while none of the goal's own mob is in sight, and nothing outside RestedXP's lists. (2) QUEST LOG: is_on_quest answered 'no' for accepted quests (Book Inventory, Rascally Rodents) - the accept was retried and given up as 'bags_full' and the turn-in skipped as 'never taken'; npc.in_log now also checks the quest log id list and every log entry by id or title (accept / turn-in verify, resolve_quest). (3) 'bags_full' only when the client said the bags are full; two misses are logged with the quest log contents and given up. (4) VENDOR GOSSIP: a vendor who also trains (Riley Peltskinner) answered with a gossip window; the vendor option is now taken while the trip waits, and the class trainer no longer selects 'Train me.' during a vendor trip. |
| 2.287.0 | **Kill goals stay on RestedXP's quests.** The 2.286.0 fallback - the step's generic RestedXP target lines within 25 yd - still had the bot fight Young Wolves on a 'Kobold Vermin slain' step (a wolf was always that close in Northshire; 20:22 session). The fallback is now guide.find_other_goal_mob: the mob of another UNFINISHED kill goal of the same RestedXP step (quest objectives, .mob / .rare - each by its own goal line and objectives); .target / .unitscan lines (RestedXP's target macro) are never a reason to fight. With none of those in sight the goal's RestedXP waypoints are walked. STOP LOGGED: every switch-off of the bot - Stop, Pause, the watchdog, a mode change - now writes 'Stop: <why>' to the session log and the console (the 20:23:58 stop left no trace). |
| 2.288.0 | **Hunter back-jump: 2.5 s backward.** 'Pet Takes Aggro' back-jump (a mob within 5 yd): backward for 2.5 s (was 1.5 s), still with the jump 0.5 s in, then stop; at most once every 4 s. |
| 2.289.0 | **AmeisenNav 1.6.8: no smoothing, quicker paths, tighter indoors.** No path is smoothed any more: AmeisenNav strips the server's smoothing bits (1 Chaikin, 2, 4) from every path request (config no_smoothing, query.lua; VALIDATE_MAS 16 kept), and the Grindbot no longer asks for Chaikin (movement/sentinel.lua AN_FLAGS 1+16 -> 16) - before, only the 20-yard window walks were unsmoothed. Quicker paths while moving: windows of 15 yd (was 20) planned 8 yd before the end of the current one (was 5), path checks every 0.15 s (0.25), wall / corner questions every 0.15 s (0.2), objects rescanned every 0.3 s (0.5). Tighter indoors: waypoints 3 yd apart (5 outdoors), 0.9 yd from walls (1.25), 0.3 yd extra from objects (0.4), a waypoint reached at 1.5 yd (2.5). |
| 2.290.0 | **RestedXP waypoints on the guide's floor.** A RestedXP waypoint is map x, y only (rested_xp_waypoint_info has no height); its height came from one ground ray started 30 yd above the player (4 indoors) - the first surface below it, so a bridge deck over the road, a cliff top over a cave - and the path went over or under the guide's route. Now (quest/guide.lua THE WAYPOINT'S FLOOR) every floor at the waypoint's x, y is read (rays from 60 yd above to 25 yd below the player; 2-8 yd and -6 indoors; a bogus 0 and anything 60+ yd off refused); one floor is used at once; with several, AmeisenNav is asked in ONE batch for the walk to each and the floor with the shortest real walk is the guide's (logged: 'N floors (z ...) - the guide's floor is z ...'); without AmeisenNav, or no answer within 5 s, the floor nearest the player's height - never simply the highest. Far waypoints (120+ yd) are unchanged: AmeisenNav's own ground read and height search. |
| 2.291.0 | **Casters no longer run into walls at low health.** A Warlock without a pet, at 0 mana and 38 % health, kept retreating from a whelp in melee (20:54 Coldridge session): the mob followed and hit the turned back, the retreat hop pushed into a wall, and the character died. A retreat only buys time to cast from range, so (movement/combat.lua RETREAT GUARDS): no retreat below 40 % health (fight back), none below 10 % mana for a mana user (nothing to cast), and a retreat that gains less than 1 yd in 1 s (a wall, slope or corner) is called off - no retreat from that target for 8 s, the character fights in place. Each is logged ('retreat called off: health ...', 'retreat from X blocked ... fighting in place'). |
| 2.292.0 | **Train steps at trainer NPCs no longer loop.** A RestedXP '.train' step completes only when the spell is learned. With 'Trained 0 spell(s); 1 more need more gold' (Train [Battle Shout] at Sten Stoutarm, the warrior trainer who also gives the Coldridge quests - 23:52 session) the bot marked the goal itself, RestedXP stayed on the step, and every 30 s the trainer window was opened again - for minutes, the NPC's quests waiting. Now, 3 s after the trainer visit with RestedXP still on that step, the step is skipped in RestedXP (new guide.skip_current: skip_current_step, once per step, logged 'RestedXP step N skipped: train step - <trainer's answer>'); the trainer is visited again by the every-3-levels check once there is gold. |
| 2.293.0 | **Melee: swings and next-swing abilities.** (1) NEXT-SWING ABILITIES: Heroic Strike, Cleave, Maul and Raptor Strike replace the next melee swing, and pressing one again while it is queued UN-queues it - the 23:51 warrior session cast Heroic Strike every 0.1-0.4 s at 50+ rage, toggling it on and off. Now they are not pressed while the game reports them queued (core.spell_book.is_current_spell) nor within one weapon swing of the last press. (2) SWING WATCH: on this client the helper's 'last attack' time moves when the Attack command is sent, not on every swing, so 2.283.0 logged 'no swing for 2.9 s' in 55 fights whose mobs were dying normally - and each re-sent Attack while the warrior was swinging, which can switch auto attack off. Now a drop in the target's health counts as a hit, the warning ('no hit for ...') needs neither for weapon speed + 1 s, and Attack is re-sent only when auto attack has really stopped (is_auto_attacking false) - while it is on, the character only turns to face the target. |
| 2.294.0 | **Strafe in Combat for Warriors; random strafe taps.** (1) Warriors get a Spells-tab box 'Strafe in Combat' (mfg_strafe_combat_warrior), the same micro-strafe the Rogue 'Strafe Combat' box gives: in combat, once in melee position, tap strafe right / left with forward held. Each class reads only its own box. (2) Each tap (each direction) now lasts a random 0.20-0.50 s (was 0.22-0.32 s); the short pause between taps is unchanged. |
| 2.295.0 | **Melee: target stacked inside the character.** The 00:30 warrior session's swing log was healthy (first hit 0.0-1.6 s after reaching melee in 12 of 13 fights, Heroic Strike cast 53 times, Attack never re-sent while swinging), but one Frostmane Troll Whelp stood 0.2-0.5 yd from the warrior for 14 s with auto attack on and not one hit landing - a target inside the model cannot be faced. Now, no hit for weapon speed + 1 s with the target closer than 1 yd: a 0.5 s step back (no jump) through movement.backpedal, then face; combat movement closes back in (logged 'stacked on the character'). Also the rotation's 'nothing cast' line shows a warrior's rage (it printed energy, always 0). |
| 2.296.0 | **Warrior stance, campfire sit, counter NPCs, stall loop (01:04 warrior session).** (1) STANCE: get_shapeshift_form_id is a class-global form id (Battle Stance 17), read as a bar index 1-3; the aura fallback found nothing, so every stance-gated ability (Rend, Charge) was refused as 'wrong form / stance' in every fight. Now the active bar index -> its name / spell id (get_shapeshift_form_info), then form id 17/18/19, then the stance auras 2457/71/2458. (2) 'Sit near the campfire' (The Great Outdoors) is an emote, not a click - the campfire was used 4 times a minute for 35 minutes: a goal with the word 'sit' walks within 4 yd of the object and sits (core.input.descend_start), jump + sit again after 5 s, 4 tries. (3) Closing in on a quest object gives up after 6 s without a yard gained and uses it from there (5 minutes 'closer to' a campfire 3 yd away ended in a Hearthstone). (4) An NPC behind a counter (Tannok Frosthammer): no yard gained for 3 s within 6 yd -> interact from where the character stands; the walk to a giver is logged with both heights.
