(5) A goal given up after 3 recoveries that comes back and stalls again skips the RestedXP step ('recovery 25 of 3' for 23 minutes). |
| 2.297.0 | **Quest NPC interaction: 2 yd reach and a recovery ladder.** Accept / turn-in / talk / flight-master NPCs are interacted with from 2 yd (TALK_REACH 4 -> 2, close-in 2.5 -> 1.5; the walks stop at 1.5 yd). An NPC behind a counter that cannot be reached still gets the 2.296.0 fallback (no yard gained for 3 s within 6 yd). A stalled interaction (no progress for 30 s) climbs a ladder: 1st - retry the interaction; 2nd - reload the UI (out of combat), wait 30 s, retry; 3rd - the NPC is blacklisted for 30 minutes (guid and npc id; excluded from every giver search), the goal is set aside and the RestedXP step skipped, with a warning. |
| 2.298.0 | **Route walk: no standing still after a failed path.** The 02:31 and 02:32 sessions walked a Rumbleshot's Ammo route point 153 yd away, AmeisenNav answered 'no_path (no height at that spot)' once, and the bot stood on 'Guide: 0/1 Rumbleshot's Ammo' with no new request and no next point. (1) A route point whose path failed for any reason (not only off-mesh) is skipped for the next point, with the reason logged. (2) Watchdog: 10 s without 2 yd of movement while the bot means to walk (more than arrive + 2 yd from the point, fight / rest time excluded) logs the movement state (moving, owner, state, last fail), forces a fresh path and, on a multi-point route, takes the next point. |
| 2.299.0 | **Game crash: spell focus objects are never used.** The sit near the campfire worked (objective done 2 s later), but the next goal ('Gain the Boosted Rest buff') called use_object on the same Basic Campfire; 1 s later the player object was gone, the map read 0 and the game crashed (02:39). The same followed use_object on a Forge at 02:30 (a 'Rumbleshot's Ammo' name match). Objects named forge / anvil / campfire / cooking fire / bonfire / brazier are never used: a rest / buff / sit goal sits near them, any other goal skips them for 10 minutes. |
| 2.300.0 | **Route points at the ground height; no endless 'every waypoint unreachable'.** Rumbleshot's Ammo (02:42): the goal's one waypoint and its ring of 6 patrol points all carried z 396 (RestedXP gives x, y only), the ground there is z 366 - AmeisenNav 'no height at that spot' for each, all 7 marked unreachable, the goal skipped, RestedXP did not move on, and the same repeated every 30 s. (1) A route point within 150 yd takes the terrain height under it when that is more than 3 yd off (read once per point per 5 min, logged; its cached reachability answer is dropped). (2) A point whose failed path has a new height pending (terrain retry) is not skipped. (3) The second 'every waypoint unreachable' on the same step skips the RestedXP step. |
| 2.301.0 | **Route ground height, really.** 2.300.0's correction never ran: engine.lua's safe() takes no arguments, so safe(Tr.height, x, y, z) asked for the height of nothing (nil) - the 02:49 session marked all 7 Rumbleshot's Ammo points unreachable again. Now called with pcall, covered by tests/test_route_ground.lua (runs the real code). Also at the source: Sentinel's reachability check (validate_destination, cached per point) asks at the terrain height within 150 yd instead of the guessed one, so no caller can mark a point unreachable from a guessed height. |
| 2.302.0 | **Trainer interaction loop.** The 03:21 session asked Granis Swiftaxe for warrior training every ~10 s: 'I require warrior training.' selected, no trainer window, 'did not train us - trying another trainer' - and he was picked again at once, because a rejection only applied to unnamed (flag-matched) trainers and he is matched by name; the quest walk was interrupted each time. Now a trainer that does not train us is not asked again for 30 minutes, whatever matched it (name, guide id, flag), and the training option selected 3 times without a window ends the check for this level. tests/test_trainer_loop.lua runs the real trainer.lua (the old one asked Granis 343 times in 10 minutes). |
| 2.303.0 | **Rotation: resource dumps adapt to the kit; finishers on dying targets; usable-only Spells tab.** (1) daaa22 13:19 session (warrior 6, 30+ fights): Heroic Strike - the only damage special - was never cast, 'its condition' at 35-49 rage, because it waited for 50 rage to spare the specials the warrior does not have yet; fights ended at 21-34% HP and a meal. Resource dumps now read the kit (c.specials(): other ticked, known melee damage specials): Heroic Strike 50 / 20 alone, Cleave 40 / 25, Maul 30 / 15 (was 20 flat). (2) Finishers (Eviscerate, Rip, Ferocious Bite, Rupture, Slice and Dice) go at 2+ points under 35% target health and at 1+ point under 12% (was 2+ under 25%): low-level mobs died with the points unspent. (3) Spells tab lists usable spells only: WoW Forever reports passives as usable, so Armor Proficiency, Block, Dodge, Parry, Languages, Engineering Specialization, Expansive Mind, Mining and Smelting were listed; passives, weapon / armor skills, languages and professions are left out by name. tests/test_rotation_tuning.lua runs the real smart.lua and class data. |
| 2.304.0 | **Corpse position per the core.game_ui.get_corpse_position API.** 'No corpse' is the API's zero vector test (x == 0 and y == 0; a (0, 0, z) read was taken as a corpse at the map origin). Since the 2026-10-10 core update the call reads the world map's corpse marker where the direct read fails (WoW Forever answered inf): x, y exact, z the ground below or our own height when the area is not loaded - so the distance to the corpse (run / revive reach) is measured flat, and the position is re-read every tick so z settles on the way in. The last-known / died-at fallbacks stay for a client that answers nothing. tests/test_corpse_position.lua runs the real death.lua. |
| 2.305.0 | **Ghost not running to the corpse.** The daaa22 13:42 session started as a ghost (scripts reloaded after the death) and sat on 'Waiting for corpse position': the call gave nothing as_vec3 accepted, and the died-at fallback lived only in memory. (1) The corpse vector is read through guarded field access, so a userdata vec3 is accepted (as_vec3 took plain tables only). (2) While the corpse is unknown, the raw answer (type and x, y, z) is logged every 10 s. (3) Where the body fell is saved with the settings (died_at), so a ghost after a reload still has a position to run to; cleared on revive. |
| 2.306.0 | **Repository audit corrections** (docs/CODEBASE_AUDIT_REPORT.md, docs/CODEBASE_REMEDIATION_PLAN.md, docs/API_VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md, evidence in docs/audit/). Fixed, each against the .api stubs: LOGIC-1 corpse-run deadlock (an avoid-area refusal was failed as 'blacklisted', an off-mesh word, which made a self-refreshing zone on the corpse - 30 min stuck + AFK logout in a measured session; death zones are also withdrawn from navigation while dead); IZI-2/CORE-A-1/CORE-B-3 health percentage (get_health_percentage is 1..100 - the x100 rescale read 1% as 100%; undeclared health_pct / get_health_percent no longer read - Execute, heals, potions, Mend Pet and the retreat guard now see low health); LOGIC-3 stall counter bounded; CORE-A-2 height 0 = miss in U.ground_z; CORE-A-3 Target Selector override refreshed every frame (500 ms TTL); CORE-A-4 continent change read from get_instance_id, not the zone map id (every zone border reset navigation); CORE-A-5 jump paired with ascend_stop; CORE-A-6 stored combat unit is_valid-checked; CORE-A-7 izi subscriptions unsubscribed on reload; IZI-3 per-tick memo cleared in pack_interrupt; LUA-2 undefined global 'hop'; LUA-3 os.* in the sandbox -> core.get_server_time; PERF-1 ghost enemy scans only within 60 yd; LOGIC-9 guarded get_class. Blocked on API information and deferred items are listed in the plan. |
| 2.307.0 | **quest/engine.lua split (audit LUA-5 / LUA-6).** The engine sat at exactly Lua's 200-locals-per-chunk limit - the next `local` would have stopped the quest pack compiling. The FLIGHT / far-travel section (RestedXP .fly goals, flight trips toward far goals, quest.in_flight; 34 locals) moved to the new quest/travel.lua. The moved code is byte-identical to the old section apart from five mechanical renames: the engine hands over walk_to, find_giver, ACT_GAP, TALK_REACH and its single shared interact timer (act_until / set_act_until) through TR.init; quest.in_flight is still exported by the engine (main.lua uses it). Engine 200 -> 167 top-level locals, tick_inner 56 -> 52 upvalues. loader.lua unloads quest/travel with the quest pack. No behaviour change intended. tests/test_quest_split.lua loads the real module and checks the shared timer, the exports and the limits; test_taxi / test_npc_ladder read the moved code from its new file. |
| 2.308.0 | **Running in circles on a quest route.** The 15:22 session (Frostmane Headhunter, a cave) cancelled its own walk once a second: standing on route point 8 counted it reached (CHAIN_WP) and moved on to point 1 (148 yd), and every missed tick 'resumed at the nearest point' - point 8 again. The character turned back and forth until stopped. (1) Resuming never picks a point the character stands on (within CHAIN_WP); the next one is taken. (2) The 2.300.0 route height correction moved the cave's points (guide z 384) to the surface above (405) and AmeisenNav found no path: every floor at the point is read now (terrain.floors) and the guide's height is kept when it is one of them; only a height matching no floor is corrected, to the floor nearest to it (Rumbleshot's Ammo 396 -> 366 still). tests/test_route_ground.lua covers the cave case (fails on 2.307.0). |
| 2.309.0 | **Game crash on engaging an attacker: no ray test at an unloaded model.** WoW crash reports (_classic_beta_/Errors) show six crashes on 2026-10-11 - 13:33, 14:05, 15:06, 15:26, 15:37, 15:48 - all ASSERT(m_data) in Model2/M2HitTest.cpp, the client's ray test against a model, called from script code; four of them in the very second the bot logged 'switch to attacker / engage' (a mob that had just come into view, while travelling). The bot's ray test at a unit is player:los_to (movement/range.lua raw_los: targeting, auto attack and every spell check go through it). It now runs only when the unit is visible and its model is loaded - get_attachment_name_position returns a zero vector when it is not (game_object.lua) - and otherwise answers 'no line of sight yet' (3 yd and closer: yes), so the cast / swing waits the moment the model takes to load. tests/test_los_model.lua (REAL range.lua; fails on 2.308.0). Not addressed: ASSERT(IsLoaded()) in DB/DBEngine/WowClientDB2.cpp (15:09 today, 10-10, 10-04), at times with no bot session running. AmeisenNav 1.7.1 in the same release: a destination is never 'snapped' more than 8 yd down to the terrain (the terrain ray ignores docks / platforms: a Zangarmarsh destination at z 16.8 went to the lake bed at -28.8 and the path failed). |
| 2.310.0 | **Grinding: pull only enemies within 15 yd of the loaded route.** grind.kill_mobs pulled the nearest valid mob within 50 yd of the character (2.233.0), wherever it stood. With a route loaded, a mob is now pulled only when it stands within ROUTE_PULL (15) yards of the route - the flat distance to its nearest segment (path_nearest), so a mob beside the middle of a long leg counts, not only near a node. The nearest such mob is pulled, as before; mobs attacking the character are still fought wherever they are (the attacker handling, not this pull). Built-in zones without a loaded route are unchanged. tests/test_grind_route_pull.lua (REAL grind/engine.lua; fails on 2.309.0). |
| 2.311.0 | **No raycasts in Questing & Grinding.** The user: "Remove ray casting from Master Farmer Questing and Grinding, use previous methods before crashes" (the 2026-10-11 M2HitTest game crashes were the game's ray test against a model). Line of sight is no ray: movement/range.lua no longer calls player:los_to (nor the 2.309.0 model gate); a unit is in sight until the game refuses a cast at it with "Target not in line of sight" (UI_ERROR_MESSAGE, events.lua records the player's target), then out of sight for LOS_HOLD 2.5 s so combat movement repositions as on a los_to "no"; 3 yd and closer always in sight. targeting.lua's stall log uses the same answer. Casts: every izi cast and item use goes with check_los = false (spellcheck.no_los on cast_safe / cast / cast_position, the rotation queue check, gather, path_runner, potions, food, hearthstone), spell_helper is_spell_castable is asked with skip_los, and its in_los queries are removed. Terrain heights: new K.TERRAIN_RAYS = false (movement/const.lua) - U.ground_z keeps the given height, terrain.height / floors answer nothing (height retry, direct-leg and wall checks stand aside as before 2.221.0), the guide's waypoint floors and map_to_world fallback are off (the waypoint keeps the player's height, AmeisenNav finds the ground), IS.fix_z (2.300.0 / 2.308.0) and the 2.301.0 reachability terrain height are removed, far hops use the player's height. K.NATIVE_RAYS stays false. tests/test_no_rays.lua; test_los_model / test_route_ground / test_waypoint_floor retired. |
| 2.312.0 | **The Slave Pens corrections: core API casts and item uses, no izi spell queue.** The user: "Slave Pens isn't crashing - use the corrections made to Slave Pens that apply to Questing & Grinding". Slave Pens stopped crashing once raycasts were removed (here in 2.311.0) and its izi spell / item / spell-queue calls were replaced by the core API (its drink crashes were inside an izi item use, its step-37 crashes inside an izi cast). Here: spellcheck.cast_safe / cast / cast_position check with core.spell_book (learned, usable, cooldown, not casting, is_spell_in_range) and send with core.input.cast_target_spell / cast_position_spell - every rotation, buff, racial, conjure, gather, path and Pick Pocket cast goes through them; the izi spell queue (castq / common/modules/spell_queue) is off, so casts go the direct way, and rotations/mage.lua no longer loads it. Items: spellcheck.use_item = core.input.use_item (not while casting; not on the move except potions) for food and drink (resting), health / mana potions (healing; izi only names the best potion id) and the Hearthstone (watchdog, vendor). Slave Pens' other corrections do not apply (no AmeisenNav there; one product at a time is in launcher 1.3.3). AmeisenNav 1.7.2 (same day): no terrain rays - the destination ground read (1.6.6) and the cave ray (1.7.0) are off; a destination keeps its height and the server's height search finds the ground, a cave reads indoor. tests/test_no_rays.lua, tests/test_ameisen_horizon.lua. |
| 2.313.0 | **Enemy targets in line of sight, above ground and reachable - from the navmesh, no game ray.** The user: "ensure enemy target is in LoS and not underground or unreachable" (game raycasts stay removed, 2.311.0). New movement/sight.lua asks AmeisenNav one find_path from the player to the enemy (sentinel N.path_probe; server request, cached per enemy 3 s / 8 s for a bad answer, re-asked once it moved 5 yd, requests 0.15 s apart) and judges it: unreachable (no path, path ends 4+ yd short, or the enemy 5+ yd above its end - a ledge), underground (the walkable ground 5+ yd above the enemy at its x, y), around (walk over 1.3x the straight distance - something between: no line of sight) or clear. targeting.can_see (new pull targets, quest kill targets, pack pick) takes only a clear enemy and marks unreachable / underground ones unreachable; movement/range.lua has_los reads around / underground / unreachable as no line of sight (casts wait, combat movement closes in); combat chase walks straight only on a clear line and otherwise walks AmeisenNav's planned path (4b) instead of running into the wall. Within 5 yd: clear without asking. No AmeisenNav: no answer and the old behaviour. tests/test_target_sight.lua. |
| 2.314.0 | **Every grind route loops; a route starts wherever the player stands.** The user: "Make Master Farmer grinding routes always loop. When starting a grinding route the player can start on any waypoint and will begin running the route." A route file without loop = true read as point to point (path_format) and the patrol turned round at its ends and walked back along itself; grind/engine.lua open_route() now answers false for every route, so the last waypoint always leads to the first (patrol steps, route chunks, the nearest-point search with the last -> first leg, lap counting) and the walking direction is always forward. grind.set_profile marks a fresh start: the next patrol tick walks in to the nearest point of the route (no walk back to waypoint 1) and runs on from the waypoint after it. The route info line reads "loops" for every route. tests/test_grind_loop.lua. |
| 2.315.0 | **No IZI per-frame engine (izi.on_update).** Six game crashes on another PC (2026-10-11 18:47-19:25, 2.313.0 / 2.314.0) left no error in the session logs; three of them came with the bot STOPPED (menu only), when the only per-frame work left was izi.on_update, the menu and the idle movement pulse. izi.on_update ran every frame to serve five IZI subscriptions - two hotkey watchers (izi.on_key_release), izi.on_combat_start / finish, izi.on_spell_success / cancel and izi.after timers - reading combat and cast state through IZI every frame. It is no longer called (the Slave Pens correction: core API, not IZI). Replacements: callbacks.tick polls the local player through the core API (is_in_combat edge -> combat_finish; a cast / channel ending 200+ ms before its end time -> spell_cancel); the movement pulse reads the combat flag from the player and ticks its own cast-lock timers (movement/locks.lua, were izi.after); the last spell sent is recorded by spellcheck.cast_done; every hotkey (incl. the extra codes the IZI watchers listened for) is polled with core.input.is_key_pressed. IZI's tracked forecasts (time_to_die, get_health_percentage_inc) are no longer read: dies-before-the-cast uses health_prediction, fight length combat_forecast / the learned kill time, potions the current health. Earlier loads' IZI subscriptions are unsubscribed on load. tests/test_no_izi_update.lua; test_izi_callbacks retired; test_adaptive reads the forecast library. |
| 2.316.0 | **Tighter turns, no circling a waypoint (+ AmeisenNav 1.7.3); a far target is approached, not walked away from.** The user: "Make navigation use tighter turns so waypoints aren't circled". simple_movement's turn speed (0.05-0.5) was never set, so the library default turned slowly: corners were taken wide and a waypoint met off-axis was circled. It is now 0.5 (the maximum) in the Grindbot walker (movement/walker.lua) and for every AmeisenNav walk (restored after, like the thresholds). AmeisenNav 1.7.3 adds an orbit guard - still within orbit_radius (5 yd) of the same waypoint after orbit_time (1.2 s) without reaching it, the waypoint is skipped (simple_movement:skip_waypoint), never the final one - and its input driver turns in place at a corner of sharp_turn (60) degrees or more. From the 19:37 daaa22 log: a quest kill target 92 yd away was 'approached' while a route leg to the guide point started 0.4 s earlier was still walking, so the distance grew 50 -> 110 yd until that leg ended (again 19:38, 50 -> 75 yd). The quest approach now counts only a walk headed within 15 yd of the target (movement.headed_to); otherwise the path to the target is asked at once. tests/test_nav_tight_turns.lua. |
| 2.317.0 | **Straight moves check for objects in the way (no raycast).** The user: "Player should check for objects when using Movement Handler API". Since 2.311.0 U.walk_open - the straight-line check behind the combat chase, the steering search and the short direct legs - read every line as open; the 20:00 daaa22 log: the path approach handed a Young Thistle Boar over at 40 yd, combat movement ran straight at it, 'no progress toward the combat for 4s (40 yd)', 'still stuck - jumping', target dropped. New movement/objects.lua (AmeisenNav's object-cache method): every 0.3 s core.object_manager.get_visible_objects() -> game objects and standing, non-attackable units within 30 yd on the player's floor, with their bounding radius; never players, corpses, moving or attackable units. U.corridor (walk_open) answers blocked when an object's circle (radius + 0.6 yd body) crosses the line, leaving out an object at either end (the destination NPC / object itself). A blocked line sends the combat chase to the navmesh path (4b) and lets the steering search side-step it. tests/test_move_objects.lua. |
| 2.318.0 | **Grinding: full bags -> a repair NPC or an inn; back on the route at its nearest waypoint.** The user: "Grinding path should vendor at repair NPC or INN if bags are full, when restarting grinding path navto the closest waypoint on the grinding path and begin grinding again." A grind route without a merchant record had only a vendor in sight, the zone merchant table (few zones), the Hearthstone and the Alliance Eastern Kingdoms inn list. New merchants.lua remembers every NPC the game flags as a repairer (0x1000) or innkeeper (0x10000) that the bot sees (scanned every 5 s from vendor.tick; npc id, name, position, continent; saved in scripts_data/mfb/merchants.txt). With full bags on a grind route that has no merchant of its own, the trip goes to the nearest remembered one on the continent - a repairer first when the gear needs repair - after a vendor in sight and before the Hearthstone / zone merchant / inn list; vendor.lua walks to it and talks to it as to any merchant record. Back on the route: farther than 40 yd from it (back from town, or a start away from it) the walk goes to the route's nearest WAYPOINT and the route runs on from the waypoint after it; nearer, the nearest point of the line as before. tests/test_grind_vendor.lua. |
| 2.319.0 | **Navigation stalls, map heights, object detours, quest items before a turn-in (+ AmeisenNav 1.7.4).** From the 20:18 daaa22 log: the Shadowthread route points (guessed z 1311, ground ~1335-1345) were read unreachable by validate_destination, which never searched the height (a walk does), so after two passes RestedXP step 22 was skipped at 1/10 Webwood Venom Sacs and the turn-in then failed three times ('NOT confirmed - still on the quest'). AmeisenNav 1.7.4: the height search tries every 5 yd to +-40 (was 8 / 20 / 40) and validate_destination runs it too, returning the height found; the next 20 yd window is planned 11 yd before the end (was 8). Grindbot: reachability keeps that height (N.reach_z) and a route point takes it (the map x, y, z check); 'unreachable' answers are asked again after 45 s (was 300); a kill / collect goal whose quest is not done is never skipped as unreachable for 3 min - its answers are dropped and the walk (with the height search) goes there; kill / collect work is remembered per quest, and a turn-in the game says is not ready (is_quest_complete false, quest in the log, objectives unfinished) goes back and finishes that work first. Object avoidance: the .api has no ray-free collision query (trace_line / native_intersect / is_line_of_sight are rays; native_intersect does not even hit game objects), so movement/objects.lua gains Ob.detour - the tangent point beside the blocking object's circle, checked clear of other objects, not inside one, and outside spell danger zones (evade_helper:is_point_safe) - and the combat chase steps round an object with it. Faster paths and repaths: move gap 0.5 s (0.85), Sentinel request gap 0.5 s (1.0), settle after a stop 0.6 s (1.25), stuck grace 3 s (4), steer backoff 0.3 s (0.5), chase path every 0.5 s / 2 s old / 4 yd (1 / 3 / 5), reachability requests 0.25 s apart (0.5), quest no-movement watchdog 6 s (10). tests/test_nav_stall_quest.lua. |
| 3.0.0 | **Movement recode on AmeisenNav 2.0.0 (clean build).** AmeisenNav does every walk and every wall trace (safe collision mask, crash-guarded), object avoidance, corner cutting, stuck recovery, swim / indoor profiles; the plugin casts no ray. The 2.x movement stack (walker, steer, repath, probe, terrain, hazards, own, fsm, sentinel, objects, geom, diag, const - ~20 modules) is replaced by movement/{core,nav,combat,locks,range,leash,zones,sight} behind the same movement.lua facade (docs/MOVEMENT.md). Travel: move_to with seamless retargets (same spot within 2 yd is a no-op, retargets 0.25 s apart), a no-path answer within 150 yd asks get_height and walks again at the ground height (remembered for the route), off-mesh twice in 2 min blacklists the spot. Questing: guide heights from the player z (AmeisenNav corrects), no terrain rays / coords helper. Combat: close / hold ranges, seamless chase, no 2 yd progress in 10 s = unreachable, retreat via client:kite, warns if AmeisenNav's combat mode is not Continue. Holds use AmeisenNav pause reasons (mf_cast, mf_loot). Grinding / gathering hand AmeisenNav one full lap (follow_path), start at the nearest waypoint, always loop (Reverse Path walks it backwards), never replace the route mid-walk. NPC runs: full bags / durability; new Vendor-tab buttons Save Target as Route Vendor / Clear Route Vendor (scripts_data/mfb/route_vendors.txt), used first on that route. Crash / lag: menu clicks run on the update tick (Menu:defer), the windows draw from a cache filled on the update tick (gui.refresh_view, movement snapshot), the pet_handler pump moved off the render pass, Path tab list sync at most 1/s, logo retry backs off to 10 s, bounded caches (nav fails / heights, unreachable mobs, loot ignore list, error keys), guid reads checked is_valid. Removed: izi.on_update load gate, quest/api_examples.lua (never loaded), gui.fights_in_melee, terrain / danger / avoid-packs options. tests/test_movement3.lua. |
| 3.0.1 | **Grinding route fixes (daaa22 log 2026-10-12 01:41).** (1) Route stall: path_to_zone built a new zone table every tick, smooth_walk keyed the running lap on it, so the whole 400-point lap went to AmeisenNav every frame ('walking 400 points (route, 2801 yd)' 10x a second) and the player sat on node 617 for 93 s - the zone is now built once per path and the lap is keyed on the path table. (2) Fights during a route walk: combat_engage stopped only a combat walk when in range, so the route walk carried the mage from 14 to 48 yd of a Forest Spider ('not facing') - any running walk now stops in range. (3) Chase stutter: the chase re-pathed every 0.25 s and AmeisenNav's walker driver restarts on each new path - it now re-paths only when the target moved 4+ yd from the goal, at most every 0.75 s. (4) Trainer visits: right after 'train me' was selected the trainer released the cascade, the grind walked back to its route and the window closed as it opened (6 tries, then 'did not train us') - it now holds while the window opens. tests/test_movement3.lua (route stop, chase), tests/test_grind_handoff.lua. |
| 3.0.2 | **2 s stop at every NPC + indoor corners (AmeisenNav 2.0.1).** Every NPC interaction (vendor, repair, food / water seller, class / profession trainer, quest giver and turn-in, flight master) now goes through movement.interact: the walk stops and travel (nav_to / follow_route) is held for 2 s from the last interaction, so the window, gossip, purchase or turn-in completes before the bot walks off. A 'too far away' answer lifts the hold so the walk up to the NPC goes out at once; fights are never held. AmeisenNav 2.0.1, indoor profile for tighter corners (Goldshire inn stuck at door frames and stairs): the simple_movement walker's look distance is now a profile setting (outdoor 8, indoor 2.5, swim 6) and is set again when the profile changes mid-walk (a walk into a building kept the outdoor 8 yd and cut each corner into the wall); indoor waypoint reach 1.0 yd (1.5), wall look-ahead 2.5 (3), object look-ahead 3 (4), clearance 0.3 (0.4), corner-cut range 3 (6), off-path limit 4 (5), stuck after 2.0 s (1.5), ledge 1.0 (1.2). The indoor sliders take new ids so these defaults apply over saved 1.x values. |
| 3.0.3 | **Pull radius 20 yd, attack only in range.** Grinding pulls the nearest enemy within the new Grind-tab 'Pull Radius (yd)' slider (default 20, 10-60) of the player - replacing the 50 yd radius filtered to 15 yd of the route - and RestedXP 'grind to N xp' steps use the same radius (was 100 yd). Grinding, questing and gathering fights now close first: while the target is outside melee / casting range (rotation.combat_range) the bot only walks (combat movement: seamless chase, facing) and casts / swings nothing; once in range the walk stops, auto attack / auto shot starts and the rotation casts. Before, the rotation ran while closing and a cast paused the walk short of range. tests/test_grind_pull_radius.lua (replaces test_grind_route_pull). |
| 3.0.4 | **NPCs: within 3 yd, 10 s to finish.** movement.interact (every NPC interaction: vendor, repair, food / water, trainers, quest givers / turn-ins, flight masters) now walks the player to within 3 yd of the NPC before interacting - the callers stopped at 4-5 yd - and answers false while walking up (the caller's next attempt interacts from there); a caller's per-tick nav_stop does not cancel that walk up. Once within 3 yd the walk stops, the interaction is sent and travel is held 10 s (was 2 s) from the last interaction so the window, gossip, purchase, repair or turn-in completes; a 'too far away' answer still lifts the hold. tests/test_movement3.lua. |
| 3.0.5 | **Paths off the corners, fast turns (AmeisenNav 2.0.2).** Corner clearance: every interior corner of a navmesh path that turns more than 25 deg is moved away from the inside of the turn along its bisector - Corner clearance slider per profile, outdoor 1.5 yd, indoor 0.7, swim 1.0, at most a third of either leg - when the ground there is within 1.5 yd of the corner (a half step is tried next; otherwise it stays). Recorded routes are walked as recorded. Turning: the simple_movement walker gets set_turn_speed 0.5 (the API maximum; it was never set), and the input driver turns standing still for turns of 60 deg or more (forward released, no wide arc). The indoor look distance is 5 yd, the API minimum (2.0.1 set 2.5, below the 5-50 range); indoor sliders take new ids again. tests/test_nav_corners.lua. |

(3) The quest route walker swapped between two stacked points every tick for a minute - three swaps back and forth within 4 s now hold the current point for 10 s. New movement/pivot.lua, run every frame from movement.pulse: when the next point of the running AmeisenNav walk (get_current_path / get_path_index) is more than 70 deg off the player's facing, the walk is held with AmeisenNav's own pause (reason mf_turn - it releases its keys), the player turns in place on the turn keys, and the walk resumes within 15 deg (or turned past it); never longer than 1.5 s, points under 1.5 yd never turned to, 0.4 s between turns. Facing (get_rotation) is used only after it matched the running direction 3 times; not while casting / channeling, backpedalling, resting or on a taxi; movement.halt releases a turn. (3) Flee: 20 % health or less with 3+ enemies within 10 yd -> Sentinel nav_client:flee path for 5 s (once per 45 s; an answer of another shape switches it off). A stall where the path climbs or drops 2.5 yd within 10 yd (stairs, ramp, cave / crypt slope) is re-planned with NO zone the first 2 times there, then gets a small zone only (3 yd, at most 6). No zone ever covers the walk's goal (shrunk to end 4 yd short, or not placed). Tests: tests/test_nav_ahead.lua 5b / 5c. |
| 4.23.0 | **Disconnect / crash on opening quest objects** (user: 'Check logs for what is causing disconnection during questing' / 'Crashed again'). Seeded with the three Pamela's Doll pieces (use_object -> interact). guide.find_named_object now skips items and objects can_be_used says are not usable (Zygor's rule). Test tests/test_objguard.lua. |

Test tests/test_quest_reward.lua. |

Anything attacking the player or the pet, or the player in combat, releases it at once: the quest engine's fight-back kills the attackers, then the step's object goal (still not complete) opens the object again and locks again, until the step completes. | 4.29.0 | **Full audit** (user: 'Preform a full and complete in-depth audit ... Automatically correct any and all issues'). 40 issues fixed, every fix tagged `-- audit 4.29.0:`; the full list is in _MASTER-FARMER_/AUDIT_RESTEDXP_4.29.0.md. High: an undefined global `player` in main.lua (the object lock never saw the Opening cast); a floor query that never answered blocked walks for 120 s; a lost flee / kite callback stopped all attacking; 'finish objectives before turn-in' never ran (`x and f() or nil`). Medium: deferred stop sent mid path request; sight probes ~7/s (now 1/s); path waits / actions never ran and path saves threw; tapped mobs counted fightable (is_tap_denied is a number); use_item given objects not ids; refused objects retried for ever; cached units read before is_valid; the rotation pack skipped the low-level rule; opener DoTs skipped on every pull; Rogue dagger openers lost on an unloaded item; resting / potions / equip could SELL at an open merchant; vendor reset left a stale supplier; supplies counted bank items. Plus 19 Low fixes. tests/api_surface.lua regenerated from the current stubs. |

| 4.33.0 | **Swing timer from the combat log** (user: check the swing timer). auto_attack_helper's last / next attack moves when the Attack command goes out, not on every swing (2.293.0), so after the first swing `adapt.swing_ok` allowed every cast (no weaving), `adapt.swing_now` never filtered melee hits out of measured spell damage, and the swing watch missed dodged / parried swings. New `swingtimer.lua`: `events.lua` hands COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED to it; SWING_DAMAGE / SWING_MISSED from the player or pet are stamped with core.time(), main hand and off hand apart; next main-hand swing = last + get_attack_speed() (lapsed after 2 swings + 1 s). `swing_ok` waits when the cast would end after the swing or the swing is due within 0.25 s, never holds a spell longer than one swing + 0.5 s, and falls back to the helper only when no swing was seen. Swing filter gap 0.08 -> 0.15 s. Offline test (LuaJIT, stubbed core): 20/20. |

New `quest/bagstart.lua` (same file in Zygor / Forever): quest class + no use spell = a quest starter; `BS.start` uses the step's items first, then every starter, accepts the panel of the wanted quest, declines another quest's panel and rules that item out. guide: `step_open_accepts`, `step_item_ids`, `step_bag_item` (a use goal naming nothing). engine: `item_goal` accepts the step's quest panel; an accept goal whose step uses an item starts it from the bags (`IS.bag_start`) before the NPC search. |

Equal or unmeasured sides keep the single zone ahead. No keys over the walk. |
| 4.37.0 | **Local A* + ray fallback alongside Sentinel** (user: "use A* + rays alongside Sentinel navigation to improve player navigation"). `movement/astar.lua` (ported from Zygor): when a Sentinel walk ends "arrived" short of its goal or "unreachable" within 15 yd, a short local grid path is searched with game raycasts (floor height query with a ray snap on a 0, octile A*, knee/chest/side/head clearance rays, string-pulled to straight legs) and walked leg by leg through Sentinel `move_direct` (`N.astar_leg`, never a key). General tab "Local A* Fallback" (`mfg_astar`), on by default; `AS.ON` kill switch, per-frame ray budget, self-check, off for the session after repeated ray/height errors. `S.move_direct` added to `movement/sentinel.lua`. Sentinel is never replaced - additive. |
| 4.44.0 | **The self-centred AoE count.** `smart.lua`'s `aoe_count` took the `spell_prediction` answer whole. A self-centred spell (Arcane Explosion, Blast Wave, Whirlwind, Consecration, Holy Nova...) draws its circle ON the player, and `get_circle_list` answers everything that geometry covers - `spell_data` carries `exception_player_included` / `exception_target_included` and what the module does with them by default is in no stub - so the player and the pet could make up 2 of a 3-enemy threshold and fire an area spell at a single mob. The bot's own pack count (`c.near`, enemies only) now caps a self-centred prediction; it can only lower the number. A ground or target-centred circle is drawn somewhere else, so its prediction still stands alone. Ported from Zygor Questing 1.46.0. Test: `tests/test_aoe_counts.lua`. |
| 4.43.0 | **Step automation diagnostics (audit F7), ported from Zygor Questing.** `quest/diag.lua` logs step transitions with the goal ledger of the step being left - how many goals RestedXP had marked complete, how many the bot finished itself, how many were still open and which ones - plus goal transitions, an arrow tally by type, corpse runs, and one summary line every 2 minutes while something is changing. A goal the bot finished itself (`guide.mark_goal_done`, which RestedXP never marks complete) is counted apart from an open goal, so it is not read as a step that moved on its own. LOGGING ONLY: it owns no state the engine reads, no decision consults it, and every call site is inside a `pcall`. Read a session with `grep diag <scripts_log>/MASTER_FARMER_ERRORS/session_*.log`: "left open goals" at 0 over a long session means goal completion IS driving progression. |
| 4.42.0 | **The death loop, and a UI reload that was never there (audit phase 1).** Deathzones group by AREA at two deaths - the killer npc id is no longer part of the match, so a mixed camp or a patrol arms the zone instead of stacking a second one next to it (the 2026-10-27 log: 6 deaths in 11 minutes in Zangarmarsh, never armed). And `core.reload_game_ui` is declared in no local stub: all of its call sites are gone, the stall ladder's rung 2 is a plain wait-and-retry rather than a no-op that burned a recovery slot. |
| 4.41.0 | **The Sentinel kite retreat survives a target switch.** |
| 4.40.0 | **Always dismount for a doorway, measured with the mounted player height** (user: "Always dismount when attempting to enter doorways, use get player height when mounted, to check entrances to keep player from getting stuck"). `get_height()` answers the body the player has right now, so mounted it IS the taller body. `movement/trace.lua` remembers the on-foot height whenever `is_mounted()` is false and, while mounted, probes 1 / 2 / 3 yd ahead at three heights: clear at the waist and at the on-foot head (the player fits through) but blocked at the mounted head (the mount does not) is a doorway, and `movement/mount.lua`'s new `M.dismount` takes us off at once, suppressing mounting for 8 s so `M.step` does not put us straight back into the same gap. Blocked at the waist as well is a WALL, left to the wall logic, and a flying mount is never dismounted in the air. The check runs while the walk is still MOVING, unlike the jump / wall checks which only look after a stall. Sentinel is not stopped for it and keeps its path. Ported from Auto BG 2.55.x; `tests/test_trace_ports.lua` runs the same doorway story over every product's copy. |

(1) movement/nav N.ahead_tick (every 1 s while a walk follows its path, not while it waits for one or recovers): Sentinel's own obstacle probe (client.obstacle:probe_path_ahead - 3 game trace lines a segment, doodad collision, run by Sentinel's ObstacleService) on the 5 segments PAST the 3 Sentinel probes itself; a segment within 40 yd climbing steeper than 45 deg (rise/run > 1, runs of 2 yd+); a spot where Sentinel's stuck recovery starts twice within 8 yd in 60 s (the Plaguewood slope) -> a Sentinel avoidance zone there (client.obstacle:add_zone, 4 yd) and a SOFT re-path (client:replan(reason, { soft = true }) - the new path is blended in, no stop), at most one re-path per 3 s, each spot zoned once. New adapter calls in movement/sentinel.lua (S.probe_ahead, S.add_zone, S.soft_replan, S.raycast), signatures from the readable v0.0.6 source, pcall'd (not yet recorded on v0.24). (2) movement/sight: a walk that reads 'clear' is also checked with Sentinel's NAVMESH raycast (server, no game ray) player -> enemy at 1.5 yd height; cut more than 3 yd short = 'around' = no line of sight, so combat_engage walks round (Sentinel's path) instead of attacking until it has sight. (3) loot.lua: our own kill counts as 'already looted' only after a loot request of ours or an empty answer (logs showed 'already looted after 0 loot request(s)' when the lootable flag dropped for a moment). Test tests/test_nav_ahead.lua. |

New quest/threat.lua, checked by the quest engine every tick after fight_back (out of combat, during a travel walk - not combat / NPC walks, not on a flight): Sentinel's path ahead (movement.path_points / path_index, now exported; 60 yd from the player) against each hostile (is_enemy_with) living mob's aggro radius - 20 yd at the player's level, +-1 yd per level, 5..45 - within 12 yd of height. The first mob crossed: level >= player + 3 or elite / rare elite / world boss -> a detour point outside its circle (+6 yd, on the side away from it) is walked first, then the walk re-plans and is checked again (4 detours per mob in 60 s at most; one standing at the destination is not avoided - logged); otherwise it is attacked (engage) before the walk reaches it, when within 40 yd, attackable, not tapped by someone else and not fighting someone else. Test tests/test_path_threat.lua. |

| 4.2.0 | **Movement on Sentinel Navigation** (branch sentinel-nav; user's choice: Sentinel walks its own path). movement/nav.lua rewritten on movement/sentinel.lua, the one adapter (docs/SENTINEL_API.md + what v0.24 did in game, docs/SENTINEL_V024.md): a walk is move_to(target, cb(success, reason)); a new destination is another move_to (Sentinel re-plans without stopping), at most one a second (combat two); walks are tracked by token - a replaced or stopped walk gets no callback, and one Sentinel ended by itself is closed from get_state(); travel walks of 40 yd+ ask the navmesh floors there first (get_all_heights, nearest the guess) to avoid ~6 s of off-mesh retries, and (false, \"unreachable\") tries the next floor, then counts toward the blacklist; server_timeout backs off 3.5 s; holds (cast, sharp turn) stop the walk and walk the remembered destination again when the last hold lifts (no pause / resume in Sentinel); a stop never goes to a walk still waiting for its path or under 1 s old (deferred to the pulse, the 2.181.0 / 3.7.1 crash pattern); recorded routes use follow_path, the sight probe nav_client:find_path, grind order plan_route. Gathering tab, new combobox 'Gathering Path' (mfg_gather_path): 'Auto (route by skill)' (the EP routes, as before) or a herbing / mining path from grind/paths/gather (EMKO). A path chosen: gather/route.from_path builds the route from it, the patrol starts at its nearest waypoint and walks it as a closed loop; switching the dropdown switches the route at once. | 3.9.0 | **Herb / ore ESP, gathering off a recorded path, the node database, the EMKO path.** The user: "Add ESP for Gathering, and Mining. When running a recorded path and ESP detects a Herb or Mining node, use the Navigation system to generate a path to the location and use herbing, or mining depending on the node type. Once gathered navigate back to the recorded path and resume path movement." Then: add the Eversong Woods ore + herb list to a herbing and mining database, and build a herbing / mining path named EMKO from iseofdorn_mine_herb.gatherprofile. ESP (gather/esp.lua, after the Sylvanas object-ESP example: an O(1) id / name lookup, a cache refreshed every 0.5 s on the update tick from core.object_manager.get_all_objects, numeric loops; no goto): every herb / ore node within 150 yd is drawn on render (core.graphics.text_3d name + distance, circle_3d) from cached names / positions only - the render pass reads no game object. Green herbs, orange ore, grey = cannot gather it now (profession not known, skill below the node's, or already looted). Gathering-tab box 'Herb / Ore ESP' (mfg_node_esp), drawn in every mode. Database: data/gather_db.lua (zone lists; the Eversong Woods list is the source file's - 38 node ids, 11 ore, 13 herb and 9 other names (lumber, books, caches: never drawn or gathered); the ids are not paired with names in the source, so the kind comes from the name) + the EP_Herb_Mine table gather/nodes.lua. Path detour (gather/detour.lua, from grind/engine while a recorded path runs): out of combat, the nearest node this character can gather within the Path tab's 'Node Detour Range' (50 yd) -> the route walk stops, movement.nav_to walks to it (the MasterArthasNav exact server path, mounting for 30 yd+), at 4.5 yd dismount / halt / core.input.use_object (the game casts Herb Gathering or Mining by the node) / loot every slot; done -> marked looted and the engine rejoins the path at its nearest point (nearest waypoint when farther than 40 yd) and the walk resumes. A fight meanwhile is the grind engine's, the detour carries on after it; give-ups (90 s, 5 uses) are blacklisted. Path-tab options: Gather Herbs / Ore Along Path, Herbs, Ore, Node Detour Range. EMKO: grind/paths/gather/EMKO.lua, built by tools/build_gather_path.py from the profile's recorded Mesh (887 points ~7 yd apart, closed loop, Isle of Dorn, continent 2552; recorder junk rows dropped; the 92 Hotspots kept for reference), listed for Alliance and Horde (faction Both). The profile has no heights, so the path is flagged no_z: walked at the player's height in 30 yd nav_to hops (the server finds the ground), never follow_path'ed. Tests: `_MASTER-FARMER_/tests/test_path_gather.lua`. |
| 3.8.1 | **Gathering fights whatever attacks the player, with the rotation.** The user: "Edit Gathering mode to use rotation against enemy mobs that attack the Player." Before, gathering on the route fought back only once health fell to the Fight-Back Health % (50) and only mobs at most 5 levels above (the EP_Herb_Mine rule), so the player was hit while walking on. Now any mob in combat that targets the player within Fight-Back Range (targeting.combat_scan) is latched at once - any health, any level - closed on and fought with auto attack + the Spells tab rotation (rotation.tick), the next attacker after each kill; at a node it is fought even with the box off. GUI: 'Fight While Patrolling' is now 'Fight Attackers'; the Fight-Back Health % slider is removed. Core API: gather/* no longer calls the izi game_object extensions distance_to (now a get_position distance), is_channeling_or_casting (is_casting_spell / is_channelling_spell) or has_buff (get_buffs) that 3.8.0 left. Tests: `_MASTER-FARMER_/tests/test_gather_fight.lua`. |
| 3.8.0 | **Gathering: the EP_Herb_Mine port on Core API, every location saved, a travel mount (the 1-60 way).** The user: "port the Mining and Herbing into the Master Farmer Questing & Grinding bot. Get all the required functions refactored to Core API and save all locations in the file. Include using a mount, like in the 1-60 product does." Routes: all 24 Path_Information routes (12 Horde, 12 Alliance) checked field by field against EP_Herb_Mine.lua - faction, skill window, map, every waypoint, merchant / ammo / food vendor names and coordinates match; the mailbox (`mail_coord`) was missing in all 24 and is now saved (as the source has it). Core API: gather/engine, supply and trainer no longer require izi (time = core.time, player = core.object_manager.get_local_player, the tracking cast = spellcheck.cast_safe by spell id). Mount: new movement/mount.lua (port of the 1-60 product's nav.lua mount_step, data/mounts.lua copied from it), used by movement/nav before every out-of-combat walk of 30 yd or more (questing, grinding, gathering; not combat or NPC walk-ups): a class mount spell (Summon Charger / Warhorse, Dreadsteed / Felsteed), else a mount item in the bags (data/mounts ids or item class 15 / subclass 5, swift first, min level), else the game's mount list (core.spell_book.get_mount_info -> core.input.mount); druids take Travel Form (aura read with get_buffs). Level 30+, outdoors, not in combat / swimming / casting (is_casting_spell / is_channelling_spell); the walk stops and the player stands for the cast; 5 failed tries -> 15 s back-off; game errors back off (can't mount / outside / in combat 25 s, swimming 60 s, not ready 15 s, while moving 1.5 s) via events.lua UI_ERROR_MESSAGE. gather/mount.lua is now a wrapper over it (node suppression kept). GUI: General tab 'Use Mount' (mfg_mount); gathering keeps its own 'mfg_gather_mount'. Tests: `_MASTER-FARMER_/tests/test_gather_mount.lua`. |
| 3.7.1 | **Crash 2026-10-16 18:06 (acct 3afa8783, Stormwind -> flight master): no stop / start churn on fresh walk legs.** Every logged crash (17:30, 17:35, 17:39, 18:06) came 0.6 - 1.6 s after a walk leg started (2.181.0 already found stopping a fresh leg to be the common factor). 18:06: the sharp-turn stop (movement/pivot) paused a 2.4 s-old walk, MasterArthasNav ended it as "arrived" 305 yd short, the turn was dropped the next frame and a fresh leg was issued 50 ms later, still facing the wrong way - crash 1.1 s into it. Now: a leg younger than 1 s (`N.leg_fresh`) is never held by the sharp-turn stop; a turn that started is finished toward its saved point even when the walk ended meanwhile; no travel leg starts while it turns (`R.turning`; combat walks still go); a deliberate `N.stop` ends the turn; an "arrived" more than 5 yd from the goal is logged and not counted (`N.false_arrival`). Exact walks also accept a partial server path (`info.partial`) instead of falling back to `move_to`, and a fallback logs the server's answer. Tests: `tests/test_fresh_leg.lua`. |
| 3.7.0 | **Exact server path, 15 yd at a time, without flooding the server.** Every out-of-combat walk (`nav_to` modes nil / move / guide / npc - combat keeps `move_to` with seamless retargets, recorded routes keep `follow_route`) = ONE `find_path` per destination, cut into 5 yd waypoints and walked exactly with `follow_path`. The path is prepared 15 yd ahead of the player per window (`guide_path.process_window`): object pushes (client scan <= every 0.5 s, props 1 yd, units their radius), wall probes 2 yd left / right of every waypoint (1 yd indoors, client `is_indoors` <= once a second) - a side the navmesh cannot reach straight is a wall and the waypoint moves 1 yd (0.5 indoors) away; both sides closed = corridor, kept on the line - and the validation of every move, in ONE `query.find_paths` batch per window: about one request per 15 yd walked, none per frame. The walk is restarted with a changed window only when a check moved a waypoint, >= 1.5 s apart (a `follow_path` restart is a walker stop - checked in the real library: `seamless` only exists for `move_to`). New objects next to the prepared path ahead re-prepare that stretch (batch, no new path; 3 per destination a minute). Exact walks keep the caller's mode (`cur.exact` flag; movement.interact's "npc" walk-up still survives a caller's nav_stop). Launcher 2.6.1 turns MasterArthasNav's corner cutting off (`string_pull_range` 0). Tests: `tests/test_guide_walls.lua` (window / request counts, walls, indoors, restarts, real library), `tests/test_movement3.lua`. |
| 3.6.1 | **Crash logs 2026-10-16 (17:30, 17:35, 17:39): guide walks stop re-planning in a loop; no model reads in the object scan.** All three crashes (the scripting core's "Your game crashed", no WoW report = a fault inside a script's native call) came 0.6-1.6 s after a walk was (re)started; the 17:39 one right after a guide re-plan, in a session that re-planned every ~2 s on the same object (a push the navmesh check had taken back stayed "too close" for ever) - each re-plan a stop and a fresh start of the walk. Now a re-check only counts objects that were not there when the path was planned (`guide_path.new_only`), waits 3 s after a walk starts, and re-plans at most 3 times per destination a minute. The object scan reads `get_bounding_radius` for units only (a unit field); props count as 1 yd, so no model data is read (an unloaded model is the M2HitTest crash class, 2.309.0). Tests: `tests/test_guide_walls.lua`. |
| 3.6.0 | **Master Farmer product window art.** The window is drawn from the product GUI mockups (`mf_skin.lua` + `mf_skin_geom.lua`, copied from `_MASTER-FARMER_/tools/product_skin` by `tools/sync_product_skin.py`; art in L333T/master-farmer-assets `gui/product`, 1:1 for 1080 / 1440 / 2160 screens): logo header with the product name (Questing & Grinding), version and hotkeys; the 5-tab row - General | Grinding (Grinding + Gathering) | Questing | Spells (Spells + Resting) | Settings, a shared tab shows its tabs as sub-tabs (`ui.new{ tab_groups }`); the left panel holds the tab, the right one the status rows; the art's Start / Pause / Resume / Stop and X run the actions / close. The drawn chrome is used until the art is loaded. `ui.lua` hook: `Menu:set_tab_groups`, `config.product`, `Menu:draw`. Test: `_MASTER-FARMER_/tests/test_product_skin.lua`. |
| 3.5.0 | **RestedXP walks: 3-yard waypoints, object-checked (`movement/guide_path.lua`).** quest/engine `walk_to` (RestedXP waypoints and movement instructions) goes through `movement.guide_to` (mode "guide"): MasterArthasNav `find_path`, resampled to 3 yd waypoints, each pushed clear of nearby objects (bounding radius + 0.9 body + 1.0 yd, at most 3 yd; never the destination; a push the mesh cannot walk straight to is taken back via `query.find_paths`), walked with `follow_path`. While walking, the next 5 waypoints are re-checked every 0.5 s against the objects around the player; one too close re-plans from where the player stands (at most every 2 s) before the collision. A nudge of a guide walk stays one; no path falls back to `move_to`. No ray casts. Wall clearance itself comes from launcher 2.6.0 (MasterArthasNav settings). Test: `_MASTER-FARMER_/tests/test_guide_walls.lua`. |
| 3.4.1 | **One loader: the Master Farmer launcher.** The standalone `plugin_loader/` and `plugin_loader.zip` are removed from the repo (copy in `_MASTER-FARMER_\projects-old\grindbot_plugin_loader`); docs, `make_manifest.py` and the manifest header name the launcher. The combat-mode warning names the MasterArthasNav menu path (ArthasNav tab > Movement > When combat starts). |
| 3.4.0 | **MASTER FARMER product chrome.** Every grindbot window uses the same layout: centered MASTER FARMER title, product tagline, six tab slots, two equal panels, Start / Paused / Stop, Discord footer. |
| 3.3.0 | **MasterArthasNav navigation server.** The nav client is looked up as `_G.ArthasNav` (MasterArthasNav's own name) first, `_G.AmeisenNav` only as the fallback - an old AmeisenNav plugin left installed can hold that alias (movement/nav.lua, gui.lua). Same client API (MasterArthasNav client 2.0.4 = AmeisenNav 2.0 calls; every call checked against the real library in _MASTER-FARMER_/tests/test_masterarthasnav.lua). Messages and the GUI say MasterArthasNav and point to MasterArthasNav-Setup.exe / its tray icon; the combat-mode hint names its ArthasNav > ArthasNav Movement page. The server start signal is sent by the Master Farmer launcher core (2.1.0). |
| 2.235.0 | **Bad terrain: read it, check routes ahead, learn it.** From the 23:04 grind session (v2.234.0): `coords_helper get_terrain_height first result: number 0` - the 2.221.0 terrain checks never saw any ground; the same spots - (-5696,-1667), (-5670,-1641), (-5692,-1652), (-5643,-1565) - caught the character every lap for an hour (stuck 20 s, 6-yd zone, expired after 15 min); Sentinel's obstacle list was repeatedly empty; "no way closer ... for 162s". (1) `movement/terrain.lua` heights: after 5 zero answers coords_helper is dropped for `core.get_height_for_position` with a chosen ray start, so ground above the character is readable (window 2.5 -> 15 yd). (2) Route check: each new Sentinel path (and every 10 yd of progress) is sampled every 2 yd for 40 yd ahead - ground under the path from 6 yd above it; where neighbouring samples on the path's layer climb more than 1.4 yd per yard, or drop more than 10 yd (fall damage - smaller drops are ordinary ledges), the spot becomes a hazard and the destination is re-planned around it (`N.repath_around`) before the character gets there; bridges / buildings (ground off the path's layer) are ignored. Shares the wall budget (6 s, 3 per destination). (3) `movement/hazards.lua` (new): stuck spots (repath area / recovery watch) and terrain walls / cliffs are learned hazards - `keep` zones in movement/zones (never pruned, evicted after temporary ones), kept within 250 yd of the character; a spot hit twice is saved to `scripts_data/mfg/hazards_<map>.txt` and loaded next session. Path tab: "Learn Bad Terrain" (on), "Forget Learned Terrain" (clears this map, unticks itself). (4) `N.move` waits up to 2.5 s for the find_path_avoid plan when zones are near, instead of a plain move_to straight through them. (5) `targeting.approach_stuck` restarts after 3 s without being asked (a mob returned to after resting was blamed for 162 s). Offline tests under lupa: hazards 7/7 (learn, merge, save, reload near, far not pushed, forget, learning off); terrain 11/11 (coords 0 -> core, uphill readable, wall refused, route cliff -> hazard + re-plan, ramp / bridge / 4 yd ledge allowed, 4 yd climb avoided); approach 7/7. |
| 2.234.0 | **Grind: start from anywhere on the path.** (1) A gap of more than 2 s between grind ticks (Start, resume, back from a vendor / rest / death run) sets the path rejoin: the character walks to the nearest point of the recorded path (5 yd = on it) and the route continues from the node that segment leads to, in the walking direction - it used to resume at whatever node index was left over, possibly the far side of the loop (the rejoin only ran once, when the module loaded). (2) Recorded grind paths are walked in their own node order: Sentinel's `plan_route` re-ordering (and its request) is kept for the built-in zones only. (3) The nearest-node snap is skipped for recorded paths - it could undo the rejoin and step back a node. |
| 2.233.0 | **Grind: 50-yard pulls for every class, paths always loop.** (1) The grind pulls the nearest valid mob within 50 yd of the character (`GRIND_PULL`), any class - 2.230.0's class-attack-distance limit (8 yd for melee) is gone. It walks to it, fights, and once nothing is attacking walks back to the nearest point of the path (2.230.0 rejoin) and continues; `targeting.approach_stuck` still drops a mob it cannot get closer to. (2) Grind paths never finish: a closed loop wraps to node 1; an open path (`loop = false`) turns round at each end and walks back along itself (`state.grind.dir`), instead of stopping at its last node unless "Loop Path" was ticked. Random Path's +2 steps follow the direction; the post-fight rejoin resumes at the segment end in the walking direction. The "Loop Path" tooltip says grind paths always loop. Offline test under lupa: open path 1-2-3-4-3-2-1-2-3, loop 1-2-3-4-1-2-3, rejoin forwards / backwards, pull 50. |
| 2.232.0 | **Rogue Strafe Combat: forward + strafe together, longer taps.** `strafe.lua`: each tap now holds two keys - right: `strafe_right_start`, `move_forward_start` ... `move_forward_stop`, `strafe_right_stop`; left: `move_forward_start`, `strafe_left_start` ... `move_forward_stop`, `strafe_left_stop`. Tap length +0.10 s (0.12-0.22 -> 0.22-0.32 s); pauses unchanged. Every release path (deadline, gate, range lost, stop, module load) lets go of the forward key too. Offline test under lupa: key order both ways, 8 taps 0.24-0.32 s, nothing held out of position. |
| 2.231.0 | **Memory spike after kills fixed (resting with no food).** 19:12 session (v2.220.0): after each kill at low health with no food in the bags, `SPIKE u:healing 59-76 ms, heap +8.7-9.0 MB`, then 60-75 ms every 2 s while waiting to regenerate - split as `rest:checks` 30-42 ms / 3-6 MB and `rest:supplies` 29-36 ms / 4.3-6 MB. Cause: `resting.lua` has_usable walked every known food / water id (~380 in data/consumables) and asked `izi.item(id):count()` for each - a bag scan per id - with nothing in the bags to end the loop early, up to four times per rest tick (food and water, in both sections). Fix: the bags are read once per second (`bags.list`, one inventory_helper call) and has_usable / use_first only query ids actually carried; after an item is used the read is refreshed. If the bags cannot be read (`bags.readable()` false) the old full walk is kept. Offline test under lupa: no food -> false with zero per-id queries; one bag read per second; carried food -> one query; unreadable bags -> full walk. |
| 2.230.0 | **Unreachable targets dropped; grinding stays on the path.** From the 19:12 session (v2.220.0): a Crag Boar below a mountain was chased 50 -> 60 -> 50 yd for 20 s and never given up - the repath ladder watches a destination on a 5-yard grid, and a moving mob keeps changing it. (1) `targeting.approach_stuck(player, unit, reach)`, called by the quest and grind fight_unit: per target GUID, outside attack reach and not casting, the distance must shrink 3 yd within 8 s; if not and `player:los_to(unit)` is false -> `state.mark_unreachable` (scans skip it), the target is released and the waypoint / path carries on; with line of sight it gets 15 s. (2) Grind pulls only mobs within the class attack distance (`rotation.combat_range`, at least 8 yd) - was the nearest within 100 yd - so the patrol keeps walking the path until something is in reach. (3) After a fight (and whenever it has drifted 8 yd off the line) the grind walks back to the nearest point of the recorded path (segments in node order, closing segment on a loop) and, once within 5 yd, resumes from the node that segment leads to. Attackers are still fought first (existing fight-back / hold). Offline tests under lupa: approach watch 6/6 (no-LoS 8 s, LoS 15 s, closing never, in reach never); path geometry and rejoin 7/7. |
| 2.229.0 | **Rogue Eviscerate id 2098 hard-coded.** Catalog entries may carry `id = <spell id>`: when the spell scan has no family by that name but the character knows the id (`is_spell_learned` / `has_spell`), `smart.lua` builds the entry (and its Spells-tab row) from that id; `ranks_of` falls back to it. Rogue Eviscerate gets `id = 2098`, and 2098 joins the spellbook's always-probed ids. When the scan does find Eviscerate, its ranks are used as before (highest known rank). Finisher rule unchanged: 4 combo points, or 2+ with the target under 25%. |
| 2.228.0 | **Rogue Stealth opener.** New rogue Spells-tab row "Stealth" (role `stealth`, Buffs section, ticked by default); Backstab now ticked by default (still only stealthed + behind, 2.227.0). `smart.lua` ROGUE STEALTH OPENER: a rogue opening a fight casts Stealth once the target is within 25 yd, casts nothing else while it sneaks in, and at melee opens with Backstab from the rear arc. Fallbacks: not behind 4 s after reaching melee -> the normal rotation opens (Sinister Strike); Backstab unticked / unknown -> no positioning, the first ticked melee spell opens; Stealth refused -> no opener for that target; already in combat -> normal rotation. The Throw pull stands aside while Stealth is ticked and known out of combat. `targeting.start_auto_attack` starts no swing while stealthed out of combat (izi `stealth_up`, else Stealth 1-4 / Prowl 1-3 buff ids), so the first swing does not break Stealth. `movement/combat.lua` behind_step: a profile `want_behind(player, unit)` (rogue, through `smart.rogue_wants_behind`) makes melee combat movement step to 2 yd behind the target along its `get_direction` facing, by its flank (3.5 yd) when the straight line would cross its front half. Offline tests under lupa: opener sequence (stealth at 22 yd, hold, behind -> Backstab, 4 s front fallback, no-Backstab path, throw returns with Stealth off) 12/12; behind_step geometry (flank first, then 2 yd behind, nothing when behind) 3/3; throw tests 15/15 with Stealth off. |
| 2.227.0 | **Rogue Backstab only from Stealth, from behind.** `data/class_spells` Backstab gets `when = c.stealthed() and c.behind()` (still unticked by default). New smart helpers: `c.stealthed()` (izi `stealth_up`, else the Stealth / Prowl buff) and `c.behind()` (izi `is_behind_unit`, else `is_behind`; unreadable = not behind), memoised per tick. The bot itself does not cast Stealth and does not walk behind the target. |
| 2.226.0 | **One target until it dies (every class).** `targeting.engaged(unit)`: alive and in combat, or alive and below full health (the combat flag drops for a moment on a fleeing / evading / crowd-controlled mob). A target being fought is never swapped: (1) `combat.acquire` (every rotation) no longer switches to a mob 3 yd closer or to a kill-first npc once the current target is engaged - those swaps apply only before the fight with it begins; (2) `targeting.attacker_to_switch` (grind and quest engines) keeps the current target while it is engaged (was: only while its combat flag was set); (3) the quest engine's fallback that took the nearest attacker when the player's combat flag dropped no longer fires while the current kill is engaged. Adds are fought after the current target dies (the existing next-target logic). A target not yet pulled is still dropped for a mob that attacks first. Interrupts on other casters in the pack (Kick, Counterspell) still go out. Offline test under lupa (combat.acquire + targeting.engaged): fighting A, closer B attacks -> A; A wounded with the combat flag off -> A; A not pulled -> B. |
| 2.225.0 | **Hunter: no Feed Pet, pet sent in reliably.** From the 18:31 hunter session (v2.220.0): "Feed Pet" was the status note every second of every fight - Feed Pet sat in "Other known spells", and a ticked other spell is cast on cooldown in fights by `racials.lua`. Pet care spells (Feed Pet, Dismiss Pet, Tame Beast, Beast Training, Call Pet, Revive Pet, Mend Pet) are no longer listed there (`smart.lua` OTHER_SKIP) and `racials.set_extra` drops them even if ticked. Pet attack: (1) `pets.attack` skipped the command whenever the pet already targeted the mob - a pet parked passive keeps its old target, so it stood by; now it skips only when the pet was sent in (assist) and is in combat. (2) `pets.passive` (out-of-combat upkeep) no longer recalls the pet within 6 s of an attack command or while the pet is in combat - it pulled the pet back during the hunter's own pull. (3) `smart.combat` sends the pet before the "player is casting" return. Offline test under lupa: passive pet with old target -> attack sent; no recall during pull or while the pet fights; no re-issue while attacking; passive after the fight. |
| 2.224.0 | **Rogue Throw pull.** New Spells-tab row "Throw" for rogues (role `pull`, ticked by default; Throw 2764 added to the spellbook's always-probed ids). With it ticked, Throw known (spell family or `is_spell_learned(2764)`) and a throwing weapon in the ranged slot (`targeting.has_thrown_equipped`: weapon subclass 16, INVTYPE_THROWN or "Thrown"; cached 2 s), a rogue opening a fight stops at 28 yd, throws once at the target (within 30 yd, line of sight), then holds there until the mob is within 5 yd and only then fights with the ticked melee rotation. `rotations/rogue.lua` gets `engage_range` (28 during the pull, else the Melee attack distance) and a 30 yd scan range while the pull is possible. Safeguards: a target picked up while already in combat (an add) is fought in melee, never thrown at; no throw within 6 s of getting ready (line of sight, failing cast) -> close in; mob not in melee 8 s after the throw -> close in. The rotation keeps running during the wait (Evasion on an add). Offline test under lupa (block extracted from smart.lua): stand at 28, throw at 27, hold, melee at 4, no second throw, add -> melee, 8 s and 6 s fallbacks, unticked / unknown / no thrown weapon -> plain melee. |
| 2.223.0 | **Hunter pet handler gate (`common/utility/pet_handler`).** `pets.handler_allowed(player)`: a Hunter uses the pet handler only at level 10+ with a live pet; at level 1-9 (or with no live pet) it is ignored - not even required, and `pets.on_render` does not pump it. Other classes (Warlock) unchanged. Cached 1 s (on_render asks every frame). New wrappers: `pets.set_state(player, "PASSIVE"|"DEFENSIVE"|"ASSIST", delay?)` and `pets.move_to(player, pos, delay?, duration?)` (`move_pet_to_position`, fresh vec3, position checked); both return false when the handler is absent or not allowed, so `pets.passive` / `pets.attack` still fall back to `core.input`. Offline test under lupa: hunter 9 -> not allowed, module never loaded; hunter 10 + pet -> render pumped, states (with delay), move_to; dead pet -> not allowed; warlock -> allowed. |
| 2.222.0 | **Hunter: Call Pet / Revive Pet hard-coded, 11-yard melee band, 40-yard range.** (1) `pets.hunter_pet` (from `smart.lua` pet upkeep, out of combat, while the Spells-tab "Call Pet" tick is on): Call Pet 883 and Revive Pet 982 by id, known via `is_spell_learned` / `has_spell` - no longer dependent on the spell scan. A dead pet object -> Revive Pet only; no pet and last seen at 25% or less within 30 s -> Revive Pet then Call Pet; no pet otherwise (dismissed) -> Call Pet then Revive Pet. Each spell gets its wait (Call 3 s, Revive 13 s) for a pet to appear; a cast refused client-side (Call Pet on a dead pet) moves on after 1 s. Both tried and still no pet -> 60 s hold (cleared when a pet is seen alive). Before, a dead pet that had vanished got Call Pet, failed, and sat in the 15 s fail gap forever. (2) `rotations/hunter.lua`: at or inside 11 yd (`hunter.dead_zone`, the game's own dead zone if ever wider) the Hunter always fights in melee - closes to 3 yd (Melee distance slider now 1-3, default 3), melee auto attack (targeting switches at the same band) and ticked melee spells; no backing out to shoot (`should_retreat` off, `can_kite` no longer used). Beyond 11 yd: Auto Shot and ticked shots from the Shooting distance (slider now 13-40, default 25), capped at 40 yd and 1 yd inside the weapon's real Auto Shot reach. Every hunter shot in `data/class_spells` has min = 11 (was 8); `smart.lua` in_reach takes the larger of catalog and game minimum and refuses a shot AT the minimum. Concussive Shot `when` 10 -> 16 yd (it can no longer fire inside 11). Offline test under lupa: dismissed -> Call; dying-vanished -> Revive; casting holds; dead object never Called; refused Call -> Revive after 1 s; no pet -> 60 s hold then retry; spells unknown -> nothing. |
| 2.221.0 | **Terrain-aware Sentinel pathing (`movement/terrain.lua`, `common/utility/coords_helper`).** (1) Waypoint height retry: a Sentinel "unreachable" for a destination within 150 yd now reads the floor heights at its x, y (`coords:get_terrain_height`, then `coords:map_to_world` at extra_height +4 / +20 / +50 / -15 after `world_pos_to_map_pos_normalized` on the current map; an answer more than 3 yd from the asked x, y is dropped) and asks again at an untried height (terrain ground first) - up to 4 heights per destination - before the old failure handling (3 s hold, off-mesh blacklist) runs. `nav.lua` sentinel_go asks `Tr.goal_z`. (2) Terrain walls: a Sentinel leg that has not moved the character 1.5 yd in 4 s (not casting, resting, planning or in Sentinel's own recovery, outdoors) samples the ground 8 yd ahead along the running path; a rise or drop over 1.4 yd per yard (or two samples with no answer) is a wall - its width is measured to each side (4-16 yd), an area of radius width+4 (6-18) is blacklisted just ahead and `N.repath_around` plans around it. One per 6 s, 3 per destination; a destination on the wall shrinks the zone or skips it. Before this a mountainside waited for the 20 s area watch and got a 6 yd zone. (3) Direct legs: `N.move` sends a <30 yd clear line as move_direct only when the terrain on it is walkable too. Raycast window: coords_helper heights come from a raycast started near player z + 4, so sampling stops once the ground is 2.5 yd above the character (uphill is never read as a cliff; such a line is planned, not walked direct). Every call pcall-guarded, off after 5 errors, first result shape logged (`terrain: coords_helper ... first result`). Caches reset on a continent change. Offline test under lupa (coords_helper stub with the player-z raycast): flat / downhill ok, cliff refused, uphill not vouched; a 20 yd wall -> one r12 zone + re-path, gap and cap respected; moving or gentle-uphill stalls -> nothing; unreachable at z 55 -> 12 -> 30 -> gives up. |
| 2.220.0 | **Lower memory, nothing given up.** Measured under LuaJIT: (1) `tools/gen_rxp_targets.py` writes each distinct NPC / mob name list once (`local L = {...}`) and the quest tables point at it - 6883 lists, 2377 distinct; `M.by_objective` is no longer written (nothing reads it). Every lookup (`by_quest`, `givers`, `takers`, `ids_by_quest`, `steps`, `items`) verified identical against the old generator on the same guide files; file 379 KB -> 261 KB. (2) `main.lua` releases the compiled main chunk of loaded `data/*` modules from `package.preload` (checked every 5 s; allowlist of data files nothing unloads at run time - grind zone / route files are not touched): the HTTP loader kept those chunks for the session although they never run again. `data/rxp_targets` resident: 2408 KB -> 801 KB; plus `ek_alliance_routes` (~275 KB compiled), `class_spells`, `taxi_nodes` and the small data files. In-game heap was 17-20 MB (peak 20.7). RestedXP guide files on disk changed since the last generation (1119 -> 1123 quests, 749 -> 761 NPC steps); this data is from the current guides. Also checked: the 16:09 session (v2.218.0) disconnected between 16:15:30 and 16:15:50 mid-fight - bot ticks stop, the client kept rendering at 60 fps for 7 min; no error, warning or memory growth was logged before it. |
| 2.219.0 | **Sentinel advanced APIs (`movement/sentinel_adv.lua`, ticked from the movement pulse).** Sentinel documents these lower-level services by name only and says they may change, so every call is feature-detected, pcall-guarded, switched off for the session after 5 errors, and logs its first result shape. (1) `nav_client.get_continent_id` (static), polled every 2 s: a confirmed continent change clears the blacklist zones and Sentinel's mirror (`Z.clear_all`), Sentinel answer caches (`N.reset_caches`: reach, prefetch, chase, avoid, check, corridor, failed destination) and halts the walk - all of those are x / y only and coordinates repeat on every continent (audit H6). (2) `nav_client.check_path` on the rest of the running Sentinel path every 3 s: an explicit "invalid" -> `replan("path_invalid")`. (3) `obstacle:probe_path_ahead` on the next 3 segments every 1 s: an explicit `blocked` field twice in a row -> `replan("obstacle_ahead")`; a bare boolean is ambiguous and ignored. Replans from (2)/(3): one per 8 s, two per destination, then only logged. (4) `obstacle:get_zone_count` every 10 s: fewer zones than were pushed (server restart) -> pushed again (`Z.resync_sentinel`). Not used on purpose: `client.movement` (the client drives it), `find_route_tsp` (plan_route already), `find_route_multi` (RestedXP owns quest order), `remove_zone` / `get_avoidance_zones` (index base / shape undocumented). Offline test under LuaJIT: continent reset once; invalid path 2 replans then muted; blocked x2 replans, bare true ignored; lost zones re-pushed; erroring API disabled after 5. |
| 2.218.0 | **Fix: nothing loaded in game since 2.215.0.** 2.215.0 reset its new NPC-identity state (`TK.gid`) inside `quest/engine.lua` tick_inner, giving that function a 61st upvalue. The client's Lua is 5.1-shaped (Lua 5.1 / LuaJIT: at most 60 upvalues per function) and refused the file - "function at line 2888 has more than 60 upvalues" - so the loader's install failed ("load failed - the download did not complete"). make_local only compiled with Lua 5.4 (limit 255), so 2.215.0 - 2.217.0 shipped. The reset now lives in `commit_pending()`, which tick_inner's goal-change block already calls (tick_inner back to 60). `make_local.py` now also compiles every file with Lua 5.1 and LuaJIT 2.1 (lupa.lua51 / lupa.luajit21) and fails the build on an error; verified it rejects the 2.217.0 engine with the same message. The `[CURL] ... result: 7` line was never the cause: it is the loader's fire-and-forget POST to the local boot log at 127.0.0.1:47110 (plugin_loader/header.lua), refused whenever nothing listens there. Loader 1.3.0 (jsDelivr mirror), built on that misreading, is reverted - the loader is 1.2.3 again. |
| 2.217.0 | **Movement audit, Phase 1 (docs/MOVEMENT_AUDIT.md) + Path mode removed.** (1) `movement/sentinel.lua`: a new leg clears a Sentinel stop still deferred for the previous one (`begin_leg`, and the underfoot-skip follow) - `move_direct` short legs did not, so the old stop killed the new leg. (2) `N.stop` cancels a pending `repath_around` job (`N.cancel_repath`); every stop path (`O.halt_all`: nav_stop, halt, rest lock, combat) comes through it - the job used to issue a Sentinel move up to 4 s after a stop, including while eating. (3) `vendor.lua`: no `nav_stop` before the walk to a merchant standing > 5 yd from its recorded spot (stop/start stutter every tick); the stop now happens in reach, or when the merchant is missing. (4) `watchdog.lua`: combat or an attacker ends the 12 s Hearthstone hold, so the bot fights back. (5) `combat.dismount` / resting: one dismount per second instead of one per tick. **Path mode removed:** `modes.lua` deleted - its race table moved to `grind/zone_lookup.lua` (its only user; the unused race_label / race_has_starter_quests dropped), the Grind / Quest mode names to `gui.lua` (`gui.modes`); the never-called Path combat code in `main.lua` (`path_fight`, `path_handle_combat`, `pause_path_for_combat`, `rotation_yards`, `in_rotation_range`, `closest_in_range`, `unit_has_los`) removed. `path_runner.lua` stays: it still draws the loaded grind route. Offline replays (lupa, real modules, stubbed game): each of 1, 2, 4, 5 fails on 2.216.0 and passes now; 3 checked by review. |
| 2.216.0 | **In combat or attacked on the way to an NPC: the walk waits, the fight comes first.** `quest/engine.lua` fight_back already ran first every quest tick, but handed the tick back to the walk in two cases: (1) attackers were looked for only while the player's combat flag was set - a mob targeting the player or pet while the flag had dropped for a moment (a fled or evading mob coming back) was ignored and the turn-in walk went on; "being attacked" (`targeting.attackers`) now counts as combat and the nearest attacker is engaged; (2) in combat with no attacker in view, the hold before walking on was 8 s for every goal; for a turn-in / accept / talk goal it is now 30 s (`targeting.combat_hold(player, max)`), note "Guide: in combat - <goal> waits". After the fight the goal's walk resumes on its own. Attackers marked unreachable are still not chased. Offline replay: attacked with the flag off -> engaged (2.215.0 walked on); turn-in hold 30 s, kill goal still 8 s; out of combat -> walk proceeds. |
| 2.215.0 | **Quest NPCs: the guide's NPC, not the nearest one.** The 12:14 session (step 23, accept Bring Back the Mug 3365): the 5-minute Scalding Mornbrew Delivery (3364) had expired, so Durnan Furcutter - the right NPC - had nothing to offer; the engine called that "not this NPC" and walked through eight Anvilmar NPCs (Marryk, Teo, Branstock, Alamar, Emrys, Thran, Grund, Gozwin), three tries each, for minutes. Later (step 19, turn in 3364) it went to Grelin Whitebeard 6 yd away instead of the guide waypoint 200 yd off, because Grelin was a "guide giver" and a learned taker. Causes and fixes: (1) `tools/gen_rxp_targets.py` gave every quest of a step every `.target` name on it, with no role (3364 / 3365 both listed Durnan, Grelin, Marryk, Nori).
