Now each `.accept` / `.turnin` takes its own `.target` line (both guide layouts: target after its quests, or before) and the data is split into M.givers (accept) and M.takers (turn-in); `guide.rxp_quest_givers(qid, kind)` reads the role's list. 3365 givers: Durnan only; 3364 givers: Nori only. Target names drop the "::npcid" suffix. (2) `find_giver`: a guide npc id, learned id, waypoint title or guide name only counts when the NPC stands within 40 yd of the goal's waypoint (names were searched around the player). After an identified NPC has been talked to, the player-target / nearest-at-waypoint / inside-the-building fallbacks are off. (3) `dialog_goal`: "not offered" from the identified NPC sets the goal aside with a diagnostic (npc, on quest, completed) and lets RestedXP decide - its guide steps back for a failed timed quest; a timeout waits BAD_GIVER_RETRY to ask the same NPC again; the 30 s stall recovery still bounds it. (4) `commit_pending` learns a turn-in NPC only when the hand-in is proven (`npc.was_turned_in` or flagged completed), not when the quest is merely gone. Offline replay of the engine (lupa, stubbed game): not-offered -> one interaction, set aside; offered -> accepted; learned NPC off the waypoint ignored; timeout -> same NPC again after 20 s; no identity -> nearest fallback kept; learning needs the hand-in. |
| 2.214.0 | **Waypoint height from the map_to_world fallback is no longer final.** Checked the IZI Maps / coords_helper API against `quest/guide.lua`. `coords_helper:map_to_world` (the fallback when `core.game_ui.get_world_pos_from_map_pos` gives nothing) raycasts from the PLAYER's height + extra_height (stub), so a far waypoint on higher or lower ground got the wrong floor, and it was cached as final - the terrain height (inside 120 yd) and Sentinel `get_all_heights` (beyond) never replaced it. Its x, y are kept and z now takes the same path as the pure conversion. Reviewed, not adopted: `izi.map_to_world` (the same call as the coords_helper one already used), `is_valid_map_coords` (a cursor edge-click guard that rejects > 0.99 - it would drop real waypoints), `get_minimap_id` (RestedXP supplies map_id), cursor functions (no click-to-move). `izi.get_terrain_height` was already the near-height source. |
| 2.213.0 | **Movement audit against the Sentinel v0.0.8 / RestedXP docs: three gaps closed, the rest kept.** (1) Sentinel server recovery: every request is gated on `is_server_available()`, which Sentinel sets only after a successful response, so once it dropped nothing of ours asked the server again. While it is down, `N.client` now sends the documented `health_check` every 30 s; a good answer re-probes the client at once. (2) Indoor narrow-corridor re-plan (`maybe_corridor`) runs once per destination (4-yd key): `find_path_corridor` is documented as a path *with widths*, not a wider path, so re-asking every 2 s could restart the walk on an equal path for as long as the character stood in the doorway. Width threshold now `2 * K.BODY_HALF` (same 1.0 as before). (3) RestedXP waypoints: a 0,0 map coordinate is rejected, and the `coords_helper:map_to_world` fallback rejects a 0,0 world result like the primary conversion already did. Kept as is (already equal or better): validate_destination (cached `N.reachable` / `node_reachable`), move_to / move_direct / follow_path / find_path_avoid / check_path, replan (ladder rung 1, rate-limited), plan_route (grind only - RestedXP order is never changed), get_progress / get_path_index (progress = not stuck), bus stuck events with the 2.192.0 stalled-recovery takeover, get_all_heights for far waypoint z, zone mirroring with TTL. Not adopted: `start_route` and `client.obstacle` probes (not in the installed stub; probe result shapes undocumented; Sentinel's own log placed an obstacle zone 270 yd from the stuck character), `remove_zone` (index base undocumented), `get_player_all_heights`, extra bus events. |
| 2.212.0 | **Rogue "Strafe Combat" (Spells tab, rogues only).** New `strafe.lua`: in combat, while combat movement is in position on a live target, tap strafe right / left 0.12-0.22 s each with a 0.06-0.12 s pause between (core.input.strafe_*_start / _stop only, deadlines, no sleeps). Range is the engine's own: `movement.combat_in_position` (new, read-only) reports the `combat_stopped` latch of `combat_engage` with its hysteresis band, while combat movement is still requested and owned, no rest / backpedal / retreat / root, target valid and alive. `strafe.update` runs once per frame after the cascade from the existing update callback, so the frame `combat_engage` leaves its band and issues the chase is the frame the key is let go. Class, setting, started, Rotation Only, in combat and casting gates are read every 0.1 s; any failing one releases the key. A stale instance and a module load release both keys. Checkbox `mfg_strafe_combat` (class_id ROGUE, so hidden on every other class); the class is checked again at run time because menu elements are saved per account. |
| 2.211.0 | **Forever guide waypoints were dropped by the generator.** 12279 of the Forever guides' 14445 .goto lines use RestedXP's "zone/continent,a,b" form, which RestedXP passes to HereBeDragons' GetZoneCoordinatesFromWorld - world coordinates in (world y, world x) order (functions.lua). tools/gen_rxp_targets.py only read "zone name,x%,y%" and plain map ids, so every Forever NPC step anchored only that way was missing from M.steps. It now keeps them as world anchors (w = { x, y, ... }) and guide.rxp_step_for matches them against the live waypoint's world position within 35 yd (749 NPC steps, was 640; e.g. vendor 1682 at Thelsamar). From ActiveItemFrame.lua (RestedXP's "Active Items" bar, fed by .use / .usespell lines): no change - .use lines are text-only and in these guides mostly "equip the weapon you just bought" hints, which equip.lua already covers by rating. |
| 2.210.0 | **Fix: quest/engine.lua did not compile in 2.209.0.** The file crossed Lua's limit of 200 local variables in one chunk ("too many local variables (limit is 200) in main function"), so the quest engine failed to load - the release went out because the build's "1 failed" syntax line was not checked. The 2.204.0-2.209.0 file-level locals are folded into two tables (TK: vendor-step / gossip-choice state; IS: the .destroy / .equip step), bringing the file back under the limit; no behaviour change. Releases now stop unless make_local reports 0 failed. |
| 2.209.0 | **RestedXP ".destroy" and ".equip" steps.** From the Forever guides (42 .destroy, 29 .equip lines): both gate their step in RestedXP (functions.lua) - .destroy until the item is gone from the bags, .equip (with text) until that exact item is in that slot - and the bot only walked to the waypoint. tools/gen_rxp_targets.py now records M.items (item name in the line's [brackets] -> item id, + equip slot); guide.rxp_item_for finds it from the goal text (also RestedXP's own "Throw away X from your bags"). quest/engine item_step_goal: .destroy deletes each stack with core.input.destroy_container_item; .equip puts the item in the named slot with core.input.equip_container_item (answering a bind-on-equip prompt); done when the item is gone / in the slot, after 5 tries, or when it is not in the bags. Out of combat only. The Hearthstone (6948, an optional .destroy in some guides) is never destroyed - vendor trips hearth home. .deathskip (die on purpose for a graveyard shortcut) is left to the player. |
| 2.208.0 | **Bag rules from RestedXP's InventoryManager.** (1) "Delete Junk When Full" (Vendor tab, on): when a loot window still holds items with the bags full, vendor.destroy_cheapest_junk deletes the cheapest grey first so the loot fits - RestedXP's rating, (max stack + count) * sell price / 2, so a nearly full cheap stack goes before a single valuable grey - through core.input.destroy_container_item (clear cursor, pick up, delete as one guarded call). Greys only; never a kept item, food / drink, consumable, ammo or quest item; at most one per second. Until now full bags with no vendor in reach could only lose the loot. (2) "Max Soul Shards" (Vendor tab, 0 = off): a warlock keeps at most that many shards; extras are deleted out of combat, one every 3 s (RestedXP maxSoulShards). (3) Grey item 6196 is RestedXP's junk exception and is kept (never sold or deleted). Not taken: RestedXP's quiver sorting (TBC hunters, bag families) and its bag-UI junk icons. |
| 2.207.0 | **WoW Forever spell list from RestedXP.** RXPGuides DB/forever/spells.lua (addon.defaultSpellList) is RestedXP's Forever trainer list: spell ids per class and race. (1) New tools/gen_forever_spells.py -> data/forever_spells.lua (17 groups, 954 ids). On Forever, spellbook.lua asks the ownership calls about this class's and race's ids on every scan (like racial ids), so a spell the client's book walk leaves out is still found. (2) Spell names Forever renamed or added, missing from data/class_spells (matched by name, so never offered): Warlock Bane of Agony (ticked) and Bane of Doom - group "bane", apart from the curses; Priest Starshards (channelled) and Chastise; Druid "Mangle" (cat). Pounce / Ravage are stealth openers the bot does not use. Regenerate after a RestedXP update: `python tools/gen_forever_spells.py`. |
| 2.206.0 | **More from the RestedXP guide files: NPC ids, quest givers, gossip choices.** tools/gen_rxp_targets.py (now also reading RXPGuides DB/*/db.lua addon.mapId to turn ".goto Dun Morogh,29.5,73.2" into UiMapIDs) adds to data/rxp_targets.lua: M.steps - 640 NPC steps with their .goto anchors, the ".vendor <npc>" / ".trainer <npc>" npc ids and the gossip choices (.gossipoption / .skipgossipid option ids, .skipgossip npc + option numbers); M.givers - friendly NPC names (|cRXP_FRIENDLY_...| and .target) on the steps that accept / turn in each quest (2449 quests). guide.rxp_step_for matches the live step by its waypoint (same UiMapID, within 1.2% of an anchor - the API gives no guide or step id). Used by: find_giver - a vendor / trainer step goes to the guide's npc id first (ids work where names are blank, WoW Forever), accept / turn-in tries the guide's giver names after the waypoint title; trainer quest visits rank the guide's trainer id first; the talk goal makes the guide's gossip choice when the frame is still open 1 s after it opened (RestedXP's own gossip automation acts first when it is on), once per frame. .choose (reward pick) is not in any Classic / TBC / Forever guide - Retail only - so the bot's own reward choice stays. RestedXP's low-priority '*' prefix no longer leaks into mob names. |
| 2.205.0 | **Vendor steps close the merchant window.** RestedXP's own source (RXPGuides functions.lua, addon.functions.vendor) completes a ".vendor" step on MERCHANT_CLOSED after MERCHANT_SHOW - and ".trainer" on TRAINER_CLOSED - not when the window opens. The quest engine's talk goal closed only the gossip when it counted the goal done, so the merchant window stayed up and the step could still not complete after 2.204.0's fix. It now also closes the merchant (core.input.close_merchant) and the trainer window it opened. |
| 2.204.0 | **Log fixes (17:41 session): vendor step loop, wrong continent.** (1) A RestedXP ".vendor" step ("Vendor Trash", step 35) looped for two hours: the bot talked to the nearest NPC at the waypoint (npc 1694, a quest giver), counted the open gossip as done, and RestedXP - which ticks the step on MERCHANT_SHOW - never moved on (59 "talk goal done", "did not move on in 30s"). quest/engine dialog_goal now, on a vendor step without a merchant window: skips an NPC the game does not flag as a vendor (npc flag 0x80), selects the gossip's vendor option (izi VENDOR icon / type / wording), and rules out an NPC that opens no merchant after 3 tries so the next NPC at the waypoint is tried; the goal is done only once the merchant window is up. (2) gamever.continent_of: core.get_map_id() answers the zone's UiMapID (1426 Dun Morogh); the fallback took the nearest flight point by raw x / y, and Eastern Kingdoms / Kalimdor coordinates overlap, so Dun Morogh was "continent 1" ("nearest Marshal's Refuge, Un'Goro Crater") and flight plans and supply-run continent checks were wrong. Known classic / TBC UiMapIDs now map to their continent first (ids from RXPGuides addon.mapId). |
| 2.203.0 | **Dead enemies from the documented API.** (1) targeting.find_corpses takes unit_helper:get_enemy_list_around(player position, range, incl_out_combat, -, -, include_dead = true) first - the core-cached enemy list the API documents for finding dead enemies - and still adds the object scan's corpses (a neutral mob the bot killed is not in an enemy list once dead), de-duplicated by GUID. (2) loot.lua near scan, every tick, with the example plugin's filter: izi.enemies_if(10 yd) dead + is_valid + can_be_looted + has_loot -> queued as ours at once (the 1 s scan and kill reports still run; looted corpses stay ignored). (3) The done trail line counts the loot requests actually sent ("looted after 2 loot request(s)"), not completed burst cycles - "empty after 0 loot cycle(s)" read as if nothing had been tried. |
| 2.202.0 | **Mages drink again on WoW Forever: one mana reader.** The rest log read "MP 100 (drink at 0)" for a Mage - the drink line is printed 0 when the character is judged to have no mana. Every module read mana through the izi extensions (mana_max / mana_current / mana_pct), which gave nothing on Forever, so has_mana was false, mana read as the 100% fallback and the bot never drank (eating and the item use itself were fine: izi.item + use_self_safe, "used Tough Jerky / Conjured Water" in earlier logs). New power.lua: mana_max / mana_current / mana_pct / has_mana try izi first and fall back to the native game_object get_max_power / get_power with Enum.PowerType.Mana (0), every method lookup and call guarded. Used by resting (drink decision), healing (mana potions), racials, smart (wand / mana conditions), supplies (water runs), targeting and the heartbeat line. |
| 2.201.0 | **Corpse run with an infinite corpse position.** After a death on WoW Forever core.game_ui.get_corpse_position answered (inf, inf, inf); death.lua took it as a position ("Corpse inf yd"), Sentinel was asked for it for over a minute ("HTTP 400: Coordinates must be finite numbers", server disconnects, "Navigation failed: unreachable") and an (inf, inf, inf) r12 blacklist zone was added every few seconds - the ghost never moved. (1) movement/util U.xyz - the coordinate gate for every move, Sentinel request and blacklist zone - now refuses infinity as well as NaN. (2) death.lua refuses a non-finite corpse position and falls back to the last good one, else to where the character died: the body's position is recorded at the death, before the release (not when the death is first seen as a ghost, which stands at the graveyard). |
| 2.200.0 | **RestedXP target mobs from the guide files.** RestedXP's own source (RXPGuides functions.lua) shows why 2.199.0 could not work alone: `.mob` / `.target` / `.unitscan` keep their mob names in `element.unitlist` with no `element.text`, and core.addons.rested_xp exposes only action, quest_id, text, is_complete, text_only and ids per goal - the names never reach the API. New generated table data/rxp_targets.lua (tools/gen_rxp_targets.py, run against the installed RXPGuides): for every guide step, the quests on its `.complete <quest>,<objective>` / `.collect <item>,<qty>,<quest>` lines are paired with the mobs on its target lines and the enemy names RestedXP colours in its `>>` text (`|cRXP_ENEMY_...|r`, plurals singularised) - 1119 quests from 118 guide files (Retail / Cata / MoP / Dailies left out). `guide.targets` adds a fight goal's quest mobs (and numeric npc ids) to the names `find_mob` attacks, ahead of the item-word and camp guesses: "0/8 Tough Wolf Meat" (quest 179) now targets Ragged Young Wolf, as RXPTargetFrame does. The per-step "RestedXP targets" trail line falls back to these names. Regenerate after a RestedXP update: `python tools/gen_rxp_targets.py`. |
| 2.199.0 | **Kill targets from RestedXP's target lines.** RestedXP's target frame (RXPTargetFrame, Targeting.lua) lists the step's .target / .mob / .unitscan / .rare lines, and a collect step ("0/8 Tough Wolf Meat") usually names its mobs only there - as text-only goals of the same step. The kill scan read only the goal being worked and the sticky steps, so those names never reached it and the bot fell back to guessing by item words or fighting whatever stood at the waypoint. `guide.step_target_names` reads every such line of the current step (progress, a leading Target / Kill / Slay verb, colour codes and brackets dropped; comma / or / and lists split) and `guide.targets` adds them (and any numeric ids) for every fight goal, so `find_mob` attacks those mobs before the drop-source and camp fallbacks. One trail line per change: "step N RestedXP targets: ...". There is no API to read the RestedXP frame itself; core.addons.rested_xp only exposes steps, goals, objectives and waypoints. |
| 2.198.0 | **New loot logic: the burst.** Looting fired one loot_object(corpse, true) a second and called the corpse done the moment its lootable flag dropped - items could still be on it. Taken from the example auto-loot plugin: once loot.tick has walked into reach and stopped (queue, walk, fight-first and give-up rules unchanged), the new `loot.frame` (main.lua, every frame) bursts at the corpse - loot_object now, again the next frame, a third time at least 20 ms later, then a 400 ms pause - and repeats while the corpse still says can_be_looted and has_loot, up to 5 cycles. Checked before every attempt after the first, so an emptied corpse stops the burst at once; the auto-loot flag is passed every call (it is sticky in the core); movement_handler:pause_movement_light(0.5) holds the character still for the request. A corpse is "looted" only when can_be_looted or has_loot answers no after a cycle. The burst target expires 0.5 s after loot.tick stops renewing it (a fight, Stop, a new corpse). |
| 2.197.0 | **Priest and Warlock wands.** Shoot (wand) was only in the Mage catalog, so a Priest never had it in the Spells tab or the rotation. (1) data/class_spells: Shoot added to PRIEST and WARLOCK as the filler (ticked). (2) Spells tab: "Wand below mana %" slider for Priest and Warlock (0-100, default 20; 100 = wand whenever no heal, DoT or other spell is due). (3) smart.lua fires the wand only when targeting.has_wand_equipped says a wand is in the ranged slot (Mage too) - with none equipped, damage spells carry on. (4) Engaging from range starts the wand auto-attack for Priest and Warlock as well as Mage, when one is equipped. (5) spellbook asks about wand Shoot (5019) directly on every scan, in case the client's spell list leaves it out. |
| 2.196.0 | **"Other known spells" lists only castable spells.** On WoW Forever the passive attribute check (spell_has_attribute 0 / 0x40) never answers, so the section showed passives - Armor Proficiency, Dodge, Engineering Specialization, Expansive Mind, Languages (15:51 log). A spell is now listed only once core.spell_book.is_usable_spell has answered true for one of its ranks; a passive never does. Remembered for the session, so an active spell short of mana at one scan stays listed. |
| 2.195.0 | **Eureka! at the start of every fight.** WoW Forever racial (1259817): the next 3 damage abilities cost 10% less mana and deal 10% more damage. New racial kind `fight_start` (data/racials, racials.lua): cast on the player as soon as there is a live fight target - at the pull, ahead of the first damage spell (the racials step runs before damage in smart.lua) - once per fight. It is free again when the player was in combat after the cast and has then been out of combat 2 s (or 30 s out of combat when the pull never became a fight). Eureka! is now ticked by default with its real tooltip. |
| 2.194.0 | **Spell scanner: every spell the API can see, racials included, on any game version.** A WoW Forever Mage's racial Eureka! (1259817) never reached the Spells tab. Two gaps: the tab built its racial rows only from the hard-coded TBC list in data/racials, so a racial not on it was never shown or used; and the book is core.spell_book.get_spells(), a walk that can leave spells out. (1) spellbook.lua asks the ownership calls (has_spell / is_spell_learned / is_spell_known) about every racial id in data/racials on each scan, listed by the walk or not, plus anything given to the new `spellbook.probe_ids`; probed ids are kept out of the "game can name it" fallback. (2) data/racials: Eureka! added; `race = nil` entries are offered to any race whose book has them; uncatalogued effects start unticked (`default = false`). (3) Spells tab: a new "Other known spells" section lists every other spell family in the book that the class catalog does not (passive spells via spell_has_attribute(id, 0, 0x40), Attack, Auto Shot and Shoot left out), unticked, with the game's own description as the tooltip; ticked, racials.lua uses it on cooldown in a fight (enemy first, then self). (4) One log line per change of what the scan found: "Spells tab: N class spell(s); racials: ...; other known spells (n): ...". |
| 2.193.0 | **Performance audit Plan A, plus a rest probe.** From the 2026-10-01 audit (0.25 ms/frame median; u:healing the only multi-millisecond stage, lined up with a 31 MB heap spike). (1) `gui.sync_player` ran twice a frame (on_update and gui.draw) and pushed class, race and the class combo into the menu every time - the largest steady cost (0.069 ms). Now only from on_update; class and race are read once a second and pushed only when they change; the combo is put back only if it moved. (2) `errorlog.tick` called collectgarbage("count") every frame just for the peak: now once a second (the MEM line still reads the heap when it writes). (3) resting.tick no longer does pcall(require, "gui") every tick - it uses its own top-level `gui`. (4) Rest probe: marks inside healing.tick (heal:attackers / loot_hold / rotation.rest) and resting.tick (rest:auras / food_ids / water_ids / checks / supplies / decide) split u:healing in the PERF line, and main.lua times each healing.tick call - one over 1 ms or growing the heap 512 KB writes a SPIKE line (at most one per 2 s) naming the slowest segments with their ms and heap change, e.g. "SPIKE u:healing 1.70 ms, heap +1910 KB (12000 -> 13910) | rest:food_ids 1.30 ms +1900 KB; ...". The heap is counted at the marks only while that call is open. |
| 2.192.0 | **Re-path when Sentinel's stuck recovery goes nowhere.** The 13:25 Sentinel log: the character frozen at one spot (to the hundredth of a yard) in navigating.recovering - stuck attempts 1-5, then a re-plan to the same 68-point path, over and over, no "recovered" event. `R.sn_recovering` held the re-path ladder, the stall check and the 20 s stuck-area watch off for as long as that lasted, so Master Farmer never re-pathed. Now a Sentinel recovery that has not moved the character 2 yd in 6 s no longer holds anything (`N.recovery_stalled` / `N.recovering`; `N.planning` stops counting it): `RP.recovery_watch` blacklists the 6 yd area just ahead and re-paths around it at once, a jump first to free a wedged character. The area ahead now follows the running path's next point (`N.ahead_point`), not the straight line to the goal. The re-path itself is new: 2.190.0's "stuck_avoid" went through N.move, which with a leg running is a plain find_path (no avoid zones, possibly the cached old path) - the same blocked route. `N.repath_around` asks find_path_avoid with the blacklisted areas, keeps the stuck leg until the plan is back (up to 4 s, `N.repath_tick` per pulse), follows it, and falls back to move_to (Sentinel's obstacle list still has the area). A new leg, a follow or a stop resets the recovery clock. |
| 2.191.0 | **Log fixes: hearthstone bind crash, profession trainer steps, buy-and-equip steps, stuck watch in melee.** (1) quest/engine dialog_goal: a local `gossip` (frame shown, a boolean) shadowed the gossip module, so a ".home" step errored "attempt to index local 'gossip'" and never bound the hearthstone - renamed `gossip_open`. (2) A RestedXP "Train [Mining]" / "Train [Blacksmithing]" step (any profession) no longer goes to the class trainer: it walks to the waypoint's titled NPC or the nearest profession trainer (NPC flag 0x40), opens its training, buys that profession's service and verifies it; a trainer that does not teach it is skipped for the next; 60 s cap, and a profession that failed is not walked for again this session. trainer.lua is held off (`trainer.hold`) meanwhile so it does not spend at a profession window. (3) A ".vendor" step reading "Buy and equip a [X]" (or "Buy a/an X") is a buy of one item through the buy step, then equip.lua re-checks the bags to wear it - before, the vendor was only talked to and the step retried every 30 s. (4) The 20 s stuck-area watch pauses while within reach of the combat target (it fired at 4 yd while meleeing). |
| 2.190.0 | **Stuck areas: blacklisted after 20 s, and paths go around them.** The re-path ladder works per goal and, when it gives up, blacklists the destination - not the spot the character is caught on. New movement/repath area watch (every movement frame): while movement is trying to move (a travel leg or a combat chase) and the character stays within 3 yd for 20 s of trying - casting, resting, standing in reach and Sentinel still planning pause the clock - a 6 yd area just ahead toward the goal is blacklisted (character just outside it, destination never inside it; too close to the goal and nothing is blacklisted). The ladder restarts, a travel leg is stopped and the same destination asked for again ("stuck_avoid"); combat movement steers clear on its next hop. Blacklisted areas already went to Sentinel's obstacle list; now they also go to find_path_avoid with the dangerous mobs, so the new path is planned around them explicitly. Trail: "stuck 20s at (x, y) ... area (x, y) r6 blacklisted, re-pathing around it". |
| 2.189.0 | **UI reload every 30 minutes while running.** main.lua calls `core.reload_game_ui()` 30 minutes after Start and every 30 minutes after that, only at a safe moment: not in combat, casting, resting, looting, at a vendor or trainer, with an NPC window open, dead, on a flight, or with a live kill target - a due reload waits for the next safe tick. Stop clears the timer. Each reload writes "Periodic UI reload (every 30 min)" to the session log. Guarded like the reload on landing (2.107.0). |
| 2.188.0 | **Questing tab trimmed.** (1) "Kill mobs on the way" is gone with its logic (the quest engine's path_pull, which walked off to fight the nearest enemy within 100 yd while travelling). Mobs that attack are still fought (fight_back), and "Grind to XP" steps still pull the nearest XP-worthy enemy (xp_goal / nearest_xp_enemy kept). (2) The "RestedXP Detection" toggle button is gone: the detection panel is always shown on the Questing tab; the button row is Clear Skipped and Vendor. (3) "Log quest and guide steps" is gone from the Questing tab: its logging (goals taken up, NPC matching, accept / hand-in steps, reward choices) now runs under the General tab's "Detailed session log", whose tooltip says so. |
| 2.187.0 | **Auto turn-in, as the API example does it, with a checked result.** quest/npc.lua now reads the gossip quest rows from `core.quests.get_gossip_active_quests()` / `get_gossip_available_quests()` first (izi's views only when those are empty), selects with `select_gossip_active_quest(quest.quest_id)` and calls `complete_quest()` straight away - the progress panel's Continue - instead of waiting for a QUEST_PROGRESS event that may come late or not at all. The completion panel still takes the best reward (`get_quest_reward`). A hand-in counts only with proof: QUEST_TURNED_IN, the quest flagged completed, or the quest gone from the quest log after being in it; otherwise it is logged as "NOT confirmed" and tried again, up to 3 times, then given up. |
| 2.186.0 | **Quests handed in to class trainers; a junk-seller addon no longer stops selling.** (1) quest/npc.lua ended an accept / turn-in as "not_offered" as soon as the interact opened a TRAINER (or merchant / flight map) window, and the NPC was ruled out - a class trainer who gives class quests opens its training window first, especially right after the trainer module picked "train me", so class quests were never handed in to their trainer. When the unit is a quest giver (npc flag 0x2), the window is now closed (close_trainer / close_merchant / taxi.close) and the NPC asked again, up to the usual 3 tries. The 10:34 Glyphic Letter failure (the mage class quest) was the wrongly learned vendor fixed in 2.185.0. (2) The 11:00 vendor read "took a different item - selling stopped" while the free slots went 3 -> 12 in one second on one sale - an auto-sell addon emptying greys at the same moment. That no longer stops selling: the item is skipped for the trip, and only three such surprises in one trip stop it. |
| 2.185.0 | **Quest NPCs: no more learning the wrong one.** "Investigate Echo Ridge" was learned for Dermot Johns, a vendor: guide.learn_npc_id saved every title in an open gossip frame against the player's TARGET, and a vendor trip leaves the merchant targeted. Every later turn-in then went to him ("via learned id 190"), stalled three times and the goal was skipped. (1) A title is learned only for a target within 6 yd that the game flags as a quest giver (npc flag 0x2; not checked where the client reports no flags). (2) A learned NPC that is no quest giver is forgotten when it is looked up. (3) A learned NPC that refuses the quest (not offered / gave up) or stalls the goal is forgotten (guide.forget_quest_npc), so the next try finds the giver by marker, waypoint title or proximity. A bad pairing already saved clears itself the first time it is used. |
| 2.184.0 | **Bags hold only what is really in them.** The 10:34 vendor tried to sell the same seven items on every trip, all at "bag 0 slot 62-72" - slots the 16-slot backpack does not have. On this client `inventory_helper:get_character_bag_slots()` also returns entries past the backpack (the engine's player container ends with bank storage on some clients), so those items were never in the bags. bags.list now keeps an entry only inside a real bag: backpack slots 1-16, a worn bag's slots 1..get_num_bag_slots(bag+1). bags.count and the free-slot count (bags.free_slots, special bags left out) come from that same list, so bank items no longer count as carried, sellable or taking space. |
| 2.183.0 | **Vendor sells through the inventory helper, as documented.** 2.182 sold nothing ("no slot proven to hold it" for every item in the 10:26 / 10:27 logs): the cursor-name check never matched. Per the inventory helper documentation, `slot_data.bag_id` + `slot_data.bag_slot` from `inventory_helper:get_character_bag_slots()` are the pair `core.input.use_container_item` takes - a sale is now exactly that, with the pair and item id read from the helper in the same tick, and confirmed by the helper's own stack total. The 10:10 "removed a different item" was a false alarm from mixing raw core.inventory counts with helper totals; every count is the helper's now, there is no use_item path, and a sale that really takes another item stops selling for the session. Consumables reported by `inventory_helper:update_consumables_list()` / `:get_current_consumables_list()` are never sold (refreshed per trip), on top of food / drink / consumable / ammo / quest item classes. bags.list calls the helper with `:`. |
| 2.182.0 | **Vendor: buy only up to the GUI amount, never sell food / water, verified sales only.** The 10:10 session: the first bag-slot sale "removed a different item", the use_item fallback then ate the bread and drank the water it had just bought ("sell item 4540 (5 in bags)" -> 4, "159 (7)" -> 6 -> 5) and the restock bought again. (1) Buying: nothing when the bags already hold the Keep Food / Keep Drink amount; the first buy_item is one unit, what arrives is measured (food and water come 5 to a purchase) and the rest is bought in whole lots to the target - no more buying (target - have) units of 5. (2) Never sold: food, drink (bags.food_water), consumables, ammo, quivers / pouches and quest items. (3) Selling: the use_item fallback is gone (it uses items, it does not sell them). Every sale picks the item up, reads its name off the cursor (core.auction_house.get_cursor_item_name), puts it back, and sells only from a slot proven to hold it - trying the helper's pair, then the raw get_items_in_bag position under both numberings, and remembering the numbering that verified per bag. No proven slot, no sale. |
| 2.181.0 | **Game crash: no Sentinel stop while its path is being planned; ranged classes fight at or inside the Ranged attack distance.** (1) Both 2026-10-01 crashes (09:17:10, and the 09:19 session that froze at 50-75 fps) came the moment combat movement took over from a Sentinel leg asked for seconds earlier - an approach to a 78 yd wolf, and a waypoint walk requested on the same tick as a path-clearing engage - in the Echo Ridge Mine area; the 2026-09-27 crashes noted in movement/combat.lua (2.76.0) also came within a second of a fresh Sentinel leg. Stopping the client while its path request is in flight is what they share: N.stop now defers the stop until the client leaves awaiting_path / repathing (at most 15 s, flushed every frame by the movement pulse), and a new leg cancels a deferred stop. (2) The Ranged attack distance (Shooting distance for the hunter) is now the limit for every ranged class: ranged spells cast only at or inside it (smart.lua in_reach), and a class's own engage distance - the shaman's Lightning Bolt reach - is capped by it (rotation.combat_range). Melee and self spells, and classes without the slider, are unchanged. |
| 2.180.0 | **Console action uses izi.print.** The General tab toggle still prints the current action, now through `izi.print` from `common/izi_sdk`. |
| 2.179.0 | **Window size is fixed.** The window is 960 by 695. The Window width and Window height sliders are gone from the Settings tab. |
| 2.178.0 | **Print the current action to the console.** The General tab has a "Print action to console" toggle. While it is on, the action the bot is performing is printed with `izi.printf` whenever that action changes. |
| 2.177.0 | **Full bags always start a vendor run.** With no free slot (ammo and profession bags not counted) the trip starts whether or not Sell is ticked. When no merchant is on the zone list, a Hearthstone on cooldown no longer cancels the trip: the bot walks to the nearest inn and sells there. |
| 2.176.0 | **A paladin no longer tries to shoot.** Every melee swing was sending the wand attack (Shoot, spell 5019) to stop a wand that had never started, and a libram in the ranged slot was counted as a wand because that slot reports `INVTYPE_RANGEDRIGHT`. The wand attack is sent only for a mage who has a wand, and only a weapon of subclass 19 counts as one. A failed wand falls back to the melee swing. |
| 2.175.0 | **Wand is mage-only.** The Shoot filler and the "Wand below mana %" slider stay on the mage. Priest and warlock no longer list them, and only a mage starts a wand from spell range. Other casters swing in melee. |
| 2.174.0 | **Grind loots every enemy it killed.** Adds that die while another mob is the target are remembered with the fight and queued. The next pull waits until those corpses are looted. The loot scan covers the same 100 yards as the enemy scan, and corpses still waiting do not expire while another corpse is being walked to. |
| 2.173.0 | **Mages conjure the highest rank and rest on it.** Conjure Water and Conjure Food, when they are in the spellbook, cast the highest known rank until the bags hold 20 of that conjured water or food. Rest eats and drinks the conjured stacks before any other food or water. |
| 2.172.0 | **Casters auto-attack when they engage.** Mage, priest, warlock, and shaman start an attack as soon as the enemy is focused inside spell range: a wand from range when one is equipped, otherwise the melee swing, including a shaman standing at Lightning Bolt range. A failed wand falls back to the swing. |
| 2.171.0 | **A mana class leaves the quest for water only when it can pay.** Below the Keep Drink count, gold on hand or gold plus junk that would sell must cover one of this level's vendor waters (Refreshing Spring Water up through Purified Draenic Water). The RestedXP step is paused and the bot walks to the inn, sells, and buys. If that one drink is not affordable, the step keeps running until it is. Mages who conjure water, and warriors and rogues, do not make the trip. |
| 2.170.0 | **Ordinary bags equip themselves.** A bag in the bags (Small Brown Pouch and the other common Vanilla/TBC bags, then item info, then the item name) goes into an empty bag slot. When all four are full, a bigger bag replaces the smallest ordinary bag only if that bag is empty. Ammo pouches, quivers and soul bags are never equipped. This runs whether or not Auto Equip is ticked, and still waits until the character is out of combat, not at a merchant, and not on a live target. |
| 2.169.0 | **Full bags always vendor; the quest step is paused and resumed where it stopped.** (1) With no free slot (ammo / soul bags not counted) a trip always starts - the after-trip cooldown and the "freed nothing" hold no longer apply, except that a trip which freed nothing is not repeated within 60 s. (2) While questing, the spot where a trip starts is saved ("quest paused at ..."); after vendoring the bot walks back there (8 yd, up to 2 min; a fight on the way comes first) and only then does the RestedXP step get the tick again ("back at the quest spot - resuming"). (3) Buy steps know Light Shot (2516), Rough Arrow, Sharp Arrow, Heavy Shot, Small Ammo Pouch (2102) and Light Quiver by id, so the bags are counted before walking to a vendor. |
| 2.168.0 | **Selling reaches the container call; no attacker ping-pong.** (1) The 13:11 log sold "via use_item" every time: 2.166.0 checked the helper's slot against get_num_bag_slots, whose backpack count the API docs call unreliable on these clients, so every slot was rejected. inventory_helper's (bag_id, bag_slot) is documented as the pair the container calls take - only a 0-4 / 1-36 sanity range is checked now, the wrong-item safety net stays, and a fallback line names the pair. (2) With the hunter pet tanking, two whelps took turns on the pet and the player and the bot switched between them every tick ("switch to attacker" x40 in 2 s): a current target alive and in combat is kept, and attacker switches are at least 1.5 s apart. |
| 2.167.0 | **RestedXP buy steps.** ".buy" ("Buy 2 stacks of Light Shot") was mapped to "collect" and looked for a mob to kill. quest/engine.lua buy_goal now reads the item and amount from the goal text (colour codes stripped: "N stacks of X", "N X", "X"), walks to the step's waypoint, opens the vendor there (the waypoint's named NPC, else the nearest NPC flagged vendor, 0x80), finds the item on the vendor by name (get_vendor_item_info) and buys until the bags hold the amount - a stack of ammo is 200, so 2 stacks of Light Shot is 400. The first buy_item is one unit and what arrives sets the size of the next, so the call's quantity meaning is measured rather than assumed. A vendor without the item is skipped for the next; no gold, 3 buys with nothing arriving, or 2 minutes end the goal with a trail line. Skipped on WoW Forever (vendor item info does not exist there). |
| 2.166.0 | **Vendor only at the slider, and selling that works.** The 12:53 log: trips started correctly at 1 and 0 free slots (5 ammo-bag slots not counted), but every sale - grey junk 4865, 1370, 2654, 2651, 7074, 7073, 7098 - came back "could not sell", so the bags stayed full, and the merchant window left open was worked again every 60 s. (1) Selling used core.input.use_item ("use a self cast item"), which never sells; it now clicks the item with core.input.use_container_item at inventory_helper's (bag, slot), with the slot checked to lie inside the bag. A sale that removes a different item switches back to item-id selling for the session, with a warning. (2) Trips only at or below the Vendor at Free Slots slider (special bags not counted): the accept-failure request and an already-open merchant window follow it too (an open window is still worked for a repair, a food / water need, or a RestedXP vendor step). (3) The merchant window is closed when a trip ends. |
| 2.165.0 | **Reverted 2.164.0; accept / turn-in fallback limited to quest givers.** At the user's request the 2.164.0 code (timed-quest rush, saved NPC positions, re-take from a learned giver) is removed - the trainer / quest logic is back to 2.163.0 and will be reworked later. The random NPCs in the 12:40 log (Marryk Nurribit, Wren Darkspring, a player's Imp Minion after Durnan refused) came from the older "nearest friendly unit at the waypoint" fallback: for accept and turn-in goals it now takes only units flagged quest giver (npc flag 0x2; not filtered where the client reports no flags). Talk goals (vendor, trainer, flight master) are unchanged. |
| 2.164.0 | **Timed quests: rush them, and re-take a failed one from its known giver.** The 12:35 log settled it: Scalding Mornbrew Delivery was FAILED in the quest log (its timer had run out during the 12:15 trainer detour and the vendor stops) - that is why the class trainer never listed it. 2.162 abandoned it but skipped the turn-in because the accept came from an earlier session. (1) Every quest NPC talked to is remembered by npc id and position (saved), and a failed quest is re-taken from the NPC learned as its giver at that position. (2) Timed quests are remembered (Scalding Mornbrew built in; any quest that fails joins the list, saved). While one is carried the bot rushes: no sell trip, no trainer visit of its own, no pulling mobs off the path - fights that come to it still happen. |
| 2.163.0 | **Class trainer gossip: wait for the quest rows.** The frame stacks from the hunter trainer show a normal GossipFrame with the quest timer (TimerTracker) still running - the quest had not failed; the rows were not read. A gossip that shows options but no quest rows yet is now given 1.5 s for them before "not offered", and a miss writes one trail line with the options, the raw active / available quest titles and the izi count, so the next log names what the NPC listed. |
| 2.162.0 | **Turn-in at a class trainer: failed timed quests, and quest rows the reader missed.** The 12:24 session reached Durnan Furcutter (the turn-in NPC, a class trainer), found no quest rows in his gossip ("not_offered" 0.5 s after the frame opened), then tried Marryk Nurribit, Wren Darkspring and an Imp. Scalding Mornbrew Delivery is a timed delivery accepted at 12:16 - it had most likely failed, and an NPC does not list a failed quest. (1) Turn-in goals read the quest log (`get_quest_log_title` is_complete -1 = failed): a failed quest is abandoned (`select_quest_log_entry` / `set_abandon_quest` / `abandon_quest`), the bot walks back to where it was accepted and takes it again, then the turn-in goal carries on; a turn-in that finds no quest checks this before ruling the NPC out. Where each quest was accepted (npc id + position) is remembered and saved with the settings; with no known giver, or no re-offer within 3 min, the turn-in is skipped with a warning. Not on WoW Forever (no quest log API). (2) An empty izi gossip quest list now falls back to the raw gossip rows before "not offered". |
| 2.161.0 | **Quest before trainer; full bags force the vendor; ammo bags found without item info.** (1) The 12:15 turn-in of Scalding Mornbrew Delivery went to an NPC who is also a class trainer: the trainer's own level-up visit (every 3 levels) walked the bot to a trainer while the quest walked it to the NPC - "Nav settle" every 5-6 s - and since 2.150.0 that visit took any NPC flagged class trainer, other classes' included. Now the bot's own trainer visits wait while the quest step is an NPC goal (accept / turn in / talk / fly), the trainer never selects training at an NPC the current accept / turn-in goal is about, and "any flagged class trainer" is used only for a quest ".train" step. (2) Full bags (no free slot, special bags not counted) force the sell trip past the 90 s after-trip cooldown. (3) The ammo pouch was not seen ("bags: 5 free" with no special-bag note): item info is empty on this client. Bags are now found at the client's own bag slot (`get_bag_inventory_slot`), by quiver / ammo pouch / soul bag item id, by item info or name, and by contents - a bag holding only arrows or bullets. |
| 2.160.0 | **Full bags: sell before the quest, and special bags no longer count as free.** The 12:07 session read "bags: 5 free slot(s)" with the bags full - the hunter's ammo pouch / quiver slots were counted - so no sell trip ran, and the accept of Scalding Mornbrew Delivery (a quest that hands over an item) failed at Nori, then at two other NPCs, and the bot stood at the door. (1) Quivers and ammo pouches (item class 11) and soul / profession bags (container with a subclass; name as the fallback on WoW Forever) are found at inventory slots 20-23 and their free slots are taken off the count. (2) Vendor rule: an accept that was clicked but never landed - with an "inventory is full" error, or twice - finishes as "bags_full" (quest/npc.lua); the quest engine does not rule the NPC out, asks `vendor.request_bag_trip`, and the sell trip runs ahead of the step (closest vendor, Vendor tab settings), then the accept is tried again. The same reason is not requested twice within 10 min, so a trip that frees nothing falls back to the old "try another NPC". |
| 2.159.0 | **Vendor at free slots: the closest vendor, then back to the quest step.** When free bag slots are at or below the Vendor tab's "Vendor at Free Slots" slider the trip now goes to the closest NPC the game flags as a vendor (npc flag 0x80, one that repairs - 0x1000 - first when the gear needs it) within 100 yd, else the zone merchant, else the nearest inn (innkeepers buy and sell) - quest mode had no route merchant and usually stopped at "No merchant for this zone". The merchant window then sells, buys and repairs with the Vendor tab settings, and the cascade hands the tick back to the RestedXP step (trail: "vendor trip done: ... - back to the quest step"). A trip that freed nothing is not repeated until the free count changes (or 5 min). Free-slot count fixed: inventory_helper already counts the backpack, and the extra backpack_free() read the whole player container (worn gear included) against get_num_bag_slots(0), which is always 0 - it is used alone now and logged on change ("bags: N free slot(s)"). The 11:56 session's accept of Scalding Mornbrew Delivery (a quest that hands over an item) failed three times at one NPC and then hung. |
| 2.158.0 | **Movement tuning from the 11:28 session** (26 min, 50 kills, 38 Sentinel requests, 0.5 ms/frame). (1) Corpse walks stalled at 4 yd: movement calls a walk arrived at ~5 yd, loot fired only inside 3.5 yd, so three corpses were walked to for 15 s and never looted; standing still within 5 yd now loots from there. The "walk to corpse" trail line is written per 5 yd, not per tick. (2) XP grind steps and path clearing (100 yd) skip critters and grey mobs: the session fought three Rabbits and walked ~20 yd to each empty corpse. (3) NPC goal stall check: a turn-in picked its NPC by the previous step's waypoint and tracked distance to him while walking 354 yd to the real waypoint - "no progress for 30 s" on a good walk; an NPC more than 40 yd from the waypoint is not tracked, the waypoint is. (4) "no path leg to X" now names the refusal reason; the approach status line moves in 5-yd steps (it was most of the 454 note lines). |
| 2.157.0 | **Movement is the previous version again. Auto-attack starts once the enemy is the focused target, and the rotation casts as soon as a ticked spell is in its own range.** The 2.156 shaman walk-in to the melee slider is gone: the approach stands at Lightning Bolt range and does not close for Stormstrike. Offensive spells are no longer held until the walk reaches the GUI stand distance. Hunter Auto Shot uses the gun's range, casters start the wand at 30 yards, and melee swings start at 5 yards. |
| 2.156.0 | **Every class uses its own Spells-tab distances, and quest pulls stop at that range before the attack.** Shaman ignored the ranged slider and never stepped into melee; area spells ignored the AoE slider whenever the catalog hard-coded a count. Questing still picks the closest mob of the step's type within 100 yd. The walk stops at that class's attack distance: melee slider (warrior, rogue, paladin, feral druid), ranged slider (mage, priest, warlock, caster druid), or both for hunter and shaman (shoot or bolt until the mob closes, then the melee slider). Auto-attack matches: melee swing, wand, hunter Auto Shot, shaman swing only in melee. Ticked spells stay in DPS order (dots and instants, then the filler; shots before hunter melee; lightning bolt until the shaman is in melee). The AoE slider is the enemy count for every class, including the rogue. |
| 2.155.0 | **Fix: not walking to RestedXP kill targets.** (1) The far-target approach (quest/engine.lua fight_unit) released combat movement - a stop - then asked navigation for a leg, which the move gap after that stop refused, then fell through to combat_engage, which took COMBAT back and could not move for the same gap; the next tick released again. The 00:49 session stood 70 s after "engage Ragged Young Wolf"; the 01:00 one dropped three boars at 30-37 yd as unreachable with no "approaching" line. Combat movement is now released once per target, the approach waits up to 3 s for its leg, and only then closes by combat movement; "approach X on a path" / "no path leg to X" trail lines show which. (2) The re-path ladder climbed while the bot stood within attack distance of its target ("no progress toward the combat (3 yd) - jumping" beside the boar): within the combat distance + 1 yd is now fighting, not stuck, in both the ladder and the walker stuck report. |
| 2.154.0 | **XP grind pulls within 100 yd of the player.** The 01:11 hunter crashed while sitting "waiting at Grind to 1130+/1400xp": RestedXP `.xp` was classed as `goto`, so nothing was scanned, and path-pull only looked 20-30 yd for red mobs (yellow boars skipped) and needed a heading. `.xp` / "Grind to" now pulls the nearest valid enemy within 100 yd of the player's position (the 360-degree scan), walks the grind spots only when none are in range, and never sits idle. Auto-attack refuses a freed unit (`is_valid`) before the native helper. |
| 2.153.0 | **Hunter shoots again.** 2.152 started melee auto-attack (id 6603) from the engage distance; Auto Shot is `ATTACK_TYPE.RANGED` (75), so `cast_safe` failed and FAIL_GAP silenced the rotation. The hunter only white-hit after the mob closed (01:00 log, 3 yd dead zone). Auto Shot is now started through the helper; shots fire from the shooting-distance slider; inside the dead zone, melee abilities (Raptor / Wing Clip) run instead of holding fire at 5 yd. |
| 2.152.0 | **Close to the GUI attack distance, then loot the corpse.** The 00:52 warrior left Sentinel at 35 yd, stalled at 32, started swinging, and every corpse timed out with 0 loot attempts while the next wolf was pulled. Sentinel now walks until the GUI engage distance + 15 yd. Offensive spells and auto-attack wait until that slider (or closer); Charge still opens. Melee stands on the slider, not 1-2 yd outside it. After a kill the quest / grind tick loots before fight-back's "combat not over" sit and before the next pull, and the corpse walk is re-issued instead of skipped as "already moving". |
| 2.151.0 | **GUI wiring audit.** Every registered control was matched against what reads it (59 controls; script check plus hand check of the ones read through tables). Fixed: **Reverse Path** was never read, and `path_format.reversed` read `waypoints` - which normalize drops - so it failed on every path; it now reverses the flat coords (and waypoint holds) and the grind engine walks the reversed copy. **Loop Path** was never read; a profile with `loop = false` now stops at its last node unless it is ticked (fights still run), and grind/catalog.lua read `loop` after normalize had overwritten it (a file without the key became false). **Vendor each lap** and the **profile's own merchant** came from path_runner, which nothing starts; the grind engine now counts laps (`grind.take_lap`) and vendor.lua uses the running profile (`grind.current_profile`). **Draw Loaded Path** shows the path actually walked, current node highlighted. Settings read with no control: druid `cat_form` (now the real Cat / Bear form, `get_shapeshift_form_id`), druid `entangling` and shaman `enhancement` (removed toggles from 2.142.0). Hidden legacy selectors (Bot Mode, Class Rotation) left as they are. |
| 2.150.0 | **Fix: standing at the vendor on "step complete".** The 00:33 session: the level-1 ".vendor" goal was skipped on our side, RestedXP kept the step (and its arrow) on the vendor, the ".train" goal walked to Brother Danil, found no trainer - the warrior list had "Llane Beanshield", he is Llane Beshere - and was marked done after 20 s; RestedXP had nothing ticked, so the bot stood still. RestedXP has no skip-step API, so: (1) a ".vendor" step below level 2 is followed again (the merchant window is what RestedXP ticks; vendor.tick still sells / buys nothing below level 2); (2) trainers are found by the quest step's waypoint title, then the fixed name list, then any class-trainer NPC flag (`get_npc_flags` 0x20); a class trainer that offers no training (another class's) or never opens is rejected and the next one tried; (3) "step complete" with goals only the bot marked done retries those goals after 30 s. |
| 2.149.0 | **Quest steps followed; food in the bags counted; no vendoring below level 2.** (1) A RestedXP ".train" step was unmapped ("goto"): the 00:20 session stood 5 minutes at "waiting at Train [Battle Shout]" until the stuck watchdog, and ".trainer" steps were skipped between the every-3-levels checks. When questing, both now ask `trainer.quest_visit` for a visit now: walk the step's waypoints until the class trainer is in sight, train everything affordable, then the goal is done (or RestedXP ticks it first; 20 s at the waypoint with no trainer moves on). The every-3-levels rule stays for the bot's own visits. (2) New `bags.food_water`: every bag item is classified by the curated lists, then its use spell (Food / Drink / Refreshment), then its item class (Consumable / Food & Drink), skipping items above the player's level. Resting eats unlisted food after the ranked items, `supplies.missing` cancels a run when any food / water is carried, and the merchant restock counts every kind toward the target. (3) `vendor.level_ok`: below level 2 no vendor trip, no supply run (resting waits for regen) and quest ".vendor" steps are passed over. |
| 2.148.0 | **Fix: NPC / enemy approach lost since 2.140.0.** 2.140.0 added `Nv.set_approach(guid)` (the mob a far-target approach walks to) in `movement/nav.lua`, but a `Nv.set_approach(kind)` already existed further up, so the new one replaced it. Every quest walk then stored "npc" / "enemy" as a mob guid: the NPC / enemy approach avoidance near the goal never switched on, and a stalled walk to an NPC halted and cleared the destination instead of blacklisting the spot. The new function is now `set_approach_target` (movement.lua export, quest/engine.lua fight_unit); `set_approach(kind)` works again. A scan found no other function defined twice. |
| 2.147.0 | **Audit phase 5 - cleanup.** Removed files nothing loads: `config.lua` (dropped from main.lua's reload list), the empty `grind/paths/catalog.lua` index (and its entries in grind/catalog.lua, loader.lua and main.lua), `leveling_paths.lua` and `data/_zones_raw.lua` (no longer listed in make_manifest.py SKIP_FILES), and the old `bootstrap/` folder plus the root `net_loader.lua` (the installed loader is `plugin_loader/`). Removed the `izi.is_los` fallbacks in main.lua and movement/range.lua - izi has no such function, so LoS is `player:los_to(unit)` plus the existing ray flags. Fixed the spellbook.lua header (a rescan follows a level-up or a trainer visit, with a 60 s safety net, not every 2 s). AGENTS.md now documents `core.reload_game_ui` as intentional and names `plugin_loader/` as the loader. Deferred: removing the equip-bind poll (needs the event confirmed in game). |
| 2.146.0 | **Audit phase 4 - one continent answer, plain version checks.** New `gamever.continent_of(pos)`: `core.get_map_id()` when it is 0 / 1 / 530, else the continent of the nearest flight point that exists on this game version. The flight planner (`quest/engine.lua` far_travel) and the supply runs (`supplies.lua`) each had their own version - the supply one ignored `get_map_id` and counted flight points that do not exist on WoW Forever - and both now use it. `quest/npc.lua` and `supplies.lua` require `gamever` once at the top instead of re-requiring it inside private `forever()` helpers. Deferred (needs WoW Forever readings, Forever step 4): a faction / race fallback for Forever clients that report 0. |
| 2.145.0 | **Audit phase 3 - less work per scan.** (1) The quest-mob finders in `quest/guide.lua` (`find_mob`, `find_npc_mob`, `find_camp_mob`, `find_source_mob`, `find_path_mob`) ran `fightable()` - about eight native calls plus a line-of-sight read - on every unit before the cheap tests. Each loop now checks `is_valid`, then its own filter (npc id / name / level / position) and the distance, and calls `fightable()` last (and, where only the closest counts, only for a candidate nearer than the best so far). A randomized old-vs-new comparison (400 unit sets) picked the same units and counts every time with 61% fewer native calls. (2) `resting.lua`: the ranked food / water lists are cached per class preference instead of rebuilt (four tables) on every 0.1 s rest check, and `rest_threat` reads the shared object-list cache instead of building a fresh `get_visible_objects` table first. |
| 2.144.0 | **Audit phase 2.4 - one gossip helper, and the vendor option fixed.** New `gossip.lua`: `is_open`, `close`, `find(spec)` / `select(spec)` - izi's option by icon (selected with the view's own `:select()`), then izi's views by type / icon / wording, then the raw rows by type / icon / wording selected by `gossip_option_id`. `trainer.lua`, `vendor.lua`, `quest/engine.lua` (flight master, innkeeper bind) and `quest/npc.lua` use it, each with its own icon / type / words. Fixed on the way: `vendor.lua` read `gossip_option_id` from izi's option view (the same bug the trainer had before 2.125.0), so izi's VENDOR icon match was always discarded and only a raw gossip_type of "vendor" could open a merchant's or innkeeper's goods; it now also matches the icon and the wording ("Let me browse your goods"). |
| 2.143.0 | **Audit phase 2.3 - one current target.** `state.target` is the authority. The combat hold latch (`combat.lua`) kept its unit after an engine dropped the target (unreachable, released), so a caller passing no target (Rotation Only) could pick the dropped mob up again. `state.reset_target()` now runs a hook list (`state.on_target_reset`) and the latch registers its release there, so clearing the target clears every copy. Checked and left as is: the engines re-read `state.target` every 0.1 s tick and hand it to `combat_engage`, so movement follows a rotation-adopted attacker within one tick. |
| 2.142.0 | **Audit phase 2.2 - dead class-module code removed, and a GUI nil error fixed.** `rotation.lua` has not called a class module's `tick`, `buffs_ooc`, `register_gui` or `interrupt` since the Spells-tab rotation (2.64.0); those functions, the private helpers and spell objects only they used (each spell object also registered a spellbook watcher at load), and `rotations/sequence.lua` (used only by those ticks) are removed - 3,979 lines out of `rotations/`. Each class module keeps its live contract (class id, combat range / engage range, melee shape, combat profile, rest, scan range, food / drink ids, hunter range helpers). The `rotation.tick` gate that tested `mod.tick` now tests `mod.combat_range`. Found by the new undeclared-read scan while doing this: `gui.lua`'s grind tab read `path_format` and `path_profiles` as undeclared globals (an error once a route was armed, and on Save) - both are now lazy module proxies. AST scan: 0 undeclared reads, 0 global writes. |
| 2.141.0 | **Audit phase 2.1 - one stuck authority.** Three detectors acted on the same leg at 4 s: the walker stuck check (`movement/fsm.lua`), the re-path ladder (`movement/repath.lua`) and the Sentinel stall watchdog (`movement/sentinel.lua`). (1) The Sentinel watchdog stopped the leg at 4 s while the ladder's first rung re-planned it - the stop won and the character halted; it is now a 12 s safety net for a leg the ladder is not tracking. (2) The walker check called the ladder's escalate directly on top of the ladder's own clock, so walker legs climbed two rungs at a time (re-plan + jump together, give up at ~8 s); `RP.stuck_now` now only reports and the ladder escalates on its own windows (re-plan 4 s, jump 7 s, give up 11 s). (3) The walker check set a 4 s stuck grace before handing over, and `may_issue` refused every move during it, freezing the character on each report; the grace now applies only to the fallback path where the walker is really cancelled. The ladder already treats Sentinel's planning / recovering states and path-index progress as progress (2.137). |
| 2.140.0 | **Audit phase 1 - correctness.** (1) `supplies.lua`: the supply-run state (`poor_gold` and friends) was declared below `supplies.tick`, so the write after a run that could not pay went to a global and "no run until the gold goes up" never held - a character with a little gold kept walking to the inn and back. The block now sits with the module state. (2) A far-target approach (2.139.0) that stalled through the re-path ladder blacklisted an 8 yd zone where the mob stood; approach legs are now tagged (`movement.set_approach`, cleared on stop / halt / combat range) and give up by marking that mob unreachable instead. (3) `quest/guide.lua`: `diag_wanted` was declared after `guide.update`, which therefore read an always-nil global and never ran a detection reading asked for from the Questing tab; the local now comes first. Global-assignment scan: 0 (was 2). |
| 2.139.0 | **Closest quest mob, GUI attack distance, and food / water when the bags are empty.** (1) Every 2026-09-29 log engaged a RestedXP raid-marked Ragged Young Wolf 43-67 yd away ('no progress toward the combat', 'target unreachable') with the same wolf much closer: the mark won outright. The mark, a name match or a drop-source match now only names the step's mob (npc id); the closest fightable unit with that id is attacked (guide.find_npc_mob) and the id is kept for the goal. (2) Ranged classes used their class reach and read the Ranged attack distance slider only when a class had none - the slider now sets the distance (class reach is just its default), capped 1 yd inside the longest ticked damage spell; melee uses the melee slider. (3) A target more than 35 yd away, out of combat, is approached on a Sentinel path; combat movement takes over inside the engage distance + 15 yd. (4) Resting with nothing to eat / drink used to log 'no usable food - not resting' and carry on. It now asks for a supply run: vendor.lua walks to the nearest inn on the recorded Alliance Eastern Kingdoms roads (or an innkeeper in sight), sells junk, buys food / water, and carries on (supplies.trip_wanted: buying on, not WoW Forever, not something this mage conjures, gold >= 25c or junk to sell, no failed-for-gold run since the gold last rose). When no run can happen the character stands and waits for 80% health and mana, then continues. (5) Mage: Conjure Water / Food waits for core.spell_book.is_usable_spell (enough mana) instead of failing the cast into a 15 s back-off. Movement API audit: every movement call (12 core.*, 5 movement_handler, 16 simple_movement, the Sentinel client) is declared in the .api stubs / documented and called with the right self convention. |
| 2.138.0 | **Quest travel and combat chase no longer freeze in place.** 2.137.0 waited in `to_world` for a confirmed navmesh height and memoized the empty waypoint list, so collect/kill goals logged "0 waypoints" and the character stood still after combat. Waypoints now walk immediately on player z (replaced when the height lands) and empty lists are not cached. Combat hops no longer treat a stale `pending` as "already moving"; a target the walker never walked toward is dropped without being blacklisted. |
| 2.137.0 | **Sentinel travel follows the documented API; classes stand in their own range.** Out-of-combat travel no longer sends `z_extent`, `avoid_zones`, `soft_update`, or rewritten `get_path_opts`. Short clear legs use `move_direct` (raycast / walk_open). Danger mobs use `nav_client:find_path_avoid`. Recorded routes run `check_path` before `follow_path`. Indoor narrow corridors use `get_corridor_widths` + `find_path_corridor`. `max_stuck` tries `random_point` once before giving up. Recovering / repathing / path-index gain count as progress; the stuck ladder calls `replan` instead of benching Sentinel. Far waypoint height waits for `get_all_heights` (no player-z + z_loose). Hunter's Mark is ranged-only. Combat hop thresholds and the 4-yard underfoot skip stay. |
| 2.136.0 | **No more tight circles on underfoot Sentinel waypoints.** Sentinel densifies paths to 1-yard points; the first waypoint sits next to the player and the character orbited it because `may_issue` treated "already moving" as success and never refused a dest closer than 2 yards. Travel dests under 4 yards flat are now arrival (or a skip to the far `keep_path` goal). Sentinel `move_to` / `retarget` refuse those dests, `follow_path` drops leading near points, and the watchdog skips the current path index when it is underfoot (or retargets the real destination). `densify_segment_length` is raised to 6 when that live `get_path_opts` key exists. Avoidance look-ahead does not fire inside 4 yards. Combat hop thresholds are unchanged. |
| 2.135.0 | **Kharanos inn door: stop, search inside, step in.** Both 2.133 and 2.134 sat 7 yards from Durnan Furcutter's waypoint (Scalding Mornbrew Delivery) and never talked: movement treats 5 yards as arrived and steers, the quest engine wanted 3, and circling the door reset the 30 s stall. Talk / accept / turn-in now stop at 8 yards, search 40 yards from the door, and walk a few yards in at the player's own height so Sentinel can enter. Circling inside 8 yards is not stall progress. |
| 2.134.0 | **Quest NPC dialogs that finish, and a 30-second stall recovery.** (1) Arrival at a waypoint and the "friendly NPC at the waypoint" search are flat (height within 12 / 30 yd, or standing still on the spot): a RestedXP waypoint's height is the terrain under it, which indoors is the ground outside, so the bot never "arrived" inside a building and never found the giver. (2) `quest/npc` waits for a window after every interact (the NPC and quest window events `events.lua` now records, or a readable gossip / greeting list): a UI error or 3 s of silence is a failed attempt, not an empty frame. The NPC's own active row must be `is_complete` before it is selected ("not_ready" otherwise); Continue that opens no completion panel is also "not_ready". A hand-in is proven by `QUEST_TURNED_IN`, the completed flag, or the quest leaving a log it was in. Gossip rows go through `izi.gossip` (real id only where `has_real_id`, else title; the selector token is never stored). Rewards wait up to 2 s for item info before `equip.rate` picks. (3) The engine resolves the quest from `quest_id` or `ids` (multi-quest accept / turn-in goals no longer fall to the talk path), counts a goal done as soon as the dialog proves it and closes the windows once without re-interacting, holds 10 s and asks once more on "not_ready", closes to 2.5 yd after a refused interact, and on 30 s without progress (no 2 yd gained, no dialog stage advanced, no quest-log change) closes frames, re-reads the guide, re-finds the NPC and requests a fresh path; the third recovery skips the goal. (4) `has_current_step()` is authoritative when it answers. (5) `.home` is a talk goal: the innkeeper's bind option is picked by icon. |
| 2.133.0 | **Recorded Alliance roads to inns and flight masters.** The 36 Eastern Kingdoms routes in `Alliance_FlightPath_Routes` are followed when a RestedXP waypoint or a vendor trip ends on an inn or a flight path and the player is on that road, or the straight line to the destination crosses it. The next recorded point is retargeted in place, so the walk does not stop. The last 18 yards use the real destination. |
| 2.132.0 | **The load gate no longer requires a player.** Headers run before the player exists and are not asked again, so the old player check meant the plugin never loaded. Both `get_game_version` and `get_exact_game_version` are pcalled. Load is on when the coarse name is `Tbc` or `Forever`, or the exact build is `wow_forever_beta_us`. The decision is posted to the local nav boot log (`127.0.0.1:47110/log?src=boot`) and written with `core.log`. Class is still not a gate. Loader 1.2.2 is the installed copy. |
| 2.131.0 | **WoW Forever beta loads when the coarse version name is not "Forever".** `core.get_game_version()` still admits TBC (`"Tbc"`) and Forever (`"Forever"`). If that check fails, `core.get_exact_game_version() == "wow_forever_beta_us"` turns the load back on. `gamever.is_forever()` uses the same exact build, so flight points, races and pet happiness follow the Forever rules. Loader 1.2.1 (installed copy) has the same gate. |
| 2.130.0 | **The Spells list draws again.** Per-class sliders were registered with `local C = enums.class_id`, which shadowed the colour helper for the rest of `gui.lua`. The spell list and the mini window then called that table (`attempt to call upvalue 'C'` in the 20:28 log) and the list was dropped, so the tab showed only the sliders and the status stayed on "Waiting for spellbook 0.0s" after the scan. The class ids are `class_ids` now, the known-spell list is drawn at the top of the Spells tab, and an idle bot clears the load note once the scan has finished. **Loader 1.2.0** (installed copy, not downloaded): follows GitHub `main` with no pinned commit, retries the branch lookup, and loads on TBC and Forever for every class. |
| 2.129.0 | **Spells tab settings are per class.** The self-heal, defensive, area, and wand sliders were one account-wide set, so a Hunter saw the caster sliders (including Wand below mana %). Each class now has its own saved sliders, and only that class's rows are shown. A Hunter gets shooting distance (default 25), melee distance, Mend Pet, defensives, and area — not a wand or a self-heal. |
| 2.128.0 | **RestedXP waypoint runs do not stop for a course change.** A path that is already moving is retargeted in place: a new waypoint, a moved arrow, and the end of a steering hop all continue the walk. The character is not halted between points. Avoidance is calculated only while closing on an NPC (25 yd), closing on an enemy (30 yd), or when the body does not fit the way ahead (a wall, or a gap only a narrow body fits). A clear approach does not detour. |
| 2.127.0 | **Class range, closest target, and steering that does not stop.** Melee engage no longer gets rewritten from under 5 yards to 30, so a melee class closes to its real swing distance (slider default 5). Ranged classes stand inside their longest damage spell (Lightning Bolt, Frostbolt, Mind Flay, Wrath, else 30), capped 1 yard inside that spell. A live target is kept unless a kill-first npc, or an enemy already inside that distance and at least 3 yards closer, should take over. Hunter shots stay outside the dead zone; Kill Command no longer spends the GCD in melee; Wing Clip, Raptor Strike and Mongoose Bite still fire once a mob has closed. Shaman stands at Lightning Bolt range (Stormstrike being ticked does not walk them in); Lightning Bolt and Chain Lightning only past 5 yards, Stormstrike and a melee Earth Shock once the mob is in. A blocked corridor or the next hop is handed to the walker as that hop plus the real goal, with no stop, and the same detour is not re-issued. Sentinel's first stuck rung retargets in place and does not bench or stop the leg; its own recovering state is treated as progress. |
| 2.126.0 | **A Hunter's Spells tab offered Battle Stance and Heroic Strike.** The spellbook scan admitted any id `core.spell_book.get_spell_name` could name, but a name is the game's own spell data and says nothing about this character - `has_spell` / `is_spell_learned` / `is_spell_known` are the per-character calls. `collect_ids` harvests every number in whatever shape `get_spells()` returns, array indices included, so on a long book index 78 named itself Heroic Strike and a stray numeric record field of 2457 named itself Battle Stance. Both entered the book, and `smart.lua` then kept those rows because `spellbook.family(name)` found them - the very check meant to prove the character knows the spell. `is_real_spell` is replaced by `owns_spell`, which asks only the three ownership calls; the name is still used for display, never for admission. A client that answers none of the three falls back to the old name test and warns once, so the rotation is never left with nothing to cast. The Spells tab and the rotation are both built from the scanned book, so both are fixed at once (the per-class `register_gui` functions have been dead code since 2.64.0). `smart.lua` also logs which catalog the class id resolved to ("Class 3 -> HUNTER spell catalog (24 entries)"), because a wrong `get_class()` would now show as an empty tab rather than another class's abilities. Offline: 14 checks over flat, set-shaped, tab-nested and record-shaped books; the pre-fix code fails 4 of them. |
| 2.125.0 | **Trainer gossip fixed.** A class trainer's gossip ('I am interested in mage training.') stood open: (1) izi.gossip.find_option_by_icon returns a view ({ name, id, select = fn }) and the code read gossip_option_id from it - never set, so the icon match was discarded; (2) the text fallback only knew 'train me'; (3) the every-3-levels gate also blocked a trainer window that was already open. Now the option is found by izi's trainer icon (selected with the view's :select()), else by gossip_type 'trainer', icon 3 or the wording (train / teach / instruct) and selected by gossip_option_id; the 3-level gate only decides walking to a trainer - an open trainer gossip is always used, except while the quest engine is taking / handing in a quest at that NPC (quest.current_kind, so class quests are not closed). Selecting stops after 3 tries without a trainer window. The trainer now writes 'trainer' lines to the session log: option selected, window opened, each spell trained, summary. |
| 2.124.0 | **WoW Forever content gating (Forever plan step 3).** Forever is vanilla content. The flight-point catalog (flight travel to far quest goals) now drops, on Forever only, every Outland point (map 530) and the three old-world points added in TBC whose flight masters have no Forever NPC on Wowhead (Forest Song, Emerald Sanctuary, Mudsprocket): 66 usable points instead of 103; name lookups on the flight map skip them too. Everything else checked is already Forever-safe: all 35 grind routes are Alliance Eastern Kingdoms 1-60; TBC-only spell ranks, foods and waters are simply never known / found there; Blood Elf / Draenei data was gated in 2.121.0. |
| 2.123.0 | **WoW Forever API replacements (Forever plan step 2).** Per the Sylvanas API docs, four TBC APIs do nothing on Forever; each now switches on gamever.is_forever(), TBC unchanged. (1) Quest log: the index API is empty on Forever - guide.log_index_of returns nothing there (RestedXP objectives were already the first source), and npc.is_complete uses is_on_quest + RestedXP's objective progress, then the NPC's gossip active list. (2) Vendor supplies: get_vendor_item_info returns empty rows on Forever, so no item can be identified; buying food / water and looking for another supplier stand down there with one log line (repair / selling unaffected). (3) Pet happiness: Forever returns a placeholder table (happiness 0) - pets.happiness() reports nil there. (4) Innkeeper bind confirm: confirm_binder is a no-op on Forever - the prompt is logged and left to the player. |
| 2.122.0 | **Loads on WoW Forever (Forever plan step 1).** header.lua, bootstrap/header.lua and plugin_loader/header.lua accepted only core.get_game_version() == 'Tbc'; they now accept 'Tbc' and 'Forever'. Loader bumped to 1.1.1 - the installed plugin_loader/header.lua has to be replaced for the change to reach the game (the loader is not downloaded). Nothing else changes yet: on Forever the quest-log, vendor-item, pet-happiness and innkeeper-confirm APIs are still the TBC ones until step 2. |
| 2.121.0 | **Races per game version (WoW Forever).** New gamever.lua (core.get_game_version: 'Tbc' / 'Forever'). Playable races from wowhead.com/forever/races: Forever has Human, Orc, Dwarf, Night Elf, Undead, Tauren, Gnome, Troll plus High Order Skyborne (95, Alliance) and Windshaper Skyborne (96, Horde) - no Blood Elf or Draenei; TBC keeps its ten. modes.lua, data/factions.lua and data/racials.lua now treat a race that is not playable on the running version as unknown, so Blood Elf / Draenei data (faction, starter zone key, Arcane Torrent, Mana Tap, Gift of the Naaru) exists only on TBC and the Skyborne races resolve their faction on Forever. Classes are unchanged: Forever has the same nine. The plugin still only loads on TBC until the Forever API plan is carried out. |
| 2.120.0 | **Hunter: ranged and melee done properly.** Ranges come from Auto Shot itself (spellbook min / max range; fallbacks 8 / 35) instead of a hard-coded 8 yd dead zone and 34 yd range, and every spell's own minimum range replaces the catalog's fixed 8 yd. New Hunter sliders (Spells/Class tab; the generic Melee / Ranged sliders are hidden for Hunters): Shooting distance 10-41 yd, default 35 = max gun range (capped 1 yd inside the weapon's real reach), and Melee distance 1-5 yd, default 5. Hybrid logic: outside the dead zone the Hunter shoots; inside it, it steps back out only when that works - the pet (or someone else) holds the mob, or the mob is slowed / rooted (Wing Clip, Concussive, Frost / Freezing Trap, Entrapment, Scatter) and nothing else is on it; otherwise it fights in melee at the Melee distance. Wing Clip is now on by default and cast first in melee (control role) so the mob becomes kitable and the Hunter backs out to shoot; Raptor Strike and Mongoose Bite follow. The old profile backed off from anything inside 8 yd unconditionally, so a chasing mob caused a step-back / get-hit loop. |
| 2.119.0 | **Mobs on the way: all around, closest first.** Clearing the path while travelling (quest walks, flight-master walks) only looked 20 yd out in a 70-degree cone ahead and up to 3 levels above: mobs beside or just behind the path were walked past until they aggroed, and with several around the nearest IN THE CONE was picked, not the nearest.
