Now every hostile within the engage distance + 5 yd (20-30 yd) is counted all the way round, up to 5 levels above (targeting's LEVEL_CAP), and the closest is engaged; the trail logs 'clear the path: <mob> at N yd (closest of M enemies within R yd)'. |
| 2.118.0 | **Sentinel improvements applied (asked).** Re-applied: 2.109.0 all out-of-combat travel through Sentinel (walker only in combat or without Sentinel); 2.112.0 failure codes read from the move_to callback detail / nav.failed table, 'still planning' covering awaiting_path / repathing / deferred, z_extent 250 for waypoints whose height is still a guess (z_loose). New: (1) retarget without stopping - a moving goal re-aims the active leg with move_to opts.soft_update (Sentinel keeps moving and repaths) instead of planning a separate path and switching with follow_path; (2) dangerous mobs as avoid_zones - the 8 nearest too-high mobs within 200 yd (radius from the danger map, cost 100) go with every leg, except one standing on the destination. The 2.116.0 height lookup and 2.117.0 arrival fix are kept. |
| 2.117.0 | **Flight planner continent; arrival is not 'stuck'.** (1) The 02:18 log refused a 3795 yd flight trip from Lakeshire with 'no flight point shortens it' - consistent with core.get_map_id() not returning a continent id (0 / 1 / 530). Any other value is now replaced by the continent of the nearest flight point, the raw id is logged once ('core.get_map_id() = ...'), and a refused plan logs continent, faction, nearest flight point and best landing. (2) The walker's stuck check ran the re-path ladder with the character already at the target ('no progress toward the nav for 0s (3 yd) - re-planning' at the quest giver): within 5 yd of a travel target a standstill now counts as arrival. |
| 2.116.0 | **Far-waypoint height and planning wait restored (asked).** After the 2.113.0 revert the Ironforge -> Kharanos walk (951 yd) sent Sentinel to Kharanos at the player's height (502, ground ~100 yd lower): 422 'Position not on navmesh' on every retry. Restored: the navmesh height lookup for far waypoints in quest/guide.lua (2.108.0, with the 2.112.0 reachable-layer filter), and the 2.111.0 wait while Sentinel is still planning (movement/sentinel.lua N.planning, stall check, re-path ladder). Nothing else of 2.108-2.112 is restored: travel still uses the walker for short legs, and the 2.112.0 failure-code / z_extent changes stay out. |
| 2.115.0 | **Flight paths to far quest goals.** A guide goal more than 1000 yd away (e.g. the Dun Morogh turn-in from Elwynn, ~4000 yd) was walked in a straight line over mountains. Now, when a flight would leave at most 60% of the trip to walk, the bot walks to the nearest flight point of its faction on the continent, talks to the friendly NPCs there nearest first until one opens a flight map (directly or via its taxi gossip option), takes the KNOWN flight point closest to the goal, flies (the bot pauses in the air), and carries on with the same goal after landing (the goal is not ticked off). No flight master, no known closer flight point or 3 failed take-offs -> logged ('travel' trail) and the goal is walked for 5 min. New data/taxi_nodes.lua: 103 flight points (EK, Kalimdor, Outland) with position and faction, trimmed from SentinelCore's TaxiNodes.dbc catalog. |
| 2.114.0 | **Conjure no longer counts the bags every frame.** Since 2.103.0 the conjure check looked up every water and food item id (conjured and bought, a few hundred) on every frame: 1.2-1.3 ms/frame in the 02:03 log - more than the rest of the bot combined - with the frame rate down from ~245 to ~160 fps, the kind of per-frame native-call load earlier crashes were tied to. When a check finds the bags stocked it now waits 2 s before the next one; stopping to conjure and a cast in progress still run every frame. |
| 2.113.0 | **Sentinel pathing reverted to v2.98.0 (PR #116).** As asked, only the Sentinel code: movement/sentinel.lua, movement/nav.lua, movement/repath.lua, movement/const.lua and the navmesh-height lookup in quest/guide.lua are back to their 2.98.0 content. Undone: 2.108.0 far-waypoint navmesh heights, 2.109.0 Sentinel-only travel (the walker again takes short clear legs and fallbacks), 2.111.0 wait-while-planning, 2.112.0 failure-code / z_extent / reachable-layer changes. Everything else (Frost Nova, looting / resting, trainer, flight master, conjure, UI reload) is unchanged. |
| 2.112.0 | **Sentinel usage corrected from its source (SentinelCore / SentinelNavClient).** (1) Failure codes: move_to calls back (success, reason, detail) with the raw message in `reason` and the normalised code in detail.code; the bot compared the raw text against the codes, so unreachable / server_timeout / max_stuck never matched, and the nav.failed bus event (a table) was logged as 'table: 0x...'. One classifier (N.fail_code) now handles both, like Sentinel's own _normalize_fail_reason, and every failure is logged with code and message. (2) 'Still planning' now covers awaiting_path, repathing and deferred (a move made while casting), as Sentinel's questing adapter does. (3) A quest waypoint whose height is still a guess is sent with z_extent 250, the server's documented override for imprecise heights. (4) The navmesh height lookup asks for reachable layers only (filter_unreachable + from_pos) and falls back to all layers when none come back. |
| 2.111.0 | **Wait while Sentinel plans.** A path request thousands of yards long keeps Sentinel in 'awaiting_path' for 4-5 s; the stall check and the re-path ladder counted that as no progress, dropped the leg, jumped and re-requested it (the idle / awaiting_path loop in the console, 01:32 log). N.planning() now reports a leg still being planned, and both wait on it for up to 15 s. (The 01:32 case itself - a Dun Morogh turn-in 4253 yd away from the character - has no walkable path; Sentinel's 422 there is correct.) |
| 2.110.0 | **Frost Nova jump 0.75 s; loot before rest fixes.** The backpedal jump now fires 0.75 s after the backward walk starts (was 0.5 s); the 2.5 s timer is unchanged. Loot: a corpse's give-up / timeout clocks stand still while the bot is under attack, so a corpse queued before another mob attacked is still looted after the fight instead of 'gave up after 30 s' (01:27 log) - looting stays ahead of the rest check in the cascade and healing still waits on loot.hold_rest. Rest: an item used in the last 3 s counts as eating / drinking, so eating the last piece of food no longer logs 'no usable food - not resting' 0.3 s into the rest and drops the eat half of it. |
| 2.109.0 | **Sentinel for all out-of-combat travel.** Every move out of combat - quest waypoints, NPCs, corpses, grind nodes, vendor / trainer walks, recorded routes - is now a Sentinel path to the real destination. Removed from travel: short straight walker legs (under 40 yd), leash rejoin hops, the walker fallback when a Sentinel request is only rate limited (the move now waits for the next 1 s slot), the 20 s Sentinel bench of the stuck ladder (it re-plans through Sentinel instead) and the ladder's walker 'unstick' hop (the jump stays). A destination Sentinel just failed is not re-requested for 3 s. The walker / movement handler moves the character only in combat, or out of combat when Sentinel is unavailable (not loaded, server down). Switch: K.SENTINEL_TRAVEL in movement/const.lua. |
| 2.108.0 | **Far waypoint heights from the navmesh; guide log spam.** Leaving Ironforge for 'Operation Recombobulation' in Dun Morogh, every far waypoint used the player's height (z 502, ground ~100 yd lower) and Sentinel answered 'HTTP 422: Position not on navmesh' with retries on each one. A far waypoint now asks Sentinel once for the navmesh heights at its x,y (get_all_heights) and keeps the layer nearest the player; one request at a time, 1 s apart. The first reply's shape is logged ('sentinel get_all_heights reply'). The quest debug line ('guide: accept quest ... at learned id') printed every tick; the same line now prints at most once per 5 s. |
| 2.107.0 | **UI reload after a flight.** When the flight tracker sees the character land (still for 2.5 s after a flight), the fly goal is closed and core.reload_game_ui() is called once. Guarded: it is not in the local API docs, so a build without it logs 'core.reload_game_ui is not available' instead of failing. |
| 2.106.0 | **Flight master fixes (RestedXP .fly).** Log showed the bot stuck at Thor (Sentinel Hill) on 'flight master gossip has no taxi option': a quest window from the previous goal was still open. Now a frame with no taxi option is closed (gossip + quest) and the NPC is talked to again (4 tries, then the step is skipped); a leftover quest window is closed before every interact. The taxi option is found through izi.gossip (TAXI icon / type / text) and selected with :select(). RestedXP colour tags (\|cRXP_..._) are stripped from the destination; an exact node-name match wins over a partial one. The flight is tracked by position (is_flying not proven on a taxi): the whole bot pauses in flight, a take-off that never leaves the ground is retried (3 tries), and on landing the fly goal is closed so the bot heads for the guide's next position. An unserved destination closes the map (core.taxi.close). |
| 2.105.0 | **Frost Nova backpedal jumps.** The mage's backward walk after Frost Nova now jumps 0.5 s in (core.input.jump, key released with ascend_stop 0.15 s later) while still moving backward. The 2.5 s backpedal timer is unchanged; a backpedal cut short releases the jump key too. |
| 2.104.0 | **Class trainer: every 3 levels, everything affordable, verified.** The trainer is checked once, then again only after 3 more levels; the level of the last check is saved per character (settings file), so a restart does not send the bot back. A RestedXP trainer step before the check is due (or with Train Spells off) is skipped to the next goal. At the trainer every available class spell is bought, cheapest first, until the list or the purse runs out; categories are expanded first; each purchase is verified (gold spent or rank no longer available) and retried up to 3 times. The window is now closed with close_trainer (close_gossip left it open), with a note: 'No new spells to train', 'Trained N spell(s)' or 'Trained N; M more need more gold'. |
| 2.103.0 | **Mage stops to conjure.** Conjure Water / Food (3 s casts) were fired while the bot walked - quest travel, grind routes - so the cast failed and a 15 s back-off followed. Now, when a mage needs water or food (fewer than 5 of ANY water / food carried, not only conjured) and is not in combat, has no attackers, is not resting / eating / drinking and not mounted: navigation is stopped and the bot waits until it stands, a cast lock (cast time + 0.3 s) holds the walker, the conjure is cast, and the whole cascade is held while it casts. Movement resumes by itself afterwards; a refused cast releases the lock and backs off. |
